Motivated by Topological Data Analysis (TDA), which captures global rather than purely local structure, we propose Topological Steering, a new framework for steering LLM behavior through the topological representation of activation spaces. Using persistence diagrams, our method connects activation-based steering with TDA and enables more robust behavioral control. We show that Topological Steering consistently modifies LLM behavior across multiple model families and model sizes.

</details>


### [57] [Confess What You Know: Forget-Set Misalignment with Model Knowledge in LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00605)
*Miso Kim,Georu Lee,Seungwon Jeong,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这项工作提出"忘记集错位"（forget-set misalignment）问题，指大规模语言模型机器遗忘中预设的忘记集与模型实际记忆内容不一致，并据此提出CONFS框架，这是一种数据不可知的数据盲遗忘框架，通过诱导和形式化模型自身记忆的知识来构建与模型对齐的忘记集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM机器遗忘研究通常假设预设的忘记集与模型实际记住的内容匹配，但在真实隐私场景中原始训练数据不可获取，该假设常常不成立，导致遗忘效果不佳或模型效用受损。

Method: 本文识别两种错位情形：欠遗忘（忘记集遗漏了已记忆信息导致泄漏持续）和超知识遗忘（算法被迫"遗忘"模型从未学过的知识从而扰动参数、降低效用）。通过梯度层面的分析，证明这些行为源于错位的遗忘目标而非特定优化选择。随后提出CONFS框架，以数据盲方式通过引导模型召回并形式化其记忆的知识来构造模型对齐的忘记集。

Result: 在合成、多模态和真实世界基准上，CONFS在多项指标上接近黄金标准（Gold-standard）表现，达到有竞争力的遗忘-效用平衡，并且在保持效用方面优于其他数据盲的忘记集构造方法。

Conclusion: CONFS证明在训练数据不可获取的真实隐私设置下，通过模型自身记忆来构造对齐的忘记集可以有效缓解错位问题，在接近理想遗忘效果的同时更好地保持模型效用。

Abstract: Machine unlearning for large language models (LLMs) often assumes that a pre-defined forget set matches what the model has memorized, but this frequently breaks in realistic privacy settings where the original training data is inaccessible. We term this gap forget-set misalignment and identify two cases. In Under Unlearning, the forget set omits memorized information and leakage persists. In Out-of-Knowledge Unlearning, the algorithm is driven to "forget" knowledge the model never learned, perturbing parameters and degrading utility. Using gradient-level analysis, we show these behaviors arise from misaligned unlearning targets rather than specific optimization choices. We then propose CONfession-to-Forget-Set (CONFS), a data-blind framework that constructs model-aligned forget sets by eliciting and formalizing the model's memorized knowledge. Across synthetic, multimodal, and real-world benchmarks, CONFS approaches Gold-standard performance on several metrics and achieves a competitive forgetting-utility balance, while preserving utility better than other data-blind forget-set constructions.

</details>


### [58] [Breaking the Structural Identity: Personalized Federated LoRA Fine-tuning under Rank Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00632)
*Lei Wang,Jieming Bian,Letian Zhang,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedRoRA框架，用于解决联邦学习中资源异质性和数据异质性带来的双重挑战，通过将LoRA适配器解耦为共享全局方向与个性化逐秩幅度（带可学习对角缩放），在服务器端利用SVD提取全局子空间并通过个性化投影和top-k选择机制分发客户端特定初始化，在NLU和NLG基准上持续超越SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在隐私敏感、分布式数据集上的适配仍是挑战；现有FL+LoRA虽然提供了资源高效的协同微调范式，但实际部署受资源异质性和数据异质性双重阻碍；现有秩异构方法主要专注于拼接维度不匹配以进行聚合，通常为所有共享相同秩的客户端提供统一的全局模型，无法在非IID场景中捕捉客户端特定特征。

Method: FedRoRA将LoRA适配解耦为共享的全局方向和由可学习对角缩放控制的个性化逐秩幅度。服务器端通过SVD提取全局子空间，并用过个性化投影和top-k选择机制重新分配客户端特定初始化，实现细粒度的秩内个性化。

Result: 在NLU和NLG基准上的大量实验表明，FedRoRA持续优于现有最先进的方法。

Conclusion: FedRoRA通过解耦共享全局方向与个性化逐秩幅度，在秩异构联邦中实现了细粒度个性化，为资源异质与数据异质并存的联邦微调场景提供了一种有效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable success across diverse domains, but their adaptation to privacy-sensitive, distributed datasets remains a challenge. While Federated Learning (FL) combined with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) provides a resource-efficient paradigm for collaborative fine-tuning, practical deployments are hindered by the dual challenges of resource heterogeneity and data heterogeneity. Existing rank-heterogeneous methods primarily focus on bridging dimension mismatches for aggregation but typically provide a unified global model for all clients sharing the same rank, failing to capture client-specific features in non-IID scenarios. In this paper, we propose FedRoRA (Federated Rank-wise Personalized LoRA), a novel framework that enables fine-grained personalization within rank-heterogeneous federations. FedRoRA decouples adaptation into shared global directions and personalized rank-wise magnitudes governed by learnable diagonal scales. On the server side, it extracts a global subspace via singular value decomposition (SVD) and redistributes client-specific initializations through a personalized projection and top-$k$ selection mechanism. Extensive experiments on NLU and NLG benchmarks demonstrate that FedRoRA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [59] [DK-GBMKKM: Dynamic Kernel-Space Granular-Ball Multiple Kernel $k$-Means Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00647)
*Xiaoyu Lian,Yuchao Zhang,Shuyin Xia,Siqi Zhong,Xuzhao Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出动态核空间粒度球多核k-means聚类方法(DK-GBMKKM)。该方法在当前融合核空间中动态生成粒度球，并交替更新核权重与粒度球隶属度，使表示能适应融合核几何结构的变化，同时构造样本量加权的粒度球核矩阵以保证正半定性及相关等价性质，在12个公共数据集上验证了聚类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多核k-means虽能融合互补的非线性相似性，但其逐样本优化对噪声和边界样本敏感，且重复操作于样本级核矩阵代价高昂；而粒度球表示虽可将局部样本组织为介观单元，但仅在输入空间一次性生成的粒度球可能无法与多核学习过程中动态演化的融合核几何保持一致。

Method: 提出DK-GBMKKM方法：在当前融合核空间中生成粒度球，并交替进行核权重学习与粒度球隶属度更新，使粒度球表示能够随融合核几何变化而自适应调整；进一步构造样本量加权的粒度球核函数，并证明其半正定性与相关等价性质。

Result: 在12个公共数据集上的实验表明DK-GBMKKM具有较强的整体聚类性能，代码已开源供复现。

Conclusion: 通过将粒度球表示嵌入动态融合核空间并交替优化核权重与粒度球成员关系，DK-GBMKKM克服了传统多核聚类对噪声敏感及静态粒度球表示失配的问题，在聚类精度上取得了有竞争力的结果。

Abstract: Multiple kernel $k$-means integrates complementary nonlinear similarities by learning a combination of base kernels. Its pointwise optimization, however, is sensitive to noisy and boundary samples and repeatedly operates on sample-scale kernel matrices. Granular-ball representations organize local sample groups into mesoscopic units, but granular balls generated once in the input space may be inconsistent with the fused-kernel geometry that evolves during multiple kernel learning. We propose dynamic kernel-space granular-ball multiple kernel $k$-means (DK-GBMKKM). The method generates granular balls in the current fused kernel space and alternates kernel-weight learning with granular-ball membership updates, allowing the representation to adapt to changes in the fused-kernel geometry. A sample-size-weighted granular-ball kernel is further constructed to preserve the contributions of balls of different sizes, and its positive semidefiniteness and related equivalence properties are established. Experiments on 12 public datasets demonstrate the strong overall clustering performance of DK-GBMKKM. The code has been open-sourced for reproducibility: https://github.com/lianxiaoyu724/DK-GBMKKM.

</details>


### [60] [EEG-AS: Instance-Level Foundation Model Selection for EEG Foundation Models via Behavior Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00653)
*Yunzhen Zhang,Ruoxi Piao,Hasan Onur Keles,Mustafa Misir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EEG-AS，一个面向脑电（EEG）基础模型的实例级算法选择框架，通过使用推理可用的潜在EEG嵌入、手工神经生理特征和锚定基础模型来表征实例，在无需运行完整模型组合的情况下估计各基础模型的行为，从而在七个EEG基础模型中进行高效选择。在七个公共基准上的实验表明，该框架显著缩小了单个最优求解器与实例级oracle上界之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型在各类神经解码任务中表现优异，但没有任何单一基础模型能在所有数据集或个体EEG实例上持续保持最佳性能。实例级别的模型选择问题此前基本未被探索，需要在推理时为每个EEG实例动态选择最合适的基础模型。

Method: 将EEG基础模型选择形式化为实例级算法选择（AS）问题。EEG-AS框架利用三类特征表征实例：推理可获得的潜在EEG嵌入、手工神经生理学特征以及一个锚定基础模型。训练阶段，框架学习在锚定基础模型的条件下，从特权预测token中重建不可用的基础模型行为；推理阶段，则无需执行完整模型组合即可估计这些行为，从而实现从七个EEG基础模型中进行高效选择。

Result: 在七个公共EEG基准上的实验表明，EEG-AS显著缩小了单个最优求解器（SBS）与每个实例的oracle上界之间的性能差距，验证了实例级算法选择在EEG基础模型自适应部署中的有效性。

Conclusion: 实例级算法选择为EEG基础模型的自适应部署提供了一条有效的路径，能够在不牺牲推理效率的前提下逼近oracle最优性能，为EEG基础模型的实用化部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is a non-invasive technique for measuring neural activity and has been widely used in neuroscience applications. Recent advances in EEG foundation models have enabled strong performance across diverse neural decoding tasks. However, no single foundation model consistently performs best across datasets or individual EEG instances, while instance-level model selection remains largely unexplored. To address this limitation, we formulate EEG foundation model selection as an instance-level Algorithm Selection (AS) problem. We propose \textbf{EEG-AS}, an instance-level algorithm selection framework that characterizes each EEG instance using inference-available latent EEG embeddings, handcrafted neurophysiological features, and an anchor foundation model. During training, EEG-AS learns to reconstruct unavailable foundation-model behaviors from privileged prediction tokens conditioned on an anchor foundation model, while during inference it estimates these behaviors without executing the entire model portfolio, enabling efficient selection from seven EEG foundation models. Experiments on seven public EEG benchmarks demonstrate that EEG-AS substantially narrows the gap between the Single Best Solver (SBS) and the oracle upper bound for each instance. These results highlight the effectiveness of instance-level AS for adaptive deployment of EEG foundation models.

</details>


### [61] [HarmoCore: Functional Latent Diffusion for Sparse Reconstruction of Oscillatory Wave Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00679)
*Lihao Chen,Xinyu Zhang,Panqi Chen,Lei Cheng,Ting Zhang,Jianlong Li,Shikai Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HarmoCore提出了一种在紧凑连续的结构化波场潜空间中进行生成先验的框架，用于从极端稀疏传感器重建振荡复值波场。该方法通过Functional Tucker核表示复数实-虚通道，学习频率条件化的核扩散先验，并直接在核空间执行扩散后验采样，在2D/3D Helmholtz及合成波场上以1%-2%传感率取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏传感器重建振荡波场是一个严重欠定的逆问题。波场响应为复值、频率敏感且高度振荡，仿真与传感成本高昂导致观测极度稀疏。现有低秩、算子及扩散方法主要面向实值且较平滑的场，密集像素空间的扩散方法对振荡复值场效率低下且难以扩展到3D。

Method: HarmoCore将生成先验置于一个紧凑、连续且结构化的波场潜空间中：用Functional Tucker核在共享连续空间基上表示实-虚联合通道，学习频率条件化的核扩散先验，并在核空间直接执行Diffusion Posterior Sampling。由于解码器为多线性结构，在固定传感器坐标处可推导出显式似然引导算子，避免密集像素空间修正；可选的目标方程残差引导进一步增强物理一致性。

Result: 在2D Helmholtz、2D合成波场和3D Helmholtz上的实验表明，在1%-2%的极低传感率下该方法相比基线有显著增益，且在实际3D场景中仍保持计算可行性。

Conclusion: HarmoCore通过在紧凑结构化潜空间中引入生成先验并利用多线性解码器推导显式似然引导，有效解决了极端稀疏观测下振荡复值波场重建这一严重欠定问题，兼顾了重建质量、物理一致性与3D可扩展性。

Abstract: Reconstructing oscillatory wave fields from scattered sensors is a severely underdetermined inverse problem. Beyond the challenges of general physical-field reconstruction, wave responses are complex-valued, frequency-sensitive, and highly oscillatory, while costly simulation and sensing often leave only extreme-sparse observations. Existing low-rank, operator, and diffusion approaches are largely designed for real-valued, smoother fields; dense pixel-space diffusion is particularly inefficient for oscillatory complex fields and difficult to scale to 3D. We propose HarmoCore, which places a generative prior in a compact, continuous, and structured wave-field latent. HarmoCore represents joint real--imaginary channels with Functional Tucker cores over shared continuous spatial bases, learns a frequency-conditioned core diffusion prior, and performs Diffusion Posterior Sampling directly in core space. At fixed sensor coordinates, the multilinear decoder induces an explicit likelihood guidance operator, avoiding dense pixel-space correction. Optional target-equation residual guidance further promotes physical consistency. Experiments on 2D Helmholtz, 2D synthetic wave fields, and 3D Helmholtz show substantial gains under 1%--2% sensing while remaining practical in three dimensions.

</details>


### [62] [A Study of Hidden-State Optimization Order in Predictive Coding Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00686)
*Xueyuan Li,Danilo Vasconcellos Vargas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"边界优先"的推理调度方法以改进基于局部学习的深度网络中的特征学习。该方法将模型划分为多个块，先协调块边界的隐状态，再细化块内表示，并在预测编码网络（PCNs）中实例化。在CIFAR-10上，相比标准预测编码，准确率在标准参数化下提升9.77%，在μ-参数化下提升5.51%，且诊断分析表明特征学习更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部学习方法作为端到端反向传播的替代方案，其无结构的局部目标会导致深层网络中特征学习较弱。作者尝试探究隐状态优化的顺序能否解决这一局限性。

Method: 提出边界优先推理调度：将模型划分为多个块（chunks），首先协调块边界处的隐状态，再在各块内部细化表示。该调度被实例化在预测编码网络（PCNs）中——一种在推理过程中显式暴露隐活动与预测误差的局部学习框架。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，边界优先的预测编码实例相比标准预测编码：标准参数化下准确率提升9.77%，μ-参数化下提升5.51%。诊断分析显示更多非平凡的早期层更新、更低的初始到最终CKA相似度以及更多样化的层间梯度，均与更强的特征学习一致。

Conclusion: 边界优先、基于块划分的推理可作为预测编码训练中实用的设计原则，并值得在更广泛的局部学习系统中进一步研究。

Abstract: Local learning methods offer an alternative to end-to-end backpropagation, but their unstructured local objectives can produce weak feature learning in deep networks. We study whether the order of hidden-state optimization can address this limitation. We propose a boundary-first inference schedule that partitions a model into chunks, first coordinates hidden states at chunk boundaries, and then refines representations within each chunk. We instantiate this schedule in predictive coding networks (PCNs), a local-learning framework in which hidden activities and prediction errors are explicitly exposed during inference. On CIFAR-10, the resulting boundary-first predictive-coding instantiation improves accuracy over standard predictive coding by $9.77\%$ under a standard parametrization and by $5.51\%$ under a $μ$-parametrization. Diagnostic analyses further show more non-trivial early-layer updates, lower initial-to-final CKA, and more diverse layerwise gradients, consistent with stronger feature learning. These results support boundary-first, chunk-based inference as a practical design principle for predictive-coding training and motivate its study in broader local-learning systems.

</details>


### [63] [MUGEN: Generating Unlearnable Graph Examples for Multiple Learning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00696)
*Ziyan Liu,Chengshuai Zhao,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MUGEN是首个能够联合保护所有下游任务（节点分类、图分类、链路预测）的不可学习图样本生成框架，通过任务对齐可分性目标（TASO）和类型自适应扰动（TAP）实现跨GNN骨干网络和任务的稳定保护效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图数据在不同领域蕴含着有价值的关联信息，可能被未经授权的表示学习所利用。现有方法只能针对单一指定的下游任务生成不可学习图样本，这使得数据拥有者无法在发布前预料到所有可能的使用场景——数据可能针对某一任务被保护，但在节点分类、图分类或链路预测等其他任务上仍然可被学习。

Method: MUGEN框架从一份干净数据集中生成单一的特征扰动发布版本，通过共享GNN编码器和任务特定头来同时保护所有启用的任务。框架包含两个核心组件：(1)任务对齐可分性目标（TASO），利用任务预测和类间可分性来增强不可学习性及其在GNN骨干网络和任务间的迁移能力；(2)类型自适应扰动（TAP），针对节点属性类型定制扰动优化——对离散节点属性采用直接搜索可行硬翻转并只接受能降低损失的更新，对连续节点特征采用定制化的基于梯度的更新。

Result: 在五个基准数据集、四种骨干网络和三种学习范式上的实验表明，MUGEN生成的不可学习图样本能够跨GNN骨干网络和全部三种任务实现可迁移的保护效果，并且在对抗训练和数据增强下仍然保持有效。

Conclusion: MUGEN提出了一种面向多任务场景的统一数据级保护方案，解决了现有方法功能单一、无法应对未知下游任务的问题，显著提升了图数据发布时的鲁棒性和泛化保护能力。

Abstract: Graph data across diverse domains can expose valuable relational information to unauthorized representation learning, creating a pressing need for protection against such misuse. Unlearnable examples offer a data-level defense by perturbing a training release so that models trained on it fail to generalize to clean data. Existing methods generate unlearnable graph examples for only a specified downstream task. Consequently, a release protected against one task may remain learnable for other plausible uses, including node classification, graph classification, and link prediction, which the data owner cannot anticipate. We introduce MUGEN, to our knowledge the first framework for generating unlearnable graph examples that jointly protect all enabled tasks. From one clean dataset, MUGEN produces a single feature-perturbed release that protects every enabled task through a shared GNN encoder and task-specific heads. We devise a Task-Aligned Separability Objective (TASO), which leverages task prediction and classwise separability to strengthen unlearnability and its transfer across GNN backbones and enabled tasks. We further introduce Type-Adaptive Perturbation (TAP), which tailors perturbation optimization to node-attribute type, with direct search over feasible hard flips that accept only loss-improving updates for discrete node attributes and customized gradient-based updates for continuous node features, thereby enabling strong unlearnability across both settings. Experiments across five benchmarks, four backends and three learning paradigms demonstrate that MUGEN generates transferable unlearnable graph examples across GNN backbones and all three tasks, and remains effective under adversarial training and data augmentation.

</details>


### [64] [Patterning in Practice: Debiasing Reward Models with Susceptibilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00699)
*George Wang,Elizabeth Donoway,Daniel Murfet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将基于奇异学习理论（SLT）的"patterning"方法应用于大规模真实场景：通过对Skywork-Reward-Preference v0.2上训练的Gemma 2 9B Instruct奖励模型按敏感性（susceptibility）对偏好对重新加权，成功消除了长度、格式等风格偏差。在RM-Bench Hard上获得+14.2±1.2 pp的提升（超过对照方法SteerRM的+13.2 pp），同时总体精度保持；权重可直接迁移至Gemma 2 2B/27B和部分迁移至Llama 3.1 8B，且干预效果可通过可解释的权重追踪到少量训练样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于人类偏好训练的奖励模型存在长度、格式及其他风格性偏差，导致其评分偏离真实质量。现有去偏方法难以在大规模模型上有效应用，且缺乏可解释性。本文旨在利用奇异学习理论中的patterning方法，通过对训练偏好对按其对基准损失后验期望的影响（susceptibility）进行重新加权，来消除这些风格偏差，并验证其在大规模真实场景中的有效性、可解释性和迁移性。

Method: 采用patterning方法：为每个偏好对计算其"被测量的susceptibility"——即该pair对基准损失的后验期望值的影响权重——并据此重新加权训练数据，以消除风格偏差。在Gemma 2 9B Instruct上训练于Skywork-Reward-Preference v0.2的奖励模型上应用该方法，并在RM-Bench的Hard分裂（风格线索与正确答案相悖的场景）中评估。通过消融实验追踪安全子集上的副作用来源，并测试权重跨模型（Gemma 2 2B/27B、Llama 3.1 8B）的迁移性。

Result: 在RM-Bench Hard分裂上获得+14.2±1.2 pp的精度提升（5个种子均值±标准误），优于公开对照方法SteerRM的+13.2 pp；RM-Bench总体精度保持不变。在简单案例中通过消融实验将安全子集上的回归副作用追溯到一小类训练pair，验证了干预的可解释性。权重在Gemma 2 2B和27B上无需重新计算即可直接迁移，部分迁移至Llama 3.1 8B。

Conclusion: patterning方法首次成功应用于大规模真实奖励模型，能有效消除风格偏见并保持总体性能，同时具备可解释性与跨模型迁移能力，验证了奇异学习理论在实用去偏场景中的可行性。该方法在安全子集上的副作用可通过权重追踪到具体训练样本类并加以确认，为进一步理解奖励模型偏差来源提供了新途径。

Abstract: Reward models trained on human preferences are known to suffer from length, formatting, and other stylistic biases. In this paper we use patterning, which reweights each preference pair according to its measured effect on posterior expectation values of benchmark losses (its susceptibility), to debias a Gemma 2 9B Instruct reward model trained on Skywork-Reward-Preference v0.2. We obtain $+14.2 \pm 1.2$ pp on RM-Bench Hard, the split where style cues point against correctness (mean $\pm$ s.e.\ over 5 seeds), with overall RM-Bench accuracy preserved, comparable to the strongest Hard-split gain reported by the closest published comparator (SteerRM, $+13.2$ pp). We demonstrate in a simple case that the reweighting is interpretable by tracing a side effect of the intervention (a regression on a safety subset of RM-Bench) to a small class of training pairs, which we confirm by ablation. The weights also transfer: those computed on Gemma 2 9B debias Gemma 2 2B and 27B with no recomputation, and transfer partially to Llama 3.1 8B. This is the first application of patterning, a program grounded in singular learning theory, beyond small models and synthetic tasks.

</details>


### [65] [Online Self-Weighted Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00734)
*Haiquan Wen,Yiwei He,Bei Peng,Guangliang Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出在线自加权微调（OSW-FT），在标准SFT基础上仅用少量在线推理路径（如2次）估计模型当前成功率，并据此对专家轨迹的SFT损失进行权重调节，兼顾SFT的稳定性和RL的自适应更新强度。在Qwen3（0.6B至4B）多个推理基准上相较SFT一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SFT对所有专家演示赋予相同显式损失权重，不随模型在训练过程中能力的动态变化而调整；而基于RL的方法虽能自适应更新力度，但需要大量采样且在困难任务上不稳定。因此需要一个既能在线自适应更新强度，又保持SFT稳定性和低采样成本的方法。

Method: OSW-FT为每个查询用少量（仅推理、无梯度）rollout估计模型当前成功率，据此重新缩放标准SFT损失。优化方向仍锚定专家轨迹，更新幅度在线自适应。针对二值可验证推理任务，从梯度层面将所提加权与SFT及RL建立联系（受方差缩减启发），证明所得估计量对任意有限rollout数目的OSW-FT代理更新是无偏的，并分析了相应代理目标的收敛性。

Result: 在Qwen3系列（0.6B至4B）多个挑战性基准（如AIME）上，OSW-FT在中小规模模型上一致地优于SFT，且仅需2次在线rollout即可获得良好的计算-性能权衡。

Conclusion: OSW-FT是面向中小规模LLM在二值可验证推理任务上微调的实用方法，以极低的计算开销（2个在线rollout）结合SFT的稳定性与RL的自适应性，提供了有利的计算-性能均衡。

Abstract: Standard supervised fine-tuning (SFT) assigns the same explicit loss weight to every expert demonstration, regardless of the model's changing competence over training queries. Reinforcement learning (RL) based methods adapt update strength using model-generated rollouts, but often require substantially more sampling and can be unstable on hard tasks. We propose \textbf{Online Self-Weighted Fine-Tuning (OSW-FT)}, a simple method that augments SFT with online, trajectory-level weighting. For each query, OSW-FT estimates the model's current success rate using a small number of inference-only rollouts and rescales the standard SFT loss accordingly. The optimization direction remains anchored to the expert trajectory, while the update magnitude adapts online. For binary-verifiable reasoning, we connect this weighting to SFT and RL at the gradient level, inspired by variance-reduction principles. The resulting estimator is unbiased for the exact OSW-FT surrogate update for any finite rollout count, and we analyze convergence with respect to the corresponding surrogate objective. Evaluated across Qwen3 series ranging from 0.6B to 4B on multiple challenging benchmarks (e.g., AIME), OSW-FT consistently improves over SFT on small-to-medium scale models. OSW-FT offers a favorable compute-performance trade-off as a practical approach for fine-tuning small-to-medium LLMs on binary-verifiable reasoning tasks with only \textbf{2 online rollouts}.

</details>


### [66] [Text Capability Loss in Vision-Language Adaptation: An Attention-Sink Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00746)
*Minsik Choi,Geewook Kim,Young Geun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文发现将预训练LLM微调为视觉语言模型(VLM)会损害骨干模型的文本能力，尤其是需要遵循严格输出格式的任务（如指令遵循、思维链推理等）。作者将这一退化归因于"注意力汇聚破坏"（attention-sink corruption），并提出一个名为Sink Strength的单一标量指标，可在数秒内基于单个GPU在基础LLM上计算，无需任何VL训练即可预测VL适应后的性能退化程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解并预测VLM微调导致的文本能力退化机制，以便在VL训练前筛选骨干模型，避免"事后发现、难以补救"的困境。

Method: ①定位退化根源：VL微调扰动早期sink位置（该位置锚定大量注意力概率），基础LLM保留sink的程度与其能力存活率相关。②提出Sink Strength：在基础LLM上计算的单一标量，数秒内于单GPU完成，用于预测VL训练后的退化程度。③验证多个基线方法：后训练注入QK-RMSNorm无法复制原生QK-RMSNorm的保护作用；多种现成权重合并设置无法在VL训练后恢复丢失能力。

Result: Sink Strength在六个VLM-LLM配对及多种格式敏感任务上能一致地追踪相对退化程度。QK-RMSNorm后训练注入和权重合并方法均为负面结果，无法恢复或保护被破坏的能力。

Conclusion: Sink Strength可作为VL训练前的骨干模型筛选诊断工具，且以上负面结果缩小了干预空间，将方向指向训练期间针对头部（head-selective）的保护策略。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a pretrained LLM into a vision-language model (VLM) can erode the backbone's text capability, with the damage concentrated on tasks that require following exact output rules, such as instruction following, chain-of-thought reasoning graded on a strictly parsed final answer, and similar evaluations with strict graders. We trace this gap to attention-sink corruption: VL fine-tuning perturbs the early sink position that anchors a large fraction of attention probability, and how well the base LLM preserves its sink tracks how much of the affected capability survives adaptation. Building on this view, we introduce Sink Strength, a single scalar computed on the base LLM in a few seconds on a single GPU that predicts post-VL degradation without any VL training. It consistently tracks relative degradation across the six VLM-LLM pairs and multiple format-sensitive tasks. Complementing this diagnostic, we find that post-pretraining QK-RMSNorm injection fails to reproduce the protection of native QK-RMSNorm, while several off-the-shelf weight-merging settings fail to recover the lost capability after VL training. These negative results underscore the value of screening backbones with Sink Strength before VL training and narrow the intervention space toward head-selective training-time protection.

</details>


### [67] [How Do Language Models Choose Between Context and Memory?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00753)
*Benjamin Shih,John Winnicki,Arianna Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过反事实实验，研究了语言模型在上下文信息与参数知识冲突时如何利用激活方向（authority directions）来决定遵循哪个信息来源。实验发现这些方向确实在因果上被模型自然使用（可再现30-68%的权威性转移），但跨任务的因果复用能力很弱（仅弥合9%的权威差距），说明权威性计算是任务相关的，而非跨任务通用的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作中激活方向的干预研究缺乏严格的因果性检验：一方面不清楚未编辑的模型是否天然使用该方向，另一方面不清楚学到的方向是否可跨任务复用。作者希望在无歧义的环境中通过反事实对照实验来厘清"表示-因果使用-跨任务因果重用"这三个层次的区别。

Method: 作者从一致性提示（context和参数知识支持相同答案）中估计权威性方向，然后在匹配的提示间交换沿这些方向的自然出现的坐标。这些提示被设计为强制模型优先选择所提供的上下文或参数知识。实验覆盖Qwen、Llama和OLMo三种模型家族。同时，在两项独立任务上分别学习权威方向，检验跨任务迁移效果，并与在同一任务上学习的方向进行对比。

Result: 干预操作可以再现30-68%由权威性诱导引起的来源选择偏移，而匹配的对照组几乎无法再现任何偏移。跨任务迁移仅能弥合9%的权威性差距（71%→80%），而针对特定任务本地学习的方向能弥合57%的差距（71%→100%）。

Conclusion: 研究表明权威性方向不仅是被表示的，而且在因果上被模型使用；但其因果效用是高度任务特定的，权威性计算可能是任务依赖的，而非跨任务共享的可复用机制。

Abstract: When contextual information conflicts with the knowledge stored in model parameters, activation directions can be used to decode and steer which source the model follows. However, steering along a direction does not establish causality: whether the unedited model would naturally use that direction or whether the direction is reusable across tasks. We test these distinctions through counterfactual experiments in unambiguous settings. First, we estimate authority directions from agreement prompts, in which the context and parametric knowledge support the same answer. We then interchange naturally occurring coordinates along these directions between matched prompts that direct the model to prioritize either the supplied context or its parametric knowledge. Across Qwen, Llama, and OLMo models, this intervention reproduces 30-68% of the authority-induced shift in source choice, whereas matched controls reproduce almost none. To test cross-task reuse, we learn authority directions on two tasks separately and see that cross-task transferability closes only 9% of the authority gap while the local direction learned on the given task closes 57%. These results distinguish authority representation, causal use, and cross-task causal reuse, and suggest that authority computations may be task-dependent, rather than reusable across tasks.

</details>


### [68] [Frozen Cores Need Task Signal: Fisher-Whitened Cross-Covariance for Low-Resource LLM Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00762)
*Wentao Ye,Zhanming Shen,Zhiqing Xiao,Yao Ding,Haobo Wang,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出FCCA方法，在冻结核心（frozen-core）适配框架下，通过校准阶段用输入-误差交叉协方差加对角Fisher白化来选择低秩子空间，使微调仅优化r×r核心矩阵。在11个任务、4种模型设置、3个种子下，FCCA在Qwen2.5-3B上达到83.0宏平均分，比次优方法高2.3分，并在仅优化36.9K参数的情况下接近LoRA和DoRA的性能（差距0.32和0.23分），而后者需约7.4M参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调通常只关注更新多少参数，但在严格的训练参数预算下，这些系数作用于哪里同等重要。现有LoRA等方法允许可训练因子在训练中修复较差的初始子空间，掩盖了子空间质量的影响。作者希望通过固定的左右基座使子空间质量直接可观测，从而研究在哪选择低秩方向最有效。

Method: FCCA方法包含以下步骤：(1) 冻结核心适配（frozen-core adaptation）框架：校准阶段为每个权重矩阵固定左右基（left/right bases），微调仅优化r×r核心；(2) FCCA估计带符号的输入-误差交叉协方差，用对角Fisher矩进行白化；(3) 在局部度量下截断所选方向，映射回原始空间；(4) 应用薄QR分解获得稳定的核心坐标。同时比较了8种基构造器。

Result: 在11个任务、4种模型设置、3个种子的对比中：FCCA在Qwen2.5-3B上达到83.0宏平均分，比次优匹配预算方法高2.3分，在全部11个任务上超越未白化的RawGrad对照组；在三个Qwen规模上均排名第一，在Llama-3.2-1B上与最佳方法差距仅0.13分。消融实验显示白化带来2.7-17.2分提升，QR分解对稳定核心优化不可或缺。FCCA与LoRA和DoRA的差距仅0.32和0.23平均分，但只优化36.9K参数而非约7.4M参数。

Conclusion: 精心选择的固定子空间可以恢复可移动低秩因子的大部分收益，同时大幅降低可训练参数和优化器状态开销。结果表明在严格的参数预算下，子空间的选择质量比可训练系数的位置灵活性更为关键，白化和QR分解等环节对核心优化的稳定性和性能至关重要。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning is usually framed as a question of how many parameters to update. Under a severe trainable-state budget, however, where those coefficients act is equally consequential. We study this choice through frozen-core adaptation: a calibration pass fixes left and right bases for each weight matrix, and fine-tuning optimizes only an $r\times r$ core. This removes the ability of trainable factors to repair a poor initial span and makes subspace quality directly observable. We introduce FCCA, which estimates the signed input--error cross-covariance, whitens it with diagonal Fisher moments, truncates it in the resulting local metric, maps the selected directions back, and applies thin QR to obtain stable core coordinates. Under a matched $r^2$ budget, we compare eight basis constructors on 11 tasks, four model settings, and three seeds. On Qwen2.5-3B, FCCA reaches an 83.0 macro-average, 2.3 points above the next-best matched-budget constructor, and exceeds its unwhitened RawGrad control on all 11 tasks. It ranks first at all three Qwen scales and finishes within 0.13 points of the best method on Llama-3.2-1B. Controlled ablations show gains of 2.7--17.2 points from whitening and identify QR as necessary for stable core optimization in the tested regime. Finally, FCCA comes within 0.32 and 0.23 average points of LoRA and DoRA while optimizing 36.9K rather than roughly 7.4M parameters. These results show that a carefully selected fixed span can recover most of the benefit of movable low-rank factors at a much smaller trainable and optimizer-state cost.

</details>


### [69] [Are You Thinking What I am Thinking? : Examining Conceptual Separation in Neural Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00764)
*Jaee Ponde,Roshni Agarwal,Subhashis Banerjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经网络内部激活中概念分离现象：同一概念的例子是否形成连贯表征，相关概念在表征空间中是否更接近。通过几何和分布分析发现，CNN中熟悉ImageNet概念形成连贯且语义有序的表示，但该连贯性对未见概念和域偏移减弱；LLM中清晰不同域保持分离，相关子域靠近，模糊主题间区分在均值和协方差层面均崩塌。结果表明概念分离可揭示仅靠输出精度无法显现的结构，可作为模型概念表征稳健性的诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络被广泛用于识别明确定义和模糊的概念，但输出级指标无法揭示概念如何在模型内部被表征。作者希望探究网络是否表现出概念分离现象，即同一概念的例子是否形成连贯表征、相关概念在表征空间中是否距离更近，以此提供超越输出准确率的内部表征诊断视角。

Method: 对CNN和LLM的内部激活进行几何与分布分析（如均值级与协方差级测度），系统评估CNN在ImageNet熟悉概念、未见概念和类内域偏移情境下的概念组织方式；同时评估LLM在清晰不同域、相关子域和模糊主题间的概念分离程度。

Result: CNN中熟悉ImageNet概念形成连贯且语义有序的表示，但对未见概念（连贯性减弱）和类内域偏移（连贯性受损）鲁棒性下降；LLM中清晰域保持良好分离，相关子域在表征空间中靠近，而模糊主题间的区分在均值和协方差层面均显著崩塌。

Conclusion: 概念分离能够揭示输出精度无法反映的内部结构，可作为评估模型概念表征稳健性的有效诊断指标；代码和数据已在GitHub开源。

Abstract: Neural networks are increasingly employed to identify both well-defined and ambiguous concepts, yet output-level metrics reveal little about how those concepts are represented internally. Our study asks if these networks exhibit \textit{conceptual separation}: if examples of the same concept form coherent representations, and whether related concepts lie closer together in the representation space. We examine this conceptual organisation in Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Large Language Models (LLMs) through geometric and distributional analysis of their internal activations. In CNNs, familiar ImageNet concepts form coherent and semantically ordered representations, while this coherence weakens for unseen concepts and suffers within-class domain shift. In LLMs, clearly distinct domains remain well separated, related subdomains move closer together, and the distinction between ambiguous topics collapses at both the mean and covariance level. These results suggest that conceptual separation can reveal structure that output accuracy alone cannot, and may serve as a useful diagnostic of how robustly a model represents the concepts it is asked to identify. Code and data available on \href{https://github.com/JaeeRoshniCapstoneProject/Are-You-Thinking-What-I-m-Thinking-Examining-Conceptual-Separation-in-Neural-Architectures}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [70] [HarnessEvolve: Learning from Reference Trajectories for Reliable Agent Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00829)
*Wen Jiang,Mingmin Chu,Yimeng Tian,Qianxin Zhang,Haofei Yang,Rui Yang,Yang Liu,Tao Lv,Fangming Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HarnessEvolve是一个自我进化智能体框架，通过参考轨迹学习来实现可靠的自进化，解决了信用分配失败、捷径学习和灾难性遗忘三大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我进化智能体通过优化自身harness（提示、技能、工具和执行逻辑）来追求自主性，但面临三大核心挑战：信用分配失败（终端成败反馈难以定位出错步骤）、捷径学习（记忆任务特定模式而非泛化能力）、以及灾难性遗忘（无约束更新破坏已有能力）。现有方法缺乏系统性解决方案。

Method: HarnessEvolve将执行智能体与进化管线解耦，将执行、评估、优化和门控分配给独立的智能体模块。具体方法：(1) 通过生成参考轨迹（给定ground-truth答案时的执行路径）并与失败执行对齐来提取错误信号，聚类以发现系统性失败模式，解决信用分配问题；(2) 候选harness更新需通过两道门控——质量门（过滤数据泄漏和提示膨胀）和性能门（要求当前批次有改进同时不损害近期批次性能）；(3) 在留出集上进行epoch-end验证，选择表现最佳的已接受智能体快照。

Result: 在涵盖开放域和企业场景的多个基准上，使用不同模型和智能体框架进行大量实验，HarnessEvolve在所有基准和设置下均一致性地优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: HarnessEvolve通过参考轨迹学习和双门控机制，实现了跨任务域的可靠自我进化，验证了其在开放域和企业场景中的泛化性和稳定性。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents advance toward autonomy by optimizing their harness---prompts, skills, tools, and execution logic---based on environmental feedback. This paradigm, however, is hampered by three challenges: \textit{credit assignment failure}, where terminal success/failure feedback makes it ambiguous which step caused the error; \textit{shortcut learning}, where agents memorize task-specific patterns rather than acquire generalizable capabilities; and \textit{catastrophic forgetting}, where unguarded updates degrade previously acquired competence. In this paper, we introduce HarnessEvolve, a self-evolving framework that learns from reference trajectories to achieve reliable agent self-evolution. HarnessEvolve decouples the execution agent from the evolutionary pipeline, assigning execution, evaluation, optimization, and gating to independent agent modules, enabling generalizable and stable harness improvements. Specifically, HarnessEvolve overcomes credit assignment failure by generating reference trajectories (execution paths produced when given the ground-truth answers) and aligning failed executions against them to extract error signals, which are clustered to reveal systematic failure patterns. To prevent shortcut learning and catastrophic forgetting, candidate harness updates must pass two gates: a quality gate that filters data leakage and prompt bloat, and a performance gate that accepts each update if it improves on the current batch without degrading recent batches, with epoch-end validation on a held-out set selecting the best-performing accepted agent snapshot. We conduct extensive experiments on several benchmarks spanning open-domain and enterprise scenarios, using different models and agent frameworks. Results demonstrate that HarnessEvolve consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across all benchmarks and settings, confirming reliability across task domains.

</details>


### [71] [Conditional Flow Matching for ML-Based Inverse Design Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00863)
*Juliana Felder,Milad Habibi,Soheyl Massoudi,Mark Fuge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在EngiOpt框架中引入条件流匹配（CFM）生成模型，并与条件扩散模型和条件生成对抗网络（cGAN）在结构（beams2d）和热传导（heatconduction2d）两个EngiBench基准任务上进行比较。结果表明CFM在累积最优性差距（COG）、最终最优性差距（FOG）、最大均值差异（MMD）和体积分数偏差等指标上均优于基线方法，同时推理吞吐量比扩散模型高约66倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程逆向设计常受限于PDE约束优化问题中迭代求解器的高计算成本以及对初始化的敏感性。深度生成模型可在推理时避免重跑模拟器来生成候选设计，但现有方法存在局限：GAN单次前向即可采样，而扩散模型需要迭代式逆时积分，计算效率低。因此需要探索更高效且质量更好的生成式设计方法。

Method: 将条件流匹配（CFM）加入EngiOpt框架，与条件扩散模型和cGAN进行对比。在beams2d和heatconduction2d两个任务上，使用相同的下游优化协议进行评估。主要评价指标为累积最优性差距（COG）和最终最优性差距（FOG），同时辅以最大均值差异（MMD）和体积分数偏差等指标。生成的优化设计作为梯度细化优化的热启动。

Result: CFM在两个任务上均取得最低的COG、FOG、MMD和体积分数偏差。在体积分数偏差上，CFM在beams2d和heatconduction2d上分别为0.4%和1.0%，而扩散模型为3.8%和11.2%。Euler s=16时CFM在beams2d上达到53.2样本/秒，为使用1000次网络评估的扩散基线吞吐量的约66倍，其COG为1.182±3.126，与Euler s=32时的1.173±3.100相当。

Conclusion: 在两个任务上，CFM相比两种基线方法能产生更低COG的热启动优化设计，且相比扩散模型需要更少的网络评估次数，在生成质量和推理效率之间取得了更好的平衡，是工程逆向设计中有前景的生成建模方法。

Abstract: Engineering inverse design is often limited by the high computational cost of iterative solvers for optimization problems constrained by partial differential equations (PDEs) and by their sensitivity to initialization. Deep generative models can produce candidate designs without rerunning the simulator at inference time. Generative adversarial networks (GANs) sample in one forward pass, whereas diffusion models require iterative reverse-time integration.
  In this work, we add conditional flow matching (CFM) to EngiOpt and compare it with a conditional diffusion model and a conditional generative adversarial network (cGAN) on structural (beams2d) and thermal (heatconduction2d) benchmarks from EngiBench using the same downstream optimization protocol. We use cumulative optimality gap (COG) and final optimality gap (FOG) as the primary metrics for evaluating the generated designs as warm starts for gradient-based refinement. On the evaluated EngiOpt implementations and two EngiBench tasks, CFM achieves the lowest measured COG, FOG, maximum mean discrepancy (MMD), and volume-fraction deviation on both tasks. CFM has mean volume-fraction deviations of 0.4% and 1.0% on beams2d and heatconduction2d, respectively, compared with 3.8% and 11.2% for diffusion. At Euler s = 16, CFM achieves 53.2 samples/s on beams2d, about 66 times the measured throughput of the evaluated diffusion baseline using 1000 network evaluations under the same timing protocol, with COG 1.182 +/- 3.126, compared with 1.173 +/- 3.100 for Euler s = 32.
  Across the two tasks, CFM produces warm starts with lower measured COG than both baselines and uses fewer network evaluations than diffusion.

</details>


### [72] [MemoryWalker: Stop Training Agents on Contexts They Never Saw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00865)
*Zinco J,Xunjie Zhu,Shen Huang,Zhenyi Wang,Pengjun Xie,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究生产环境智能体框架（如Claude Code、Qwen-Agent）在压缩上下文（rollout时的上下文淘汰）下的训练问题。由于每次淘汰都会使有效历史产生分支，学习对象从序列变为树。现有线性化方法要么保留最右路径导致“时间穿越泄漏”，要么回放深度优先遍历导致训练与推理不匹配。作者提出两种精确且梯度等价的修正方法：LogitTree（分段K前向遍历）和打包的4D注意力掩码，并提出SDCC（用于条件一致性的自蒸馏），一种单次反向传播的变分松弛方法。实验表明精确方法保持无压缩基线水平，SDCC大幅缩小差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级智能体工具链在rollout期间会对上下文进行压缩（如上下文淘汰），但现有T训练框架未考虑这种压缩造成的条件化问题：每次淘汰都会改变有效历史，使目标函数变为一棵树而非序列。已有线性化方案存在时间穿越泄漏或训练推理不匹配的双重困境，亟需一种既能处理树结构又不牺牲训练效率的优化方法。

Method: 提出三种方法：(1) LogitTree，通过分割的K-forward遍历，对每个淘汰节点做精确的K+1次反向传播，实现梯度等价的树形目标优化；(2) 打包的4D注意力掩码，需自定义kernel和白盒淘汰记录，以单次/批量前向精确计算树形目标的梯度；(3) SDCC（Self-Distillation for Conditioning Consistency），一种单次反向传播的变分松弛：每次淘汰时，在重建的淘汰前前缀上，最小化压缩后的学生模型与stop-gradient教师模型之间的前向KL散度。每个junction处残余KL为epsilon_KL，给出O(sqrt(epsilon_KL))的train-deployment总变差界。SDCC还适用于黑盒harness。

Result: 在七个网络搜索基准上（使用TC-RAG、AgentFold、MemexRL、Claude Code和OpenCode），朴素训练会显著放大train-rollout对数概率差距，尤其在淘汰密集的batch上。精确方法（LogitTree和4D mask）保持在无压缩时的下限水平，而SDCC大幅缩小了该差距，同时实现更低的logit漂移和更高的rollout奖励。

Conclusion: 上下文压缩使训练目标变成树结构而非序列，现有线性化方法不够精确。LogitTree和4D注意力掩码提供了精确的梯度等价解法，但各有代价（前者多K次反向传播，后者需自定义kernel）。SDCC作为单次反向传播的实用近似，在不需白盒淘汰记录的情况下也能逼近精确解的收益，适用于实际生产系统的黑盒场景。

Abstract: Production agent harnesses such as Claude Code and Qwen-Agent compress context during rollout, but training under compression creates a conditioning problem: every eviction branches the effective history, so the learning object is a tree rather than a sequence. Existing linearizations either retain the rightmost path, causing time-travel leakage, or replay a depth-first traversal, causing train-inference mismatch. We introduce two exact, gradient-equivalent corrections: LogitTree, a segmented K-forward traversal, and a packed 4D attention mask. LogitTree requires K+1 backward passes; the 4D mask requires a custom kernel and white-box eviction records. We also propose SDCC (Self-Distillation for Conditioning Consistency), a single-backward-pass variational relaxation. At each eviction, it minimizes forward KL between the compressed student and a stop-gradient teacher on the reconstructed pre-eviction prefix. A residual per-junction KL of epsilon_KL gives an O(sqrt(epsilon_KL)) bound on the train-deployment total-variation gap. SDCC also applies to black-box harnesses. On seven web-search benchmarks with TC-RAG, AgentFold, MemexRL, Claude Code, and OpenCode, naive training inflates the train-rollout log-probability gap, especially on eviction-heavy batches. The exact methods stay at the no-compression floor, and SDCC substantially closes the gap, with lower logit drift and higher rollout rewards.

</details>


### [73] [iPINN for Broadband CARS Phase Retrieval: A Framework for Function Approximation and Inverse Modeling Problems in Nonlinear Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00883)
*Ravi Teja Vulchi,Carl Messerschmidt,Mohammadsadegh Vafaeinezhad,Rajendhar Junjuri,Tobias Meyer-Zedler,Juergen Popp,Thomas Bocklitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种逆物理信息神经网络(iPINN)，用于宽带相干反斯托克斯拉曼光谱(BCARS)中的相位恢复问题。iPINN通过预测Lorentzian峰参数并利用可微解析前向模型重建共振磁化率，采用transformer编码器将光谱特征分配到24个可学习的峰槽中，并通过多视角一致性损失确保对非共振背景模式、强度和噪声的不变性。在公开基准上达到最低误差(MAE 0.016)，且在28个跨7种溶剂和4个焦平面的零样本测试光谱上，5/7的溶剂呈现深度不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BCARS中的相位恢复是一个病态逆问题：拉曼类信号蕴含在共振磁化率的虚部中，且与因采集条件而变化的非共振背景(NRB)相干混合。现有直接光谱回归方法在采集条件(如NRB模式、强度、噪声)变化时精度下降，亟需一种在多变测量条件下仍保持鲁棒性的相位恢复方法。

Method: 提出iPINN架构：transformer编码器将原始BCARS光谱特征映射到24个可学习峰槽中，每个槽预测Lorentzian峰参数(位置、宽度、振幅等)；随后通过一个可微分的解析前向物理模型重建共振磁化率。训练中引入多视角一致性损失，强制模型在不同NRB模式、NRB强度和噪声水平下预测保持一致。

Result: 在公开基准上，iPINN达到所有测试基线中最低误差(MAE 0.016，次优为0.046)。在28个零样本光谱上(覆盖7种溶剂与4个不同焦平面位置)，其中5/7的溶剂实现了深度不变的精度，表明方法具有出色的跨采集条件泛化能力。

Conclusion: 逆参数预测结合可微分物理解码器是实现跨测量条件鲁棒相位恢复的有效途径。相比直接光谱回归，将问题参数化到物理峰参数空间并嵌入可微前向模型，显著提升了BCARS相位恢复在不同采集条件下的准确性与泛化性。

Abstract: Phase retrieval in broadband coherent anti-Stokes Raman spectroscopy (BCARS) is an ill-posed inverse problem. The Raman-like signal is encoded in the imaginary part of the resonant susceptibility, which mixes coherently with a non-resonant background (NRB) that varies across acquisitions. We introduce an inverse physics-informed neural network (iPINN) that predicts Lorentzian peak parameters from raw BCARS spectra and reconstructs the resonant susceptibility through a differentiable analytical forward model. A transformer encoder assigns spectral features to 24 learnable peak slots, and a multi-view consistency loss enforces invariance across NRB pattern, NRB strength, and noise. Unlike direct spectral regression approaches, the method retains accuracy under varying acquisition conditions. On a public benchmark, iPINN achieves the lowest error among the tested baselines (MAE 0.016 vs. next-best 0.046). On 28 zero-shot test spectra acquired across seven solvents and four focal positions, accuracy is depth-invariant in five of seven solvents. These results show that inverse parametric prediction with a differentiable physical decoder supports robust phase retrieval across measurement conditions.

</details>


### [74] [Poisson-Gamma Dynamical Systems with Time-varying Transition Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00896)
*Jiahao Wang,Yijun Wang,Nan Fang,Sikun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种带时变转移核的泊松-伽马动态系统（TV-PGDS），通过三种Dirichlet马尔可夫链结构（Dir-Dir、Dir-Gam-Dir、PR-Gam-Dir）建模随时间演化的转移矩阵，以捕捉真实计数时间序列中常见的异质结构突变，并利用Dirichlet-Multinomial-Beta数据增广技术设计了全共轭的高效Gibbs采样器进行后验推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有的泊松-伽马动态系统（PGDS）能够有效捕捉计数序列的潜在演化动态，但其转移核（transition kernel）是静态的，无法刻画真实世界计数时间序列中普遍存在的时变转移动态（如结构突变、依赖关系随时间变化等），这限制了模型的表达能力。

Method: 提出TV-PGDS模型，允许底层转移矩阵随时间演化。具体设计了三种Dirichlet马尔可夫链（Dir-Dir、Dir-Gam-Dir、PR-Gam-Dir）来分别容纳依赖关系中不同类型的异质结构突变。在推断方面，利用Dirichlet-Multinomial-Beta数据增广技术，构建了一个全共轭且高效的Gibbs采样器用于后验模拟。

Result: 与相关基线模型相比，实验表明所提出的TV-PGDS因能学习由时变转移矩阵所捕捉的时变依赖结构，取得了更优的预测性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入随时间演化的转移核，TV-PGDS有效弥补了传统PGDS在刻画时变转移动态方面的不足，具备更强的对真实计数时间序列依赖结构的建模与预测能力。

Abstract: Bayesian methodologies for handling count-valued time series have gained prominence due to their ability to infer interpretable latent structures and to estimate uncertainties. Among these Bayesian models, Poisson-Gamma Dynamical Systems (PGDSs) are proven to be effective in capturing the evolving dynamics underlying observed count sequences. However, the state-of-the-art PGDS still falls short in capturing the transition dynamics that are commonly observed in real-world count time series. To mitigate this limitation, a PGDS with time-varying transition kernel (TV-PGDS), is proposed to allow the underlying transition matrices to evolve over time. Three specifically-designed Dirichlet Markov chains (Dir-Dir, Dir-Gam-Dir, PR-Gam-Dir) are constructed to accommodate heterogeneous structural mutations within these dependencies. Leveraging Dirichlet-Multinomial-Beta data augmentation techniques, a fully-conjugate and efficient Gibbs sampler is developed to perform posterior simulation. Experiments show that, in comparison with related models, the proposed PGDS achieves improved predictive performance due to its capacity to learn time-varying dependency structure captured by the time-evolving transition matrices.

</details>


### [75] [SAGE: Subpopulation-Aware Generative Enhancement for Mitigating Spurious Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01051)
*Yiming Luo,Rongqiang Zhao,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAGE是一个两阶段的生成式增强框架，在没有群体标签的情况下，通过聚类得到的子标签和类别标签来微调条件生成模型，生成针对性合成数据以填补训练集中代表性不足的区域，并构建平衡的验证集用于最后一层重加权。在Waterbirds、CelebA和MetaShift上分别达到89.5%、85.7%和79.1%的最差组准确率，超越最佳无组标签基线最多7.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚假相关性（spurious correlations）对现代机器学习鲁棒性构成重大挑战。数据集分布的固有失衡使传统ERM模型倾向于依赖大多数群体的虚假属性进行分类，导致少数群体性能不佳。在虚假属性不可获取的无组标签场景下，现有方法通常对少数群体或误分类样本进行上采样，但重复相同实例会降低有效多样性并鼓励过拟合。

Method: SAGE采用两阶段生成式增强框架：(1) 使用聚类派生的子标签和类别标签微调条件生成模型与文本编码器；(2) 生成针对性的合成数据以填充训练集中代表性不足区域，并构建平衡验证集用于最后层重加权（last-layer reweighting）。

Result: 在Waterbirds、CelebA和MetaShift上分别达到89.5%、85.7%和79.1%的最差组准确率，超越最佳无组标签基线最多7.7个百分点。

Conclusion: SAGE从数据视角有效缓解了无先验知识下的虚假相关性问题，通过生成式数据增强替代传统上采样方法，在保留数据多样性的同时提升少数群体性能，验证了生成式增强作为数据层面缓解策略的有效性。

Abstract: Spurious correlations pose a significant challenge to the robustness of modern machine learning. The inherent imbalance in dataset distributions often leads traditional Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM) models to rely on majority spurious attributes for classification, resulting in poor performance on minority groups. This problem becomes particularly challenging when the spurious attributes are unavailable. Existing group-label-free methods often upsample minority groups or misclassified real training examples; repeating the same instances can reduce effective diversity and encourage overfitting. To mitigate these spurious correlations from a data-centric perspective in the absence of prior knowledge, we introduce Subpopulation-Aware Generative Enhancement (SAGE), a two-stage generative augmentation framework. Using cluster-derived sub-labels and class labels, we fine-tune a conditional generative model and text encoder, generating targeted synthetic data to fill underrepresented regions in the training set and construct a balanced validation set for last-layer reweighting. We experimentally show that SAGE achieves 89.5%, 85.7%, and 79.1% worst-group accuracy on Waterbirds, CelebA, and MetaShift, respectively, outperforming the best group-label-free baselines by up to 7.7 percentage points.

</details>


### [76] [Let Confidence Change, Not the Prediction: Prediction-Preserving Repair for Post-hoc Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01072)
*Daehwan Kim,Haejun Chung,Ikbeom Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CORD，一种后置适配器，通过修复完整校准概率向量来精确保留原始top-1预测，不改变校准器本身，且无需额外调参，在多个数据集上实现零TPCR并降低ECE/NLL/Brier。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事后校准虽然能修正置信度，但多类校准器可能会改变top-1预测。精度只衡量这些改变对正确性的净效应，无法反映预测变化的频率。作者希望在校准的同时保留原始预测决策。

Method: CORD从原始输出和校准输出出发，确定分配给原始top-1类别的质量；校准的类别条件分布将剩余质量分配给其他类别，生成修复后的概率向量，其argmax恢复原始预测。在校准集上，CORD协调修复后质量，在可行时保留校准输出中原始预测的平均质量。该适配器不改变已拟合的校准器及其直接输出，不拟合额外的监督映射，也不需要用户或验证调参的超参数。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100和ImageNet-1K上，CORD按构造实现零TPCR，并在每个数据集上相对直接输出降低了平均ECE、NLL和Brier；在分布偏移和不同校准集大小下也保持配对的改进。

Conclusion: CORD将预测保留约束从校准器拟合中移除，将原始决策的精确恢复交由后续输出修复完成，实现了无损预测的校准。

Abstract: Post-hoc calibration corrects reported confidence, yet a multiclass calibrator can also change the associated top-1 prediction. Accuracy captures only the net effect of these changes on correctness, not how often predictions change; the Top-1 Prediction Change Rate (TPCR) instead measures this frequency. We propose Calibrator-Output Repair for Top-1 Decision Preservation (CORD), the first post-fit adapter to impose exact prediction preservation by repairing the full calibrated probability vector. From the original and calibrated outputs alone, CORD determines the mass assigned to the original top-1. The calibrated conditional distribution allocates the remaining mass over the other classes, yielding a repaired vector whose own argmax recovers the original prediction. On the calibration split, CORD coordinates the repaired masses to retain the calibrated outputs' mean mass on original predictions whenever attainable. The adapter alters neither the fitted calibrator nor its direct output, fits no additional supervised map, and requires no user- or validation-tuned hyperparameter. Across CIFAR-10/100 and ImageNet-1K, CORD attains zero TPCR by construction and lowers mean ECE, NLL, and Brier relative to the corresponding direct outputs in every dataset; paired gains persist under distribution shift and across calibration-set sizes. CORD thus removes the preservation constraint from calibrator fitting and assigns exact recovery of the original decision to subsequent output repair. Our code is available at https://github.com/labhai/ORCU.

</details>


### [77] [Modelpedia: A Catalog of Model Findings for the Meta-Science of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01090)
*Franciszek Bernat,Dawid Płudowski,Michał Jan Włodarczyk,Luca Longo,Jianlong Zhou,Andreas Holzinger,Riccardo Guidotti,Wojciech Samek,Przemysław Biecek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Modelpedia是一个自动化的、LLM辅助的框架，用于从已发表的论文中提取关于AI模型的研究发现，将其与相关的模型、数据集、方法和概念进行关联，并聚合成一个可搜索的公开目录。在ICLR 2024和2025论文上应用原型后，提取了超过一千条发现，并对社区如何研究模型进行了元分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于AI模型的科学知识产生速度远超社区组织整理的能力。每隔几个月就有新的基础模型重塑领域，数百篇论文、博客和技术报告记录了它们的行为或失败，但这些发现仍然分散且实际上无法检索，亟需一个系统化的整理框架。

Method: 提出Modelpedia——一个自动化的、LLM辅助框架，从已发表的论文中提取关于模型的发现，将提取的发现关联到其涉及的模型、数据集、方法和概念，并将结果聚合为一个可搜索的公共目录。在ICLR 2024和2025被接收的论文上应用该原型进行验证。

Result: 在ICLR 2024和2025论文上应用原型，成功提取了超过一千条关于模型的研究发现，并将目录本身作为研究对象，对社区如何研究模型进行了元分析。

Conclusion: 邀请社区探索、贡献和构建这个开放的目录，帮助建立模型发现作为AI元科学的共享基础。

Abstract: Scientific knowledge about AI models is produced faster than the community can organize it. Every few months a new foundation model reshapes the field and hundreds of papers, blogs, and technical reports document how each behaves or fails. Yet, these findings remain scattered and effectively unretrievable. To address this gap we present Modelpedia, an automated, LLM-assisted framework that extracts findings about models from published papers, links it to the model, dataset, method, and concept it concerns, and aggregates the result into a searchable public catalog. Applying the prototype to accepted ICLR 2024 and 2025 papers, we extract over a thousand findings and, treating the catalog itself as an object of study, run a meta-analysis of how the community investigates models. Now, we invite the community to explore, contribute to, and build on the open catalog, and to help establish model findings as a shared foundation for the meta-science of AI.

</details>


### [78] [Subliminal Learning as Trait-Direction Drift: A Mechanism and Targeted Control under SFT Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01091)
*Zhixuan Liu,Zhichen Dong,Yuyu Fan,Xiangtian Li,Chao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了"特质方向漂移"（trait-direction drift）作为模型蒸馏中隐性学习（subliminal learning）的机制，并提出探针空间走廊正则化（probe-space corridor regularization）作为针对性防御方法，能显著减少隐藏特质传递而几乎不损失主任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型蒸馏可以超越预期能力，将教师模型的隐藏特质（如由系统提示导致的偏见）传递给下游学生模型，形成"隐性学习"现象。然而，该信号如何在训练过程中累积并导致行为层面的迁移仍不清楚，使得针对性的缓解措施难以设计。本文旨在揭示这一过程的底层机制，并据此开发有效的防御方法。

Method: 本文提出并验证了特质方向漂移机制来解释隐性学习：有偏生成在教师数据中产生可测量的偏好差距（preference gaps），学生模型可识别的差距在监督微调过程中诱导沿特质方向的更新，累积后形成行为迁移。基于该机制，作者提出探针空间走廊正则化，通过在蒸馏过程中沿校准的特质方向约束漂移来阻断隐性特质传递。

Result: 该方法在保持主任务性能的同时大幅降低隐藏特质传递：例如，恶意回复迁移率从29.55%降至6.45%，且主任务精度损失很低；在主Qwen设置中，也持续抑制了动物偏好的传递。偏好差距、训练轨迹和干预实验三类证据共同支持了特质方向漂移机制。

Conclusion: 偏好差距、训练轨迹和干预证据共同将隐性学习与特质方向漂移联系起来，并证明走廊正则化可作为蒸馏过程中的一个针对性控制手段，既揭示了下游模型继承教师隐藏偏见的机理，也提供了实用的缓解方案。

Abstract: Beyond intended capabilities, model distillation can transfer hidden traits from a teacher. A teacher biased by a system prompt can generate semantically clean training data, such as numeric sequences, that still causes a downstream student to inherit the hidden preference, a phenomenon known as subliminal learning. Prior work has identified several parts of this process. How the signal builds up during training and produces behavioral transfer remains unclear, making targeted mitigation difficult. We propose and validate trait-direction drift as a mechanism for subliminal learning: biased generation creates measurable preference gaps in teacher data, and student-recognizable gaps induce trait-aligned updates during supervised fine-tuning that accumulate into behavioral transfer. Guided by this mechanism, we propose probe-space corridor regularization, a targeted defense that constrains drift along a calibrated trait direction during distillation. The method substantially reduces hidden-trait transfer, preserving task performance: for example, it lowers malicious-response transfer from 29.55% to 6.45% with low main-task accuracy cost, and consistently suppresses animal-preference transfer across the main Qwen setting. The preference-gap, training-trajectory, and intervention evidence links subliminal learning to trait-direction drift and motivates corridor regularization as a targeted control during distillation.

</details>


### [79] [Neural Symbollic Regression Using Deep Learning and Sparse Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01102)
*Ravi Kumar U,Sumitra S*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号回归（NSR）框架，将神经网络作为符号发现的函数预处理器：神经网络首先在交互感知的非线性特征空间中学习目标函数的平滑、抗噪近似，随后使用LASSO提取稀疏、可解释的闭式表达式，并集成Ray Tune和ASHA进行分布式超参数优化。实验表明该方法在Nguyen基准上优于SINDy和非调优的神经基线，在RMSE、噪声鲁棒性和分布外泛化方面均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统符号回归方法面临两大瓶颈：遗传编程等传统方法可扩展性差且对噪声高度敏感，而SINDy等稀疏回归技术过度依赖预定义的特征库。为此需要一种既能学习复杂函数关系、又具备噪声鲁棒性和可扩展性的神经-符号混合方法。

Method: 采用解耦的两阶段流水线：(1) 神经网络在交互感知的非线性特征空间中学习目标函数的平滑、抗噪近似，作为符号发现的"功能预条件器"；(2) 对神经网络提取的中间表示应用LASSO，以获取稀疏、可解释的闭式表达式。同时引入基于Ray Tune和ASHA调度器的分布式超参数优化来提升预测精度与符号保真度。

Result: 在Nguyen基准套件上，该方法在RMSE、噪声鲁棒性和分布外泛化方面一致优于SINDy及未调优的神经基线。消融实验验证了特征交互、神经网络深度和调优策略对模型表现的重要性。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个可扩展且可理解的神经-符号框架，在神经近似与稀疏方程发现之间建立了坚实的桥梁，为科学机器学习中的符号发现提供了新范式。

Abstract: Symbolic Regression (SR) seeks to find succinct mathematical expressions that represent the fundamental relationships within data, providing interpretability and scientific understanding that exceeds that of black-box models. Nevertheless, traditional methods like Genetic Programming face challenges with scalability and are highly sensitive to noise, while sparse regression techniques such as SINDy rely significantly on predetermined feature libraries. In this work, we present a Neural Symbolic Regression (NSR) framework that treats neural networks as functional preconditioners for symbolic discovery. Our approach uses a decoupled pipeline: a neural network first learns a smooth, noise-robust approximation of the target function in an interaction- aware nonlinear feature space. LASSO is then applied to extract sparse, interpretable closed-form expressions. To improve predictive accuracy and symbolic fidelity by integrating distributed hyperparameter optimization with Ray Tune and ASHA scheduling. Experiments on the Nguyen benchmark suite show that our approach consistently outperforms SINDy and non-tuned neural baselines in RMSE, noise robustness, and out-of-distribution generalization. Ablation studies confirm the significance of feature interactions, neural depth, and tuning strategies. In general, this study presents a scalable and understandable neural-symbolic framework, creating a solid link between neural approximation and the discovery of sparse equations for scientific machine learning.

</details>


### [80] [Replicating TRACE: A Practitioner's Guide to Its Threshold and Particle Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01108)
*Alex Chadyuk,Alicia Zhang,Roy Kucukates*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文独立复现了TRACE方法在因果事件图抽取上的合成结果（词表1000时F1达0.90-0.91），但揭示了四方面局限：最优阈值并非固定常数而是钉在真值边距上；单一全局阈值主要恢复相邻(lag-1)直接因果图而对滞后边恢复极差；论文默认基准的滞后衰减造成的分布偏斜使头条F1仅认证lag-1恢复能力；F1随N=2即饱和是阈值边距性质而非估计器本性。最终提炼出五条实践规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TRACE论文声称可从预训练自回归模型中以固定阈值的条件互信息估计恢复事件类型的因果图，但其有效性边界与机理尚不清晰。本文旨在独立复现其头条结果，检验阈值选择、滞后结构、基准分布偏斜及粒子数影响，厘清方法真实能力与局限。

Method: 在合成基准上独立复现TRACE的逐位置条件互信息估计加阈值化流程；在验证集上选择阈值tau计算逐序列F1对照精确干预真值；系统变化词表大小(100-5000)、滞后衰减分布、粒子数N与真值边距delta，比较单阈值与逐滞后阈值族的表现，并做样本外验证与N^(-1/2)收敛性检验。

Result: 头条结果被复现（F1 0.90-0.91）；但最优阈值随真值边距delta与估计器校准决定，非固定常数；单全局阈值下lag-1召回0.97-0.99而lag≥2召回低数倍；逐滞后阈值族可恢复1/3到1/2的lag-2真值；默认基准85%干预真值集中于lag-1，头条F1仅认证lag-1恢复；F1从N=2即饱和而估计器按N^(-1/2)收敛。

Conclusion: TRACE实质上是直接相邻影响图抽取器而非完整因果图恢复器；其头条成绩部分源于基准分布偏斜。算法在绝对因果搜索上的能力边界需以展平的滞后分布、样本外阈值验证和原子干预对照来评估。据此作者给出五条面向实践者的使用规则。

Abstract: TRACE (Math & Lienhart, arXiv:2602.01135) reads causal graphs over event types out of a pretrained autoregressive sequence model by thresholding a per-position conditional-mutual-information estimate at a fixed tau. We independently replicate its headline synthetic result: with tau selected on a validation split, mean per-sequence F1 against exact interventional truth reaches 0.90-0.91 at vocabulary size 1000 (paper: 0.91) and 0.86-0.91 from 100 to 2000. First, the optimal threshold is pinned to the truth margin, not to any constant: at every size the errors at tau* straddle the delta = 0.05 margin defining ground truth (missed true edges lie just above it, accepted false ones just below), and the blind optimum lands near delta/2 times the estimator's calibration, confirmed out of sample at 5000. Second, at a single global threshold TRACE mostly recovers a direct, adjacent-influence graph: lag-1 true edges are recalled at 0.97-0.99, while true edges at lag 2 or more read orders of magnitude lower---the reading-scale price of randomizing mediating positions, which an exact test of direct causal effect requires when the truth is unknown. A per-lag threshold family recovers a third to a half of lag-2 truth; on lag-uniform data one validated threshold recalls every lag at 0.40-0.87, 8-26 pp below an atomic-intervention control at lags 3-6. Third, the default lag decay of the paper's synthetic benchmark concentrates about 85% of interventional truth at lag 1 and pushes the rest below the estimator's noise floor, so headline F1 there certifies lag-1 recovery only and conflates the benchmark's skew with the algorithm's own limit; a flatter decay separates the two. Fourth, F1 saturates from N = 2 particles at the selected threshold---a property of the threshold's margin over the noise floor, not of the estimator, which converges as N^(-1/2). We distill five practitioner rules.

</details>


### [81] [When Does Online Adaptation Pay on the Edge? A Leakage-Free Evaluation of Warmup, Learning-Rate Selection, and Resource Trade-offs for Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01126)
*Takumi Fujimoto,Hiroaki Nishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究边缘时间序列预测中的在线适应性在分布漂移场景下的真实收益，发现两个额外比较偏差来源——静态基线的热身预算（双向效应）以及优化器比较中共享学习率导致的混淆。通过在留出验证段上选取热身预算和优化器学习率的验证专用（validation-only）流程下，Adam在310/360个单元中胜过SGD+m，但4个Adam单元仍低于静态基线。研究还刻画了全参数、头部（head-only）与基于校准的适配方法在精度、状态内存与A100延迟方面的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线适应（online adaptation）有望帮助边缘时间序列预测应对分布漂移，但其衡量收益对评估选择高度敏感。已有研究忽略了热身预算和优化器选择等额外比较偏差来源，导致对适应方法真实收益的估计可能失真。

Method: 在六个公开多变量时间序列流（建筑传感器、智能电表数据）上运行无泄漏的流式协议；识别两个额外偏差来源：(1) 静态基线热身预算的双向效应（不足导致欠训练，过多损害漂移前泛化），跨度1,000-20,000步使估计收益变化3.0-18.8个百分点；(2) 以共享默认学习率比较SGD+m与Adam会混淆优化器质量和学习率敏感性。采用留出的漂移前验证切片（不接触测试数据）为热身预算和各优化器的在线学习率做选择，并在PatchTST前沿设置下评估精度、适配状态内存（full/head-only/calibration-based）与A100延迟。

Result: 验证专用流程下，Adam在310/360个评估单元中优于SGD+m，但仍有4个Adam单元低于静态基线。多个参数高效变体在适配状态内存轴上非支配（nondominated）。智能电表分析显示报告收益依赖于仪表选择规则。

Conclusion: 研究支持采用验证专用（validation-only）的部署流程来公平评估在线适应收益；目标设备的延迟与能耗有待进一步实测。代码、数据与完整结果已公开。

Abstract: Online adaptation can help edge time-series forecasting under distribution drift, but its measured benefit is sensitive to evaluation choices. We study six public multivariate streams, including building-sensor and smart-meter data, under a leakage-free streaming protocol. We identify two additional sources of comparison bias. First, the warmup budget of the static baseline has a two-sided effect: insufficient warmup undertrains the baseline, whereas excessive warmup can degrade its pre-drift generalization. Across six dataset-backbone settings, the estimated adaptation benefit changes by 3.0 to 18.8 percentage points (pp) over the 1,000-20,000-step warmup range. Second, comparing SGD with momentum (SGD+m) and Adam at a shared default learning rate conflates optimizer quality with rate sensitivity. We select both the warmup budget and each optimizer's online rate using a held-out pre-drift validation slice without accessing test data. Under this validation-only procedure, Adam outperforms SGD+m in 310 of 360 evaluated cells, while 4 Adam cells remain below the static baseline. We further characterize accuracy against adaptation-state memory and A100-measured per-update latency for full, head-only, and calibration-based adaptation. In the evaluated PatchTST frontier settings, several parameter-efficient variants are nondominated on the adaptation-state-memory axis. Smart-meter analyses also show that reported gains depend on meter-selection rules. These findings support a validation-only commissioning procedure, while target-device latency and energy remain to be measured. Code, data, and all reported numbers: https://github.com/keiotakmin/tsf-edge-adaptation.

</details>


### [82] [Scaled Idempotence in Transformer Attention: Paired OV Geometry and Shared-Value Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01129)
*Jiming Feng,Junliang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Transformer注意力中存在一种递归代数规律：有效的OV算子T=OV^⊤的子集近乎在复合下封闭，满足T²≈αT（近似幂等）。在2.8B–235B参数的六个预训练端点上，3.98–8.00%的注意力头达到平方闭合对齐P≥0.9；精确主坐标分解T=Q_OKQ_V^⊤和T²=Q_O(KDK)Q_V^⊤可分离支持内传输与读写返回几何。通过打乱K的方向（保留奇异值、范数、因子张成空间和主角度），7,304个头的中位闭合度从0.336降至1.04×10⁻⁴，证明训练方向对98.64%的头在每层都更优。构造性搜索显示每层都有高闭合可行但通常未达到。在精确值共享下，逐头闭合扩展为右作用代数T_iT_j=α_jT_i。总之，缩放幂等是稀疏训练方向，值共享将逐头关系扩展为局部算子代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer注意力头算子的代数结构，特别是OV算子闭合成近似幂等这一反复出现的规律，并探究其是否反映了训练获得的方向性质还是内在的几何可用性。

Method: 在大规模预训练模型（2.8B–235B，9个MHA/GQA模型，7,304个注意力头）上进行系统实证分析；提出精确主坐标分解方法将矩阵分解为T=Q_OKQ_V^⊤与T²=Q_O(KDK)Q_V^⊤两部分；设计对照组实验（打乱K方向、构造性搜索）区分训练方向与可用容量；在精确值共享设置下分析右作用代数结构。

Result: ①3.98–8.00%的头达到平方闭合对齐P≥0.9，且无匹配的层内O/V错位能达到该水平；②打乱K方向将中位闭合从0.336降至1.04×10⁻⁴，训练方向在98.64%的头且每层均获胜；③高闭合在每个层都几何可行但通常未被训练达到；④在值共享下T_iT_j=α_jT_i近似成立，且揭示了具有共享值定义核的斜投影结构。

Conclusion: 缩放幂等（scaled idempotence）是Transformer注意力中一种稀疏的训练方向特征，而非普遍的几何容量；值共享机制将逐头的幂等关系扩展为局部算子代数，这一规律在不同模型规模与架构中广泛存在。

Abstract: We identify a recurrent algebraic regularity in Transformer attention: a sparse subset of effective OV operators $T=OV^\top$ nearly closes under composition, $T^2\approxαT$. Across six pretrained endpoints spanning 2.8B--235B parameters, 3.98--8.00% of heads reach squared closure alignment $\mathcal{P}\geq0.9$, while no matched within-layer O/V mismatch does. An exact principal-coordinate factorization, $T=Q_OKQ_V^\top$ and $T^2=Q_O(KDK)Q_V^\top$, separates within-support transport from read--write return geometry. Across all 7,304 heads in nine MHA/GQA models, scrambling only the orientation of $K$ while preserving singular values, norms, factor spans, and principal angles reduces median closure from 0.336 to $1.04\times10^{-4}$; trained orientation wins for 98.64% of heads and in every layer. Constructive searches show that high closure is feasible in every surveyed layer, but usually not attained. Retrospective trajectories in three independently trained lineages further separate broadly available capacity from the orientations attained by final strong heads. Under exact value sharing, headwise closure extends to a right-action algebra, $T_iT_j=α_jT_i$. Seven-model experiments verify the approximate law and reveal distinct oblique projections with a shared value-defined kernel. These results characterize scaled idempotence as a sparse trained orientation within broadly available geometric capacity and show how value sharing extends a headwise relation into a local operator algebra.

</details>


### [83] [Superposed Latent Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01158)
*Quanling Zhao,Jiaying Yang,Tianqi Zhang,Ziyang Hao,Fatemeh Asgarinejad,Flavio Ponzina,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Superposed Latent Autoencoder (SLAE)，挑战传统自编码器"存储预算紧时需压缩每个潜在表示"的范式，改为通过学习的叠加方式让多个更宽的潜在表示共享存储空间，在相同存储预算下显著提升重建-存储权衡并改善下游分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自编码器在记忆预算受限时通过缩减每个潜在表示的维度来满足要求，但这会带来不可逆的维度瓶颈并牺牲表示容量。作者提出不同的问题：能否将多个更宽的潜在表示一起存储（叠加）而非缩小单个表示？

Method: SLAE包含四个核心步骤：(1) 将潜在表示变换为便于存储的编码；(2) 用随机化密钥绑定各编码；(3) 将多个编码叠加为单一记忆张量；(4) 通过解纠缠学习在解码前恢复各自的潜在表示。与不可逆的维度瓶颈不同，SLAE利用可被抑制的结构化干扰来实现共享存储。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100、SVHN、STL-10、Tiny ImageNet等多个数据集及广泛的存储预算设置下，SLAE在相同存储条件下将重建误差最多降低56%。此外，在相同存储预算下，SLAE保留的信息使下游分类准确率最多提升16.79个百分点。消融分析证实其优势源于使更宽的表示在相同存储预算下可用。

Conclusion: 该工作提出表示压缩的新原则：不应一味追求让每个潜在表示更小，而应保持表示的宽度并让其通过叠加共享存储。这一原则在重建任务和下游任务中均表现优越，为自编码器的潜在压缩提供了新的设计思路。

Abstract: Autoencoders typically meet tight latent-memory budgets by making each latent representation smaller, sacrificing representational capacity. We ask a different question: can multiple wider latents be stored together instead? We introduce the Superposed Latent Autoencoder (SLAE), which preserves high-capacity latent representations while sharing storage through learned superposition. SLAE transforms latents into storage-friendly codes, binds them with randomized keys, superposes multiple codes into a single memory tensor, and learns to recover each latent before decoding. Under the same storage budget, SLAE replaces irreversible dimensional bottlenecks with structured interference that can be suppressed. Across CIFAR-10/100, SVHN, STL-10, Tiny ImageNet, and a wide range of memory budgets, SLAE substantially improves the reconstruction--memory tradeoff, reducing reconstruction error by up to 56% over conventional autoencoders at matched storage. Further analysis shows that SLAE's advantage comes from making wider representations usable under the same storage budget. These gains also extend beyond reconstruction: the information preserved by SLAE improves downstream classification by up to 16.79 percentage points under the same memory budget. Our results suggest a new principle for representation compression: instead of making every latent smaller, keep representations wide and let them share memory.

</details>


### [84] [CopyShield: A Cross-Level Benchmark of Copyright Defenses in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01161)
*Maryam Alshehyari,Dushyant Singh Chauhan,Samuele Poppi,Martin Takac,Salem Lahlou,Nils Lukas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CopyShield，一个受控基准测试，用于在统一协议下比较三种不同干预层级（输出层的对比解码、行为层的直接偏好优化、表征层的激活干预）的版权防御方法。在两个模型家族上评测后，发现干预层级与合规-效用权衡密切相关，且指出了针对非字面泄密的抑制仍是开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型能逐字复现记忆化的文本，但现有版权防御的评测常使用不兼容的协议，缺乏能在统一标准下横向比较不同干预层级方法的基准。

Method: 引入CopyShield受控基准，基于五本公有领域书籍构建受控记忆化场景，在LLaMA-3.1-8B和Mistral-7B-v0.3上评测三种代表性防御方法：对比解码（输出层级干预）、直接偏好优化（行为层级干预）、激活干预（表征层级干预），使用统一的评测协议度量字面泄漏、校准后的非字面泄漏、效用与退化（degeneracy）。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1-8B上：对比解码几乎无退化（0-2%）但字面抑制存在下限（NV-Recall 0.192-0.203）；DPO几乎完全消除字面泄漏（0.263降至0.002）但导致58%的QA输出出现释义循环退化且无效用增益；激活干预通过阻断84%的非字面查询达到了最低的非字面标记率（1/200）。人类评测确认DPO连贯性低，激活干预通过广泛拒答降低了感知版权风险。在Mistral-7B-v0.3上，输出与表征层级模式一致，但DPO退化降至10-14%，表明其严重程度依赖模型。

Conclusion: CopyShield提供了跨干预层级的参考基线，揭示了输出、行为、表征三种防御层级各自的合规-效用权衡特性，并指出了针对性的非字面泄漏抑制是仍需攻克的开放难题。

Abstract: Large language models can reproduce memorized text verbatim, yet copyright defenses are usually evaluated under incompatible protocols. We introduce CopyShield, a controlled benchmark comparing three representative defenses at distinct intervention levels: contrastive decoding (output), Direct Preference Optimization (behavioral), and activation intervention (representation). We evaluate CopyShield on two model families, LLaMA-3.1-8B and Mistral-7B-v0.3, using controlled memorization over five public-domain books and a shared protocol measuring literal leakage, calibrated non-literal leakage, utility, and degeneracy. Across these methods, intervention level is associated with distinct compliance-utility trade-offs. On LLaMA-3.1-8B, contrastive decoding remains near-degeneracy-free (0-2%) but reaches a literal-suppression floor at NV-Recall 0.192-0.203. DPO nearly eliminates literal leakage (0.263 to 0.002) but induces paraphrase-loop degeneracy in 58% of QA outputs, with no utility gain over the SFT baseline. Activation intervention attains the lowest non-literal flagging rate (1/200) by blocking 84% of non-literal queries before generation. Human evaluation confirms that DPO has low coherence, whereas activation lowers perceived copyright risk through broad refusal. On Mistral-7B-v0.3, the output- and representation-level patterns persist, while DPO degeneracy falls to 10-14%, showing that its severity is model-dependent. Together, CopyShield provides cross-level reference baselines and identifies targeted non-literal suppression as an open challenge. The code is available at https://github.com/spotai-mbzuai/CopyShield.git.

</details>


### [85] [Pre-carved Niches: The Formation Dynamics of Modular Task Partitions in Early LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01170)
*Guangqi Li,Yongxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文逐步追踪Pythia-410M模型从零训练全过程，发现模块化组织布局在训练前就已"预雕刻"（任务对重叠为基线的~3.6倍），随后通过两次尖锐跳跃锁定分区（第二次达20.4 sigma），该过程伴随梯度层面的相对剥夺现象（胜者梯度供给为败者2.25-2.73倍），但未传导至更新与权重层面。偏离基底子层仅出现在正在学习的领域中，支持模块化追踪学习的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型展现出与人类大脑功能网络相似的模块化内部组织，但先前研究只刻画了训练完成的模型，对训练过程中这种组织如何形成一无所知。作者旨在填补这一空白，逐步揭示模块化结构的形成机制。

Method: 从零训练Pythia-410M模型（两条轨迹：bf16和fp32），在每个训练步骤执行归因修补（attribution patching），结合梯度范数、有效更新、权重范数以及一阶损失分解探测，跨14个任务、4个认知领域进行分析。同时采用噪音控制基线与宁静窗口（quiet-window）等统计方法量化显著性，并预注册尺度阈值假设以支撑后续2.8B实验。

Result: 三个核心发现：(1) 模块化地图在训练前已预雕刻——主导任务对在归因基底上重叠为任务无关基线的~3.6倍，且其layer-0浓度是该模型家族的结构级常数；(2) 分区通过两次与学习率调度无关的尖锐跳跃锁定（第二次达20.4 sigma宁静窗口/6.2 sigma全局），伴随梯度级相对剥夺现象（2.25->2.73倍，低于随机对照9.5-11.5个标准差），但该现象不传播至更新或权重；(3) 对基底子层的偏离仅出现在正在学习的领域中。

Conclusion: 模块化组织在模型学习前已由架构预置（基底存在），学习过程仅通过离散跳跃锁定既有分区，梯度层级的相对剥夺现象提示资源竞争机制，但其不传播至权重暗示存在更深层的保护机制。作者将可辩护的特征级解释与无法回答的机制性问题加以区分，并预注册了2.8B规模实验的尺度阈值假设。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit a modular internal organization that mirrors well-studied functional networks of the human brain, but how this organization forms during training is unknown: prior work has characterized finished models, not the formation process. We track formation step by step: we train a Pythia-410M model from scratch (two trajectories, bf16 and fp32) and run attribution patching at every step, alongside probes for gradient norms, effective updates, weight norms, and first-order loss decomposition across 14 tasks in four cognitive domains. Three findings. First, the modular map is pre-carved: before any learning, the dominant task pair already overlaps at ~3.6x the attribution substrate (a task-independent baseline), and its layer-0 concentration is an architecture-level constant on this model family. Second, the partition locks in through two sharp jumps whose amplitudes do not track the learning-rate schedule (the second reaching 20.4 sigma quiet-window / 6.2 sigma global), accompanied by gradient-level relative deprivation--winners receive 2.25->2.73x the loser's gradient supply, 9.5-11.5 standard deviations below a random control--that does not propagate to updates or weights. Third, deviation from the substrate appears only in the domain being learned, consistent with the hypothesis that modularity tracks learning. We close by separating the feature-level account we can defend from the mechanistic questions we cannot, and we pre-register the scale-threshold hypothesis behind our ongoing 2.8B experiments.

</details>


### [86] [Births are difficult to predict even with rich survey and full-population register data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01194)
*Elizaveta Sivak,Emily M. Cantrell,Thomas Emery,Javier Garcia-Bernardo,Flavio Hafner,Kasia Karpinska,Malte Lüken,Adrienne Mendrik,Joris Mulder,Hanzhang Ren,Varun Satish,Mark Verhagen,Angelica M.
