Perdomo,Tanzir Pial,Alessio Piraccini,Federica Querin,Ziwei Rao,Christian Rellama,Adrien Remund,Frederieke Richert,Arnout van de Rijt,Mojtaba Rostami Kandroodi,Stijn J. Rotman,Lucas Sage,Germans Savcisens,Katrin Schwanitz,Steven Skiena,Alessandro Spata,Yannick Stadtfeld,Benedikt Stroebl,Gaetano Tedesco,Mathilde Theelen,Gianluca Tori,Abigail Tun-Mendicuti,Rishabh Tyagi,Keyon Vafa,Luiz Felipe Vecchietti,Linda Vecgaile,Willem R. J. Vermeulen,Maria-Pia Victoria Feser,Lionel A. Voirol,Thom B. Volker,Xinran Wang,Jiani Yan,Xinyi Zhao,Flora Zhou,Zuzana Zilincikova,Malvina Nissim,Matthew J. Salganik,Gert Stulp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过一个大规模数据挑战，让147名研究者利用调查数据和全人口登记数据预测荷兰18-45岁居民三年内是否生育子女。结果显示预测准确度中等（最佳F1：登记数据0.59，调查数据0.76），先进模型未超越传统方法，更大规模的登记数据也未优于调查数据。通过模拟受孕与妊娠的随机生物学过程，作者估计了预测上限（调查数据F1约0.86-0.94，登记数据0.88-0.96），表明观察到的性能差距源于数据、方法的不足及未建模的随机性，而生育本身的随机性就已对个体生命可预测性设定非平凡上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在探讨重大人生事件难以预测的根本原因——究竟是理论、数据和算法的局限，还是随机性（机会）在人类生活中的主导作用。生育作为可验证的重大人生事件，提供了一个近乎理想的预测实验环境。

Method: 研究采用数据挑战的形式，邀请147名研究者使用调查数据与全人口登记数据预测荷兰18-45岁居民三年内生育结局。参赛方法涵盖从逻辑回归到大型语言模型和Transformer的多种模型。随后通过模拟受孕与妊娠的随机生物学过程，估算预测的理论上限，并将实际表现与上限进行对比归因。

Result: 最佳F1分数为登记数据0.59、调查数据0.76。先进模型并未优于经典模型，更大规模的登记数据也未超越样本调查数据。模拟估算的预测上限为调查数据F1约0.86-0.94、登记数据约0.88-0.96，实际表现显著低于该上限。

Conclusion: 生育预测的实际表现低于理论上限，差距来自数据不完美、方法不足以及未建模的随机因素；但即便数据和方法达到理想状态，受孕与妊娠本身的生物学随机性也会对个体生活预测设定一个不可忽视的限制上限。

Abstract: Major life events have proven difficult to predict. Does this reflect limits of theory, data, and algorithms, or the large role of chance? We examine one outcome - having a child within three years - through a near-ideal setting for prediction: a data challenge where 147 researchers predicted births for Dutch residents aged 18-45, using survey data and full-population registers. Methods ranged from logistic regression to a large language model and transformers. Predictions were moderately accurate (best F1: register 0.59, survey 0.76); advanced models did not outperform classical ones; and the larger registers did not beat the survey. Simulating the stochastic biology of conception and pregnancy, we estimated a predictive ceiling (survey F1 ~ 0.86-0.94, register 0.88-0.96). Observed performance falls short of this ceiling, implicating imperfect data, methods, and unmodelled chance, while the ceiling itself shows that chance in reproduction alone sets a non-trivial limit on predicting individual lives.

</details>


### [87] [Recent Developments in Transformer Inference Deployment on FPGA Platforms: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01212)
*Arjan Blankestijn,Uraz Odyurt,Amirreza Yousefzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对Transformer架构的机器学习模型在FPGA平台上进行推理部署的最新进展进行了系统性文献综述，探讨了FPGA作为CPU和GPU替代加速器的可行性，并总结了实现与优化的关键技术、趋势和设计选择，提供了分类体系以指导学术界和工业界的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于Transformer架构的机器学习模型持续快速增长并广泛融入实际应用，迫切需要高效部署方案。这里的"高效部署"既涵盖吞吐量、延迟等运行性能，也包括能耗等效率指标。在推理任务中，专用硬件加速器相比CPU和GPU是更具吸引力的选择，而FPGA凭借实现灵活性、能效、低延迟及适合现场部署等优势成为重要替代方案。因此有必要系统梳理该领域的进展与技术选择。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述（Systematic Literature Review）方法，提取并深入分析Transformer推理在FPGA平台上的实现与优化偏好技术，构建涵盖相关主题的分类体系（taxonomy），系统性地考察设计选择、最新进展与趋势。

Result: 识别出Transformer推理在FPGA平台上的最新进展、趋势和关键设计选择，提取出实现与优化所偏好的具体技术，并形成了系统性的主题分类框架，可作为学术界与工业界研究者的指南。

Conclusion: 该综述及其提供的主题分类体系能够有效指导学术界和工业界研究者在FPGA平台上进行Transformer推理的部署与优化，填补了该领域系统性知识整理方面的空白。

Abstract: With the rapid and continuous growth in the incorporation of machine learning models based on the Transformer architecture, capable deployment is in high demand. In this context, capable deployment refers to operational performance aspects, e.g., throughput and latency, as well as efficiency aspects, e.g., energy consumption. When it comes to the task of inference using such models, purpose-built hardware accelerators provide a lucrative alternative to common deployment choices, such as Central Processing Units (CPUs) and Graphics Processing Units (GPUs). The Field Programmable Gate Array (FPGA) platforms category is an example of such alternative accelerators, promising implementation flexibility, energy efficiency, improved latency and suitability for on-site deployment. We investigate the most recent advances, trends, and design choices for Transformer inference on FPGA platforms. We perform a systematic literature review, extracting and delving into preferred techniques for implementation and optimisation. This study and the provided taxonomy of topics could act as a guide for researchers from the academia and industry alike.

</details>


### [88] [REFACTOR-VLA: Unsupervised Library Learning of Typed Motor Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01215)
*Riyaaz Shaik,Chandru Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 REFACTOR-VLA，一种"唤醒/睡眠"（wake/sleep）两阶段学习系统，用于为视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型学习可复用的行为技能抽象。睡眠阶段利用学习到的潜在世界模型计算"行为等价核"（BEK）并聚类运动程序片段；唤醒阶段生成基于 Hindley-Milner 词汇的带类型 lambda 项，供库条件化的整流流动作解码器使用。在 LIBERO 基准上发现世界模型容量增大反而使性能下降，但加入辅助的 InfoNCE 对比损失显著提升睡眠阶段聚类质量，在全部 4 套任务上超过最强的已发表基线（平均 NMI 提升 +0.184）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLA 模型（如 OpenVLA、π₀、RT-2、RDT-1B）都是单体式架构，直接输出原始电机指令或短动作片段，缺乏将行为组织为可复用抽象的能力，导致长程任务上性能退化且难以解释。已有的技能发现方法回避了一个核心问题——何时两个动作序列在行为上是等价的，它们要么对对比嵌入做聚类，要么委托给与机器人动力学未校准的语言模型进行判断。

Method: REFACTOR-VLA 采用 wake/sleep 框架：睡眠阶段通过学习到的潜在世界模型 Mφ 计算"行为等价核"（BEK），据此对运动程序片段进行聚类；唤醒阶段在 Hindley-Milner 启发的词汇上发射带类型的 lambda 项，由库条件化的整流流动作解码器消费。抽象只有通过最小描述长度（MDL）和返回保持（return-preservation）门槛才被接纳。此外，在世界模型预热期间加入辅助的有监督 InfoNCE 对比损失以提升睡眠阶段的聚类质量。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准上报告两个主要发现：(1) 将世界模型从 188M 扩到 430M 参数反而使 4/4 套任务性能变差，表明单纯容量增加无益；(2) 训练目标远比模型容量重要——加入 InfoNCE 辅助损失后，睡眠阶段聚类的 NMI 在 n=3 种子下达到：object 0.462±0.021、spatial 0.867±0.025、goal 0.915±0.013、LIBERO-10 0.754±0.010，在全部 4 套任务上以平均 Δ=+0.184 超过最强已发布基线。n=12 提供者下成对 NMI 的 95% 自助法置信区间为 [0.683, 0.729]（均值 0.705）。睡眠阶段还产生了首个真实 LIBERO 任务语言库：解码器使用了 3 个被接纳抽象中的 2 个，并重写了全部 256 条采样的演示。

Conclusion: REFACTOR-VLA 表明，对于 VLA 技能发现而言，训练目标的设计比模型容量增加更关键；通过世界模型上的行为等价核聚合并辅以对比学习，可以有效学习可复用的技能抽象，产生真实的任务语言库，并在聚类质量上显著超越既有基线。这为可解释、可复用的长程机器人行为学习提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Most vision-language-action (VLA) models -- OpenVLA, $π_0$, RT-2, RDT-1B -- are monolithic: they emit raw motor commands or short action chunks without organizing behavior into reusable abstractions, so they degrade on long-horizon tasks and resist interpretation. Existing skill-discovery methods sidestep the core question of when two action sequences are behaviorally equivalent, either clustering contrastive embeddings or delegating the judgment to a language model uncalibrated to the robot's dynamics. We introduce REFACTOR-VLA, a wake/sleep system for learning reusable skills. Its sleep phase clusters motor-program fragments under a Behavioral-Equivalence Kernel (BEK) computed from rollouts of a learned latent world model $M_φ$; its wake phase emits typed lambda terms over a Hindley--Milner-inspired vocabulary, consumed by a library-conditioned rectified-flow action decoder. Abstractions are admitted only if they pass Minimum Description Length and return-preservation gates. On LIBERO we report two findings. First, enlarging the world model from 188M to 430M parameters worsened performance on 4 of 4 suites, so capacity alone does not help. Second, the training objective matters far more: adding an auxiliary supervised contrastive (InfoNCE) loss during world-model warmup substantially improves sleep-phase clustering, giving Normalized Mutual Information at $n=3$ seeds of $0.462 \pm 0.021$ (object), $0.867 \pm 0.025$ (spatial), $0.915 \pm 0.013$ (goal) and $0.754 \pm 0.010$ (LIBERO-10), and beating the strongest published baseline on all 4 suites by a mean $Δ= +0.184$. Across providers ($n=12$) the 95% bootstrap confidence interval for mean pairwise NMI is $[0.683, 0.729]$ (mean $0.705$). The sleep phase also yields the first real-LIBERO task-language library: the decoder uses 2 of 3 admitted abstractions and rewrites all 256 sampled demonstrations.

</details>


### [89] [Post-Training Science for Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01244)
*Charles O'Neill,Mudith Jayasekara,Harry Partridge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对监督微调（SFT）中的关键超参数和决策进行了系统化、大规模的实证研究，涵盖稠密和混合专家（MoE）模型（Qwen3和Llama两个家族），在四个真实客户SFT数据集上对LoRA和全参数微调逐一控制变量进行扫描，测量学习率、批次大小、LoRA秩与alpha、训练轮数、优化器等决策的最优选择及其迁移性，并给出每个建议的置信度度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每次监督微调运行都面临相同的一系列决策（学习率、批次大小、LoRA或全参微调、轮数、优化器、数据等），但这些决策通常针对每个新模型和新数据集从头重新摸索，缺乏一个统一的、可衡量的经验指南来指导这些选择。

Method: 在统一实验框架下进行单因素控制变量的扫描（一次只改变一个杠杆），覆盖两个模型家族（Qwen3和Llama）、稠密与MoE架构、四组真实客户SFT数据集，同时对比LoRA与全参数微调。数据集具有内部一致的监督目标和配套的任务评估器。还通过验证损失、损失景观平坦度等指标检验其对下游质量的排序能力，并将模型阶梯扩展到235B参数的MoE模型。

Result: 文章得出了关于学习率和批次大小如何随模型规模、家族和数据变化以及最佳选择规则是否可迁移的结论；量化了LoRA相对全参数微调的权衡，以及秩和alpha对适配器学习能力的影响；检验了验证损失等指标对下游质量的排序可信度；探究了后训练收益随模型大小和数据量的缩放规律；确定了通用指令跟随能力开始退化前可训练的轮数；并评估了几何感知优化器是否优于AdamW。每项建议均附带不确定性度量。

Conclusion: 通过将所有SFT决策放在同一测量仪器下进行系统比较，本文为实践中常见的微调超参数选择提供了数据驱动的、附带不确定性估计的经验性指导，弥补了以往依赖单点经验或缺乏统一衡量基准的空白。

Abstract: Every supervised fine-tuning run forces the same chain of decisions, such as learning rate, batch size, LoRA or full fine-tuning, how many epochs, which optimiser, and what data to feed the model. Each of these is typically rediscovered from scratch for every new model and dataset. Here we measure them under one instrument: a sweep that varies one lever at a time, and spans dense and mixture-of-experts models in two families (Qwen3 and Llama), on four real-world customer SFT datasets, for both LoRA and full fine-tuning. These datasets give a controlled testbed: each task carries an evaluation built with the customer, and its training data is produced by iterative supervised fine-tuning that refines model outputs until they pass that evaluation, so the supervised target is internally consistent and the task judge we report against is the criterion the data was built to satisfy. We ask how the optimal learning rate and batch size move with model scale, family, and data, and whether one selection rule transfers across them; what LoRA trades against full fine-tuning, and how its rank and alpha set what the adapter can learn; whether validation loss (or other metrics, such as loss landscape flatness) faithfully ranks downstream quality; whether post-training gains scale with model size and data volume, on a model ladder extended through mixtures-of-experts to 235B parameters; how many epochs to train before general instruction-following erodes; and whether a geometry-aware optimiser improves on AdamW. Each recommendation is paired with a measure of its uncertainty.

</details>


### [90] [Explore More, Drift Less: Outcome-Only Reinforcement Learning Can Suffice for Long-Horizon Interactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01245)
*Liming Pu,Xiaoxia Li,Yifu Liu,Teng Cao,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出CANOPY（Coverage-ANchored On-PolicY RL）协议，直接解决小规模开源模型在仅靠端到端结果验证进行强化学习时性能受限的两个根本问题：信号饥饿（signal starvation）和策略漂移（policy drift）。通过扩展同任务探索直至自然奖励信号重现、保持所有更新为on-policy且KL锚定、并限制在智能体自身的动作token上，CANOPY在仅通过环境交互训练的Qwen3-14B策略上登顶AppWorld公开榜（Test-Normal TGC 86.9），并将Qwen3.5-9B在SWE-bench Verified上提升16.6个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者反驳了"结果奖励RL对小模型很快触顶"的普遍信念，指出该天花板实为两种常见实践缺陷的产物：一是组相对（group-relative）RL在稀疏结果奖励下，只有当任务回滚组同时包含成功与失败样本时才产生梯度，探索不足会导致最困难且最有信息量的任务被完全静默（信号饥饿）；二是从小任务池中挤出过多更新会使未锚定的目标函数导致采样分布坍缩，恰好在饱和度已使信息性组变得罕见时恶化策略本身（策略漂移）。因此，现有工作往往诉诸密集奖励、SFT先验、技能库等复杂补偿手段。

Method: CANOPY采用极简协议：第一，将同任务探索规模放大直到自然（稀疏结果）信号重现，克服信号饥饿；第二，保持每次更新严格on-policy、KL锚定，并仅作用于智能体自身的动作token，防止策略漂移导致的分布坍缩；第三，在测试时利用更大的交互预算兑现训练中扩展探索所带来的能力增益。该方法无需任务特定监督、辅助信用信号或精细的智能体脚手架。

Result: 在AppWorld长程交互式编码基准上，仅通过环境交互训练的Qwen3-14B策略登顶公开排行榜（2026年2月数据；Test-Normal TGC 86.9，Test-Challenge 67.6）。相同的设计原则使Qwen3.5-9B在SWE-bench Verified上提升16.6个百分点，表明纯粹的智能体RL即可将长程能力内化到小型开源模型中。

Conclusion: 作者论证结果奖励RL对小模型的性能瓶颈是可消除的人为产物，而非固有局限。CANOPY证明无需外部密集信号或复杂架构，精心设计的RL训练协议本身就能让小型开源模型获得与大型模型竞争的智能体长程交互能力，并计划开源完整训练栈。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is a natural way to post-train LLM agents for long-horizon interactive tasks judged only by end-of-task verification, yet a shared belief holds that outcome-only RL soon hits a ceiling on small open models. Recent work therefore compensates around the training with denser rewards, SFT priors, skill libraries, curated memory, or multi-agent orchestration. We argue the ceiling is an artifact of two failures of common practice. Signal starvation: group-relative RL with sparse outcome-only rewards yields a gradient only when a task's rollout group mixes successes and failures, so under-scaled exploration silences exactly the hardest, most instructive tasks. Policy drift: squeezing many updates out of a small task pool degrades the policy itself, as an unanchored objective lets the sampling distribution collapse exactly when saturation has already made informative groups rare. We present CANOPY (Coverage-ANchored On-PolicY RL), a minimalist protocol attacking both directly: scale same-task exploration until the natural signal reappears, keep every update on-policy, KL-anchored, and confined to the agent's own action tokens, then cash in an enlarged interaction budget at test time. On AppWorld, a long-horizon interactive coding benchmark, a Qwen3-14B policy trained with CANOPY through environment interaction alone--without task-specific supervision, auxiliary credit signals, or elaborate agent scaffolding--topped the public leaderboard (Feb. 2026; Test-Normal TGC 86.9, Test-Challenge 67.6), and the same design principles lift Qwen3.5-9B on SWE-bench Verified by 16.6 points. Agentic RL alone thus internalizes long-horizon capability directly into a small open model; we plan to release the complete training stack at https://github.com/AlibabaResearch/SignalCoverageRL.

</details>


### [91] [Solving In-Table Prediction Problems by Deep Neural Networks with Performance Evaluation Using Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01262)
*Xiao Zhao,Daniela Oelke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种称为In-Table Prediction (ITP)的新问题，即利用深度神经网络基于表格中已知列的值来预测任意选定列的值，采用自监督学习方式（随机掩码部分列作为学习目标），并针对仅含连续特征的表格数据，提出一种可同时嵌入数值与缺失值的新颖神经层。在合成数据上比较MLP、ResNet和Transformer三种架构后发现，在训练样本充足且嵌入长度较大的条件下，注意力结构表现最优；但作者强调这是一项在受控合成条件下的小规模初步研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统表格深度学习（TDL）依赖监督学习范式，需要显式给定目标特征。作者探索不同的思路：使用深度神经网络学习表中各列之间的相互关系，即让NN基于已知列预测任意选定列的值。这一问题与表格缺失值填充方法以及TDL的预训练任务略有不同，且作者认为现有的三个潜在应用场景尚未被广泛研究。

Method: 采用自监督学习方法，随机选择列进行掩码并作为学习目标。聚焦于仅含连续特征的表格数据，提出一种新型神经层来同时嵌入数值和空值。基于预定义的列关系生成合成数据，并通过两种不同机制插入空值；同时采用自适应的掩码策略构造测试数据。在合成数据上分别评估MLP、ResNet和Transformer三种NN架构的性能。

Result: 实验结果表明，当训练样本数量足够大且选择较大的嵌入长度时，基于注意力（Transformer）的结构优于MLP和ResNet两种网络。

Conclusion: 这些发现是在受控的合成条件下、列数较少的情况下获得的，因此应被视为一项初步的、范围狭窄的研究，而非对ITP在真实世界表格数据上的通用性结论。作者对此局限进行了明确说明。

Abstract: Tabular deep learning (TDL) leverages neural networks (NN) to extract patterns from tabular data. Traditional TDL methods follow a supervised learning paradigm, where a target feature is explicitly given. In this work, however, we explore a different approach by employing deep NNs to learn relationships among individual columns within a given table. We investigate whether NNs can predict the values of arbitrarily selected columns in a given table based on the remaining known columns. We call this problem In-Table Prediction (ITB), which is slightly different from table imputation methods and the pretraining task of TDL. Three potential usage scenarios are identified, which, to our best knowledge, have not been extensively studied in the literature. A self-supervised learning approach is applied to address this problem by randomly selecting columns to be masked out and used as learning targets. This work focuses on tabular datasets containing only continuous features. To handle missing values in continuous features, a novel neural layer is proposed to embed both numerical and empty values. Synthetic data is generated based on predefined column relationships, with empty values inserted using two distinct mechanisms. Additionally, an adapted masking strategy is employed to create test data. Performances of three NN architectures, namely MLP, Resnet and Transformer, are evaluated using the generated synthetic data. We conclude that, the attention-based structure outperforms the other two networks, when a sufficiently large number of training examples is available and a relatively large embedding length is chosen. We stress that these findings are obtained under controlled, synthetic conditions with a small number of columns and it should therefore be regarded as an initial, narrowly-scoped investigation rather than a general characterization of ITP on real-world tabular data.

</details>


### [92] [Position: Privacy Is a Claim, Not a Property of Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01273)
*Jiachen Zhao,Antonia Januszewicz,Taeho Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为一篇立场论文，指出合成数据在隐私敏感场景中的使用已从"基于假设的残余推断风险"悄然转变为"基于数据生成过程的外观属性"这一隐性质量标准。作者通过实证分析指出，机器学习顶会论文频繁在隐私敏感场景中使用合成数据，却未明确威胁模型、推断风险或可证伪的隐私主张，导致隐私保障隐式化、难以验证且分布不均（少数群体记录风险更高）。作者主张将隐私视为明确、可循证的科学主张，并建议ML顶会采纳规范，要求隐私相关断言需明确范围、可检验、可争议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据虽被广泛采用，但其在隐私敏感场景中的使用标准已发生了隐性转变——从严格的推断风险论证退化为基于生成过程本身的表面属性。作者认为这反映了社区对"何为充分隐私证据"标准的隐性变化，而非对隐私原则的误解，因此有必要指出这一问题并推动社区标准改革。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）方法，结合对近期各大机器学习顶级会议出版物的实证分析，考察合成数据在隐私敏感场景中的使用方式，重点检查是否存在明确的威胁模型阐述、推断风险量化及可证伪的隐私声明。

Result: 实证分析显示，合成数据在隐私敏感场景中经常被使用，但论文普遍缺乏对威胁模型、推断风险或可证伪隐私主张的明确阐述。隐私保证往往保持隐式、难以验证且分布不均，少数群体和稀有记录面临更高的隐私暴露风险。

Conclusion: 作者建议将隐私对待为明确、基于证据的科学主张，并呼吁机器学习顶会采纳相应规范，要求隐私相关断言必须明确界定范围（scoped）、具备可检验性（testable）和可争议性（contestable），从而保障隐私证据的质量与公平分配。

Abstract: Synthetic data has become a common component of machine learning research. While widely adopted, its use in privacy-sensitive contexts has quietly shifted from a claim of residual inference risk under stated assumptions to an appearance-based property inferred from data generation itself. In this position paper, we argue that this shift reflects an implicit change in community standards for what counts as sufficient privacy evidence, rather than a misunderstanding of well-established privacy principles. Drawing on an empirical analysis of recent publications across major ML venues, we show that synthetic data is frequently used in privacy-sensitive settings without explicit articulation of threat models, inference risks, or falsifiable privacy claims. As a result, privacy assurance often remains implicit, difficult to verify, and unevenly distributed, with heightened exposure for rare and minority records. We argue for treating privacy as an explicit, evidence-based scientific claim and recommend that ML venues adopt norms requiring privacy-relevant assertions to be clearly scoped, testable, and contestable.

</details>


### [93] [The Constitutional Coverage Trilemma in AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01275)
*Natalija Mitic,Soona Sedahmed A. O.,Mamadou Selly Ly,Moustapha Cisse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这篇论文研究了前沿AI系统作为“宪法性机构”的伦理价值供给与人类需求之间的匹配问题。通过对23个前沿LLM默认宪法与1649名美国参与者的成对权衡研究，发现人类需求广泛（覆盖安全、帮助性、诚实、自主性、公平五种价值），而AI供给狭窄且漂移（仅覆盖需求凸包的约2%），且漂移方向背离已覆盖不足的价值。作者提出稀疏的2列治国方案即可超越完整23种原型的前沿，并将发现形式化为“预算式多元主义三难困境”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI系统在部署时隐式编码了安全、帮助性、诚实、自主性和公平等价值之间的排序，相当于“宪法性机构”。作者核心动机是探究一个根本性的治理问题：这些AI系统所供给的价值取向类型（宪法类型）是否覆盖了人类对这些价值的需求？供给面是否与需求面匹配？

Method: 采用三部分实证方法：(1) 对23个前沿LLM原型的出厂默认宪法进行“释义对照审计”（paraphrase-controlled audit）；(2) 对1649名美国参与者在相同测量工具上进行成对权衡研究以刻画需求；(3) 结合噪声匹配估计与距离加权福利模型，进行凸包覆盖分析、跨家族版本趋势检验（排列检验）以及多治国菜单的遗憾值优化。

Result: 报告三个核心事实：(1) 需求广泛：五大价值观均有需求，最大单一群体占比不足三分之一；(2) 供给狭窄且漂移：23原型凸包仅占需求凸包约2%（全审计精度下0.10%），无任何原型将帮助性或自主性置于首位（37%用户“宪法性无家可归”）；跨6大家族中，自主性在5/6下降、公平在5/6上升、安全在4/6上升，版本趋势具有单调性（p=0.013）；(3) 修复稀疏：2列治国菜单优于完整23原型前沿47%的平均后悔率（CI 43%-52%），三顶点增补可分别降低平均/最差群体后悔率最高81%/64%。

Conclusion: 供应侧的漂移方向是背离——远离一个已经覆盖不足的价值维，机械性恶化了最小服务群体的福利底线。论文将这些发现形式化为“预算式多元主义三难困境”，证明其约束性状态在实证中成立，并验证结论对距离加权福利和降级路由（degraded routing）具有稳健性。这为前沿AI的价值多样性治理提供了量化证据与稀疏修复路径。

Abstract: Frontier AI systems function as \emph{constitutional institutions}: each deployed model encodes an implicit ranking among safety, helpfulness, honesty, autonomy, and equity. We ask whether the supply of frontier constitutional types covers human demand. Combining a paraphrase-controlled audit of the as-shipped default constitutions of $23$ frontier LLM archetypes with a pairwise-tradeoff study of $1{,}649$ US participants on the same instrument, we report three facts. \emph{Demand is broad}: it spans all five values, with the largest constituency under one-third. \emph{Supply is narrow and drifting}: the $23$-archetype hull occupies ${\sim}2\%$ of the demand hull under conservative noise-matched estimation ($0.10\%$ at full audit precision), no archetype puts helpfulness or autonomy first ($37\%$ of users are constitutionally homeless), and across six model families autonomy decreases in $5/6$, equity increases in $5/6$, and safety increases in $4/6$, with monotone within-family version trends (order-permutation $p = 0.013$) and the autonomy decline concentrated in scenarios where safety is not at stake. The drift's importance is directional: \emph{away} from a value already undercovered, mechanically worsening the welfare floor for the least-served users. \emph{The fix is sparse}: a $2$-vertex menu $\{e_{\mathrm{HON}}, e_{\mathrm{AUT}}\}$ beats the full $23$-archetype frontier by $47\%$ on mean regret (CI $[43\%, 52\%]$); three vertex additions cut mean/worst-group regret by up to $81\%$/$64\%$. We formalize these findings as a budgeted-pluralism trilemma, show the binding regime is empirically realized, and verify the conclusions are robust to distance-based welfare and to degraded routing. The instrument and audit harness are described in full in the appendices.

</details>


### [94] [Bandits in Prod: Hyperparameter Optimization at Inference Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01335)
*Louis Abraham,Tuan-Anh Nguyen,Nicolas Devatine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将生产系统中只能在线上请求上评估配置并观察嘈杂反馈的场景形式化为在线超参数优化（OHPO），将其建模为混合和条件搜索空间上的无穷多臂老虎机问题，并提出IMABO框架和IMOSS策略，在经典机器学习模型和LLM智能体的调优中取得最低累积遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多生产系统（尤其是现代智能体系统）只能在实时请求上评估配置并获得嘈杂反馈，且通常缺乏代表性验证数据，传统离线超参数优化方法不适用，需要一种能够在线学习的超参数优化框架。

Method: 将OHPO形式化为无穷多臂老虎机问题，提出IMABO通用框架，将任何用于选择已采样配置的bandit策略与任何用于提议新配置的oracle相结合。基于IMOSS（无重启的任意时间策略，活跃集以t^β增长）用三个实用oracle实例化：Tree-structured Parzen Estimator、逐坐标bandit驱动的incumbent-mutation oracle，以及预训练的tabular基础模型。

Result: 证明IMOSS的期望累积分位数遗憾界为O(p_ρ^{-1/β} + T^{(1+β)/2})，其中β∈(0,1)控制活跃集增长，p_ρ为提议配置落入搜索空间top-ρ比例的下界概率。三个oracle均优于均匀随机oracle基线，IMABO在从经典ML模型调优到LLM智能体配置的多种OHPO场景中获得最低累积遗憾。

Conclusion: IMABO为在线超参数优化提供了通用的、理论上有保证的框架，能够在嘈杂反馈下有效处理混合和条件搜索空间，并适用于从传统机器学习到LLM智能体配置的广泛生产场景。

Abstract: Many production systems can assess a configuration only by using it on live requests and observing noisy feedback. Modern agentic systems are a prominent example, with inference-time choices such as model selection, retrieval depth, prompting strategy, and decoding temperature, yet often with no representative validation data. We formalize this setting as Online Hyperparameter Optimization (OHPO) and cast it as an infinitely many-armed bandit over mixed and conditional search spaces. We introduce IMABO, a general framework that combines any bandit policy for choosing among already sampled configurations with any oracle for proposing new ones. We instantiate it with IMOSS, a restart-free anytime policy whose active set grows as $t^β$, and prove an expected cumulative quantile-regret bound of $O(p_ρ^{-1/β} + T^{(1+β)/2})$, where $β\in(0,1)$ controls active-set growth and $p_ρ$ lower-bounds the probability that a proposed configuration falls in the top-$ρ$ fraction of the search space. We combine IMOSS with three practical oracles: a Tree-structured Parzen Estimator, an incumbent-mutation oracle driven by a per-coordinate bandit, and a pretrained tabular foundation model, all three improving over the uniform random oracle baseline. IMABO obtains the lowest cumulative regret across diverse OHPO settings, from tuning classical machine-learning models to configuring LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [95] [SMELT: Scaling Laws for Compute-Matched MoE Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01343)
*Shaowen Wang,Ge Zhang,Kairong Luo,Yuhao Wu,Shaofan Liu,Jiaheng Liu,Wenhao Huang,Shen Yan,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SMELT配方（稀疏MoE Transformer，中层循环两次），在匹配每token FLOPs、总非嵌入参数和KV cache三项预算的前提下，将Transformer中一半的层循环执行两次，从而在不增加计算预算的情况下提升模型性能。在最高达54B非嵌入参数的规模上，SMELT在计算最优前沿节省6.8%-18.0%的训练FLOPs，且收益在代码等下游基准上表现突出，并在机制层面揭示第二次循环会减少注意力sink并将注意力权重转移至内容相关token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对Looped Transformer的评估大多在固定模型大小下进行，混淆了架构优势与额外FLOPs带来的收益。作者希望严格匹配各项计算与存储预算，检验层循环是否仍然能带来真实的性能提升。

Method: 在MoE Transformer上进行层循环实验，严格匹配每token FLOPs、非嵌入参数总量和KV cache三个预算。通过一系列消融实验，得出SMELT配方：仅将中间一半层循环两次。在四种规模（最高54B非嵌入参数）下分别拟合Chinchilla式缩放定律，并进行下游基准评估和机制分析。

Result: SMELT损失随计算量下降更快，在计算最优前沿节省6.8%-18.0%的训练FLOPs；下游基准增益超过验证损失所预测的水平，在代码任务上最大，并随样本长度和上下文示例数量增长；机制分析表明第二次访问降低了注意力sink并提升了对内容相关token的注意力权重。

Conclusion: 即使在严格匹配预算的条件下，层循环仍能改善Transformer性能，展现出"深度复用"作为一种实用的、可带来可度量增益的训练配方的潜力。

Abstract: Looped Transformers increase effective depth by iterating a shared block of layers, but most evaluations compare at fixed model size, conflating architectural advantage with extra FLOPs. We study looping on Mixture-of-Experts Transformers while closely matching per-token FLOPs, total non-embedding parameters, and KV cache. Through a series of ablations, we arrive at a recipe we call SMELT (Sparse MoE Transformer, middle layers Loop Twice), which loops the middle half of layers twice while matching the unlooped Baseline on all three budgets. We scale SMELT across four sizes up to 54B non-embedding parameters and fit a separate Chinchilla-style scaling law for each architecture. SMELT's loss drops faster with compute, saving 6.8--18.0\% of training FLOPs on the compute-optimal frontier. The advantage transfers to downstream benchmarks beyond what validation loss predicts, is largest on Code, and grows with sample length and the number of in-context examples. Mechanistic analysis shows that the second visit reduces the attention sink and redirects mass toward content-relevant tokens, an inductive bias that may underlie the observed performance gains. These results show that looping can improve Transformers even under budget matching, offering a practical recipe that turns depth reuse into measurable gains.

</details>


### [96] [Contribution-Aware Bandwidth Allocation for Multimodal Split Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01406)
*Iason Ofeidis,Leandros Tassiulas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ModalShare，一种面向多模态分裂学习的跨模态分配机制。在固定上行带宽预算下，通过Shapley贡献分数决定各模态激活的保留率，而非均分预算，从而提升多模态融合训练的准确性（在CREMA-D和MVSA上分别提升15.4和12.4个百分点，5倍压缩下）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型逐渐成为边缘感知的主流，但训练几乎全部在数据中心完成，因为客户端持有多种传感器流却无法为每种模态各托管一个编码器。分裂学习通过在设备上仅保留前几层使训练可行，但代价是每步都要经上行链路传输每种模态的smashed activations。现有压缩方案给每个模态相同的keep-ratio，使共享预算按smashed-activation维度比例分配，而这个比例与各模态对融合预测的实际贡献无关，导致资源分配不合理。

Method: 作者将预算在模态间的分配显式化为一个决策，称之为跨模态分配（inter-modality allocation）：在固定上行预算下，每个策略传输相同的期望负载，仅在负载如何在模态间切分上不同。提出的分配器ModalShare基于Shapley贡献分数为每个模态设定keep-ratio，该分数由服务器基于其已收到的激活联合体（coalitions of activations）计算。该分数衡量无需增加任何上行流量或客户端计算，且无需知晓哪条流是哪条的先验知识。

Result: 在5倍压缩下、匹配负载的条件下，ModalShare在CREMA-D上比均等keep-ratio提升15.4个百分点，在MVSA上提升12.4个百分点。在三种压缩器、三个数据集和四种预算下均表现出强劲性能。实验还表明现有多模态场景下的压缩器表现不佳，ModalShare可恢复其遗留的增益。

Conclusion: 跨模态分配（而非单纯的模态内压缩）是多模态分裂学习上行预算优化的关键。基于Shapley贡献的自适应keep-ratio分配在无额外开销的前提下显著优于均等分配，并能为现有多模态压缩器恢复其未充分利用的性能增益。

Abstract: Multimodal models are increasingly the default option for perception at the network edge, yet they are trained almost entirely in the datacenter, because a client holding several sensor streams cannot host an encoder per modality. Split Learning makes such training feasible by keeping only the first layers on the device, at the cost of an uplink that must carry smashed activations for every modality at every step. Existing compression schemes give each modality the same keep-ratio, so the shared budget is divided in proportion to smashed-activation dimension, a quantity unrelated to how much each modality contributes to the fused prediction. We make that division an explicit decision and call it inter-modality allocation: under a fixed uplink budget, every policy transmits the same expected payload and differs only in how that payload is split across modalities. Our allocator, ModalShare, sets each modality's keep-ratio from a Shapley contribution score that the server computes over coalitions of activations it has already received. Measuring this score adds no uplink traffic and no client-side computation, and needs no prior knowledge of which stream is which. ModalShare improves accuracy over equal keep-ratios by 15.4 and 12.4 percentage points on CREMA-D and MVSA at matched payload in 5x compression, with strong performance across three compressors, three datasets, and four budgets. We show that existing compressors underperform in multimodal settings, with ModalShare recovering what gains are left behind.

</details>


### [97] [Predicting Subsurface Abnormalities Growth using Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01417)
*Mehrdad Shafiei Dizaji,Hoda Azari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合物理信息神经网络（PINNs）的深度学习框架，用于探地雷达（GPR）数据的预测。该模型包含CNN、空间特征通道注意力（SFCA）、ConvLSTM和时间特征帧注意力（TFFA）模块，通过将电磁波传播的物理定律嵌入网络架构，显著提升了GPR数据预测的准确性，可广泛应用于桥梁桥面状况评估及其他民用基础设施的无损检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的GPR数据预测方法难以同时兼顾物理规律与数据特征，而现有深度学习方法缺乏物理约束，预测结果可能违背基本物理原理。研究者希望借鉴医学影像中预测肿瘤行为的范式，将物理信息嵌入深度学习模型，以提高基础设施退化预测的精度和可靠性。

Method: 构建了一个物理信息驱动的深度学习模型，由三个核心组件构成：CNN用于提取空间视觉特征；SFCA（空间特征通道注意力）机制通过自适应计算通道注意力权重来精调视觉特征响应；ConvLSTM结合TFFA（时间特征帧注意力）模块用于提取和精调时序特征。物理知识（电磁波传播规律）被直接嵌入神经网络架构中，确保预测结果符合物理约束。

Result: 实验表明，将物理知识直接集成到神经网络后，模型在GPR数据预测方面展现出更高的准确性。这种改进对桥梁桥面状况评估及其他土木基础设施的评估工作至关重要。

Conclusion: PINNs在无损检测（NDE）领域展现出变革性的潜力，不仅能提升基础设施退化预测的精度，还能通过物理模型视角加深对退化根本机制的理解。

Abstract: The research explores the pioneering integration of Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) into the domain of Ground-Penetrating Radar (GPR) data prediction. This research presents a detailed development framework for a specialized PINN model, proficient at interpreting and forecasting GPR data, much like how medical imaging models predict tumor behavior. By harnessing the synergy between deep learning algorithms and the physical laws governing subsurface structures or in medical terms, human tissues the model effectively embeds the physics of electromagnetic wave propagation into its architecture. This ensures that predictions not only align with fundamental physical principles but also mirror the precision needed in medical diagnostics for detecting and monitoring tumors. The suggested deep learning structure comprises three components: a CNN, a spatial feature channel attention (SFCA) mechanism, and ConvLSTM, along with temporal feature frame attention (TFFA) modules. The attention mechanism computes channel attention and temporal attention weights using self-adaptation, thereby fine tuning the visual and temporal feature responses to extract the most pertinent and significant visual and temporal features. By integrating physics directly into the neural network, our model has shown enhanced accuracy in forecasting GPR data. This improvement is vital for conducting effective assessments of bridge deck conditions and other evaluations related to civil infrastructure. The use of Physics Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) has demonstrated the potential to transform the field of Non-Destructive Evaluation (NDE) by enhancing the precision of infrastructure deterioration predictions. Moreover, it offers a deeper insight into the fundamental mechanisms of deterioration, viewed through the prism of physics-based models.

</details>


### [98] [Provably Safe Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01418)
*Tingting Ni,Maryam Kamgarpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究安全模拟器到现实迁移（safe sim-to-real transfer）问题：在真实环境数据采集受安全约束的条件下，如何利用不完美的模拟器减少真实世界交互次数，同时保证安全探索并学习任意潜在奖励函数的近最优可行策略。作者将问题形式化为奖励无关（reward-free）安全强化学习框架，设计了计算高效的算法，其真实世界样本复杂度上界显式刻画了模拟器带来的收益（即模拟与现实不匹配程度的函数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界强化学习样本获取成本高昂，常见做法是先在有廉价样本的模拟器中训练策略再迁移到现实。然而由于模拟器与现实不匹配，该直接迁移常常失败。纠正不匹配需要在真实系统上收集数据，但许多应用（如机器人、医疗）中数据收集本身受安全约束。这引出了安全模拟器到现实迁移问题：如何在利用不完美模拟器的同时，确保真实世界数据收集安全并学到目标系统的近最优可行策略。

Method: 将安全模拟器到现实迁移问题形式化为奖励无关安全强化学习框架。设计一个计算高效的算法，利用模拟器信息来可证明地减少真实世界交互次数，同时保证安全探索，并能对任意潜在奖励函数计算近最优可行策略。算法提供真实样本复杂度界，该界以模拟与真实不匹配程度为参数，刻画使用模拟器所带来的收益。

Result: 提出了一种计算高效的算法，实现了在安全约束下利用模拟器信息减少真实世界交互；给出了真实世界样本复杂度的理论界，阐明了模拟器在降低样本复杂度方面的益处与模拟到真实不匹配程度的关系。

Conclusion: 本文在奖励无关安全强化学习框架下解决了安全模拟器到现实迁移问题，证明了在不完美模拟器辅助下能够既保证真实世界安全探索，又对任意奖励函数学习近最优可行策略，并量化了模拟器收益与不匹配程度之间的权衡。

Abstract: To mitigate the sample complexity of real-world reinforcement learning (RL), a common practice is to first train a policy in a simulator, where samples are cheap, and then deploy the learned policy in the real world with the hope that it generalizes effectively. Such direct sim-to-real transfer is not guaranteed to succeed: simulator-trained policies can be suboptimal in the real world due to sim-to-real mismatch. Correcting this mismatch requires collecting data from the real system, but in many applications, such as robotics and healthcare, this data-collection process is itself subject to safety constraints. This gives rise to the problem of safe sim-to-real transfer: how can an agent exploit an imperfect simulator while ensuring safe real-world data collection and learning a near-optimal feasible policy for the target system? We address this problem by formulating safe sim-to-real transfer within the framework of reward-free safe RL. We design a computationally efficient algorithm that exploits simulator information to provably reduce real-world interaction while ensuring safe exploration and enabling the computation of a near-optimal feasible policy for any potential reward function. Our real-world sample complexity bound characterizes the benefit of using the simulator in terms of the sim-to-real mismatch.

</details>


### [99] [CATeye: Coupled Attribute-Topology Invariance Learning for Voucher Abuse Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01425)
*Tian Tian,Shuaicheng Niu,Hao Kuang,Yuanhang Hu,Dong Li,Zhiqi Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CATeye框架应对电商优惠券欺诈检测中的耦合属性-拓扑分布偏移问题，通过两个可学习选择器（属性不变性选择器AIS和边不变性选择器EIS）过滤非不变信息，构造多视图以学习域不变表征，在Lazada私有数据集和公开基准上超越九个强基线，最高F1提升8.61%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商优惠券滥用是重大挑战，恶意用户利用促销券牟利。欺诈模式随时间与地域快速演化，导致分布偏移使现有检测模型性能退化且需频繁重训。现有方法未有效应对属性偏移与拓扑偏移相互耦合放大的问题（属性近邻构建的边使环境驱动的属性偏移引发拓扑偏移，并通过GNN消息传递放大变异信号），亟需能够同时解耦并抑制属性-拓扑耦合偏移的框架。

Method: 提出CATeye框架，核心包括两个可学习选择器：(1) 属性不变性选择器（AIS），学习节点自适应掩码以过滤非不变属性；(2) 边不变性选择器（EIS），在保留不变属性的条件下采样不变子图并隔离非不变边。基于得到的不变与非不变组件，构造多个视图并施加视图特定目标，以强调域不变表征、抑制域特定变异。

Result: 在Lazada（东南亚主要电商平台）专有数据集及一个公开基准上，CATeye在九个强域泛化与图异常检测基线中表现最优，相比最强基线在平均F1分数上最高提升8.61%。源代码已公开。

Conclusion: CATeye有效解决了优惠券欺诈检测中属性与拓扑耦合的分布偏移问题，通过解耦不变与可变异因素实现鲁棒的跨域图异常检测，在工业与公开数据上均验证了优越性。

Abstract: Voucher abuse poses a major challenge in e-commerce, where malicious users exploit promotional vouchers for profit. Unfortunately, fraud patterns evolve rapidly over time and across regions, causing distribution shifts that degrade existing detection models unless retrained frequently. To tackle this, we propose the Coupled Attribute-Topology Invariance Learning framework (CATeye). The key challenge arises from coupled attribute-topology shift, where edges built from attribute proximity cause environment-driven attribute shift to induce shifted topology, thereby amplifying variant signals through GNN message passing. CATeye sees through such coupled shifts with two learnable selectors. First, an Attribute Invariance Selector (AIS) learns node-adaptive masks to filter out non-invariant attributes. Then, conditioned on retained invariant attributes, an Edge Invariance Selector (EIS) samples an invariant subgraph and isolates non-invariant edges. Using the resulting invariant and non-invariant components, CATeye constructs multiple views and applies view-specific objectives to emphasize domain-invariant representations while suppressing domain-specific variations. Experiments on both a proprietary dataset from Lazada, a major Southeast Asian e-commerce platform, and a public benchmark show that CATeye consistently outperforms nine strong domain generalization and graph anomaly detection baselines, achieving up to an 8.61% improvement in average F1 score over the strongest baseline. Source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Tian0426/CATeye.

</details>


### [100] [TRIAGE: Three-level Routing and Intelligent Agent Guidance for Efficient Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01428)
*Ruocan Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TRIAGE三层路由框架，通过TaaS（Trajectory-as-a-Skill）将历史执行轨迹抽象为可复用技能，实现推理经验的复用。在安全监控查询上节省62.3% token，跨域ToolBench上节省76.3%，且具备从冷启动到成熟的在线学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReAct范式的LLM智能体存在根本性效率问题：每次查询都从零开始完整推理循环，相似查询重复执行相同步骤，无法利用历史执行经验，导致token消耗高昂。

Method: 提出TRIAGE三层路由框架：(1) Direct Reuse级直接复用相同查询轨迹，零token；(2) Skill Substitution级通过确定性参数替换实现技能复用，零token；(3) Full ReAct级处理新查询并自动存储供未来复用。核心创新TaaS将历史轨迹抽象为可复用技能，并配套自动Skill提取机制从高频轨迹模式中蒸馏出确定性技能，形成正反馈循环。

Result: 在1007条安全监控查询上实现62.3% token节省，其中L2占比56.0%、L1占比5.5%（均零成本执行）；在ToolBench 15个领域345条查询上实现76.3% token缩减，验证跨域泛化性。在线学习实验中L2命中率在前100条查询内从0%升至57%，平均token成本从198降至74.7。

Conclusion: TRIAGE通过层级化路由和经验复用显著降低LLM智能体的token消耗，实现"越用越高效"的正反馈效应，且在多个领域验证了泛化能力。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents based on the ReAct paradigm have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in tool use and task execution. However, ReAct suffers from a fundamental efficiency problem: every query triggers a complete reasoning loop from scratch, and similar queries repeat identical steps without leveraging historical experience. We propose TRIAGE,a three-level routing framework that reduces token consumption by reusing historical execution trajectories. Its core innovation is TaaS (Trajectory-as-a-Skill), which abstracts historical execution trajectories into reusable skills, realizing 'experience as a service'. TRIAGE classifies queries into three levels: (1) Direct Reuse-identical queries, 0 tokens; (2) Skill Substitution-similar queries, 0 tokens via deterministic parameter substitution; (3) Full ReAct-novel queries, automatically stored for future reuse. In large-scale experiments on 1,007 security monitoring queries, TRIAGE achieves 62.3% token savings, with 56.0% of queries at Level 2 and 5.5% at Level 1, both executing at zero cost. Cross-domain validation on ToolBench (15 domains, 345 queries) achieves 76.3% token reduction, confirming the generalizability of semantic routing. An online learning experiment demonstrates cold-start-to-mature evolution: the L2 hit rate rises from 0% to 57% within the first 100 queries, and the average token cost drops from 198 to 74.7. We also propose an automatic Skill extraction mechanism that distills high-frequency trajectory patterns into deterministic Skills, creating a positive feedback loop of 'the more you use it, the more efficient it becomes'.

</details>


### [101] [Learning Sparse Decision Trees via Transformer Variational Auto-Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01430)
*Giacomo Fidone,Alessio Cascione,Riccardo Guidotti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TREVIS方法，利用Tree Transformer VAE的潜空间将决策树离散搜索空间转换为连续空间，通过可微代理模型进行梯度优化，实现决策树预测性能与结构稀疏性的联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策树学习算法主要关注预测性能，忽视了与其他期望属性（如结构稀疏性）的联合优化，因此需要一种能够针对复杂目标学习决策树的方法。

Method: TREVIS基于Tree Transformer Variational Auto-Encoder（TTVAE），将决策树映射到潜在表示空间，将原本离散的搜索空间替换为连续空间，从而通过可微替代模型实现基于梯度的优化。

Result: 实验表明，TREVIS发现的决策树在预测性能上可与现有近最优算法相媲美，同时改进了其结构稀疏性。

Conclusion: TREVIS成功实现了决策树预测性能与结构稀疏性的联合优化，证明了利用潜空间连续化方法学习具备复杂目标的决策树的有效性。

Abstract: Decision trees are among the most widely used models in machine learning, largely due to their transparent decision logic, making them well-suited for high-stakes decision-making contexts. However, most existing learning algorithms focus on predictive performance, overlooking the joint optimization of other desirable properties, such as structural sparsity. In this work we propose TREVIS, an approach for learning decision trees with respect to complex objectives, based on the exploration of the latent space of a Tree Transformer Variational Auto-Encoder (TTVAE). By mapping decision trees onto latent representations, TREVIS replaces the discrete search space with a continuous one, enabling gradient-based optimization via a differentiable surrogate model. We experiment with TREVIS for learning decision trees that jointly optimize predictive performance and sparsity. Results show that TREVIS discovers decision trees matching the predictive performance of existing near-optimal algorithms while improving their structural sparsity.

</details>


### [102] [Efficiently Estimating Optimal Hyperparameter Scaling Laws through Power-Law Entropy Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01431)
*Zhiliang Chen,Sebastian Ament,David Eriksson,Maximilian Balandat,Eytan Bakshy,Jihao Andreas Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Power-Law Entropy Search (PLES)，一种基于多保真贝叶斯优化的计算成本感知采集函数，用于高效估计大语言模型训练中最优超参数的幂律缩放规律。PLES通过自适应实验选择能最大化降低缩放律估计不确定性的候选配置，在合成基准、真实LLM训练数据的代理模型以及实际预训练运行中，均以不到传统网格搜索十分之一的计算预算收敛到准确的最优超参数缩放律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型训练的最优超参数随模型规模和数据规模变化存在幂律缩放规律，但传统方法需要通过数千次训练运行的穷举网格搜索来估计这些规律，消耗巨大的计算资源。因此需要一种计算高效的方法来预测生产规模下的最优超参数配置，避免昂贵的大规模调优。

Method: PLES是一种构建在多保真贝叶斯优化框架上的计算成本感知采集函数。其核心创新在于不优化单一目标函数，而是搜索能够降低整个缩放律估计整体不确定性的候选配置。每次迭代中，PLES选择在单位计算成本下能最大化减少缩放律估计不确定性的候选配置，从而自然倾向于选择信息量丰富的小规模实验。

Result: PLES在合成基准、基于真实LLM训练数据拟合的代理模型以及实际LLM预训练运行三种场景下均进行了验证。在所有设置中，PLES以不到传统网格搜索和其他基线方法十分之一的计算预算，即可收敛到准确的幂律缩放律估计。

Conclusion: PLES证明了通过计算成本感知的自适应实验设计，可以在极低的计算预算下高效估计最优超参数缩放规律，显著降低了大规模LLM训练中超参数预测的计算开销，为生产环境中的大规模超参数配置提供了实用且经济的解决方案。

Abstract: Optimal hyperparameter scaling laws describe how the best hyperparameters for large language model (LLM) training change with model and data scale, enabling practitioners to predict optimal configurations at production scales without expensive large-scale tuning. However, estimating these scaling laws conventionally requires exhaustive grid searches over thousands of training runs, consuming enormous computational resources. We introduce Power-Law Entropy Search (PLES), a computational cost-aware acquisition function built on multi-fidelity Bayesian optimization that efficiently estimates optimal hyperparameter scaling laws through adaptive experimentation. A key innovation in PLES is that it searches for candidates that reduce the overall uncertainty of a scaling law estimate, instead of optimizing a single objective function. At each iteration, PLES selects the candidate configuration that maximally reduces the uncertainty of the scaling law estimates per unit computational cost, naturally favoring informative small-scale experiments. We evaluate PLES on synthetic benchmarks, surrogate models fitted to real LLM training data, and actual LLM pre-training runs. Across all settings, PLES converges to accurate optimal hyperparameter scaling laws using less than one-tenth of the computational budget required by conventional grid search and other baselines.

</details>


### [103] [Edge-Girth as a Structural Edge Feature for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01441)
*Lilian Marey,Charlotte Laclau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于边围长（edge-girth）及其重数（multiplicity）的逐边结构描述符，可在任意长度下报告经过每条边的最短环信息，注入门控消息传递架构（EGAGNN）后显著超越1-WL表达能力，在ZINC-12k上以104k参数达到测试MAE比最强门控基线低三倍的效果；但同时证明当所有边看到相同长度和数量的最短环时，该描述符退化为常数，模型表达能力回落至1-WL界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于消息传递的GNN表达能力受限于1-WL测试，常规补救方案是通过预计算的固定小图结构描述符（如三角形或长环计数）增强节点或边特征，但此类方法需提前确定子结构大小，且该选择通常在数据未知的情况下盲目做出。作者旨在研究一种无需预先设定环长度的结构描述符。

Method: 采用边围长（经过某边的最短环长度）及其重数（此类最短环的数量）作为逐边不变描述符，该描述符可通过每条边上的单次广度优先搜索（BFS）精确计算，能报告任意长度的环。将此描述符注入门控消息传递架构，构成EGAGNN模型。同时在理论上证明该描述符在特定对称条件下的局限性。

Result: EGAGNN在ZINC-12k回归基准上以104k参数达到测试MAE，比最强门控比较器低三倍；与有界环计数描述符相比（同一架构下），需用两倍通道数才能匹配仅计数至长度八的字典，而字典上限为四时效果与无结构信息相当。在BREC基准的400对图中，90对满足所有边看到相同最短环的条件，EGAGNN无法区分其中任何一对。

Conclusion: 边围长描述符提供了一种免预选环长度上限的结构信息注入方案，在实践中能显著增强GNN表达能力，但其能力存在结构性上限：当图中所有边具有相同的最短环长度和数量时，描述符退化为常数，模型回到1-WL表达力界限，这一局限在BREC基准上得到完美验证。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNN) based on message passing are provably no more powerful than the one-dimensional Weisfeiler--Leman colour-refinement test (1-WL): two graphs it cannot tell apart receive identical representations, however deep or wide the network. A common remedy augments node or edge features with precomputed structural descriptors, most often counts of a fixed small subgraph such as triangles or longer cycles, but such counts require committing in advance to the size of the substructure counted, a choice usually made blind to the data. We study a descriptor that avoids this choice. The edge-girth of an edge is the length of a shortest cycle through it, and its multiplicity is the number of such shortest cycles; together they form a per-edge invariant that reports cycles of arbitrary length, computable exactly by a single breadth-first search per edge. Injected into a gated message-passing architecture, EGAGNN, it reaches a test MAE a factor three below the closest gated comparator on the ZINC-12k regression benchmark at 104k parameters; against bounded cycle-counting descriptors under the same architecture, it matches only a dictionary counting cycles up to length eight, using twice as many channels, while a dictionary capped at length four performs no better than no structural information at all. On graph discrimination we prove a matching limitation: on graphs where every edge sees the same number of shortest cycles of the same length, the descriptor becomes constant and any model built on it collapses back to the 1-WL bound. This holds without exception across all 400 pairs of the BREC benchmark: not one of the 90 such pairs is distinguished.

</details>


### [104] [Diffusion as a Training Curriculum for Timestep-Free Iterative Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01449)
*Mariia Drozdova,Aidan Sirbu,Pietro Miotti,Robert Obryk,Mayalen Etcheverry,Eyvind Niklasson,Blake Richards*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将扩散模型与递归推理器相结合，在去噪器中加入持久隐状态并移除时间步条件，得到一个可任意深度运行的统一更新，作为随时求解器。在Sudoku-Extreme上达到99.90%精确求解率，Maze-Unique上达到98.93%。推理时无需渐进去噪，只需持续注入高斯噪声即可高效探索解空间；但训练时有序退火损坏至关重要，表明扩散的核心贡献是去噪训练课程而非推理采样过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和递归推理器都是迭代过程，但两者跨迭代传递信息的方式不同。作者希望探究如何将扩散模型的机制与递归推理结合，以构建一个可任意深度运行、性能随推理深度持续提升的随时求解器，从而避免传统推理模型所需的并行rollout、候选选择和外部验证器。

Method: 在扩散去噪器中添加一个持久隐状态，并移除时间步条件化，得到一个可任意深度运行的统一共享更新。训练时使用有序退火损坏；推理时则保持最大损坏程度，每次用新鲜高斯噪声替换所有非线索变量，通过噪声注入机制让单条轨迹高效探索解空间。

Result: Sudoku-Extreme上达到99.90%精确求解率，Maze-Unique上达到98.93%求解率。推理深度远超训练时的rollout长度和反向传播窗口，精度仍持续改善。推理时保持最大损坏（无渐进去噪）仍能近乎完美求解并收敛到稳定解。

Conclusion: 扩散模型对此随时求解器的主要贡献不在于推理时的采样过程，而在于其作为去噪训练课程所发挥的作用。这一发现为理解扩散模型与推理器的结合提供了新视角，表明噪声注入本身足以在推理时实现有效的解空间探索。

Abstract: Diffusion models and recursive reasoners are both iterative, but they carry information across iterations differently. We add a persistent hidden state to a diffusion denoiser and remove its timestep conditioning, leaving a single shared update that can be run to arbitrary depth. The result is an anytime solver: accuracy keeps improving with inference depth far beyond the rollout lengths and backpropagation window used in training, reaching 99.90% exact solve on Sudoku-Extreme. We also obtain 98.93% solve rate on Maze-Unique. Surprisingly, progressive denoising is unnecessary at inference: holding corruption at its maximum by replacing every non-clue variable with fresh Gaussian noise at each step retains near-perfect solving and converges to stable solutions. This simple noise-injection mechanism enables a single trajectory to efficiently explore the solution space and settle on the correct answer without parallel rollouts, candidate selection, or external verifiers required by prior reasoning models. Nonetheless, ordered annealed corruption remains critical during training, which suggests that diffusion's primary contribution to our anytime solver is not a sampling procedure at inference, but a denoising training curriculum.

</details>


### [105] [Rethinking Learnability in Offline Data-driven Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01493)
*Chao Qian,Chen-Guang Wang,Rong-Xi Tan,Ke Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"算法依赖可学习性"（algorithm-dependent learnability）概念，证明其在若干代表性离散与连续优化设置中充分（而非仅必要），并据此形式化轨迹学习框架，提出UGTL方法，在Design-Bench五个任务上取得25种方法中最优的总体平均排名3.1/25。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线数据驱动优化仅利用固定历史评估数据寻找高质量解，无需在线评估，因此极具吸引力。然而一个根本问题尚未回答：离线优化所需的最小可学习性条件是什么？已有理论研究指出PAC可学习性不充分——即使大多数区域被良好学习，最优点所在区域仍可能未被学透。这促使研究者探索更强或更匹配算法需求的充分性条件。

Method: 1) 理论上提出算法依赖可学习性（algorithm-dependent learnability），要求学习器仅在优化器搜索轨迹上准确；证明其值查询形式对代表性离散设定（子模最大化的贪心和局部搜索）充分，其一阶类比形式对凸最小化上的投影梯度下降充分。2) 据此形式化轨迹学习框架，包含轨迹构建、轨迹建模与候选生成三部分，并分析已有基于轨迹的方法。3) 提出UGTL方法：构建局部连贯的改进轨迹以反映可行的搜索路径，用条件扩散模型建模，并选取多样化的候选解集合。

Result: 在Design-Bench五个任务上，UGTL在25种方法中获得最佳总体平均排名3.1/25。受控轨迹分析与跨架构替换实验进一步证实轨迹构建策略对性能提升起重要作用。

Conclusion: 算法依赖可学习性为离线优化提供了比PAC更匹配实践的理论保障，弥补了理论充分性条件的空白；轨迹学习框架统一了现有方法，UGTL验证了理论动机指导实践方法的有效性，轨迹构建是离线优化的关键环节。

Abstract: Black-Box Optimization (BBO) has found broad applications, but evolutionary algorithms and Bayesian optimization face efficiency challenges as real-world BBO problems grow increasingly complex. Data-driven optimization improves the efficiency of BBO algorithms by learning from data. Offline data-driven optimization seeks high-quality solutions using only a fixed set of previous evaluations, attracting substantial attention because it requires no additional online evaluations. Many offline optimization methods have been proposed, but a fundamental question remains unanswered: what learnability is sufficient for offline optimization? Prior theoretical studies show that Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) learnability is insufficient, as the optimal region may remain poorly learned even when most regions are well learned. In this paper, we propose algorithm-dependent learnability, which requires accuracy only on the optimizer's trajectory. We prove that its value-query form is sufficient for representative discrete settings, including greedy and local search for submodular maximization, while its first-order analogue is sufficient for projected gradient descent on convex minimization. Motivated by this notion, we formalize a trajectory-learning framework comprising trajectory construction, trajectory modeling, and candidate generation, and analyze existing trajectory-based methods under it. We further propose Uncertainty-aware Gradient-guided Trajectory Learning (UGTL), which constructs locally coherent improvement trajectories reflecting plausible search paths, models them with conditional diffusion, and selects a diverse candidate set. On five Design-Bench tasks, UGTL achieves the best aggregate mean rank, $3.1/25$, among 25 methods. Controlled trajectory analyses and cross-architecture replacements confirm that our trajectory construction plays a significant role in the improvement.

</details>


### [106] [Optimizing Byzantine Node Placement in Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01495)
*Edoardo Gabrielli,Gabriele Tolomei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出拜占庭放置影响（BPI）度量，用于形式化去中心化联邦学习（DFL）中拜占庭节点的最优放置问题，该问题以往被忽视。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DFL安全评估仅关注拜占庭参与者的行为（如投毒策略），而忽视了哪些节点被攻陷这一维度。由于聚合在通信图上分布式进行，拜占庭节点的位置决定了恶意影响的传播路径。攻击者应在固定预算下选择能最大化对诚实节点有限时间影响（累计暴露）的节点集合。

Method: 将拜占庭放置建模为显式对抗决策，基于实际gossip动态（八卦协议传播机制）推导出集合级影响度量BPI，该度量量化训练周期内诚实节点对拜占庭源的累计暴露程度。BPI捕获加权多跳传播及被攻陷节点间的交互效应，区别于基于节点中心性的启发式准则。作者设计了优化BPI的高效算法。

Result: 在六个异构图族、非定向模型投毒及后门攻击场景下评估，BPI引导的放置方案能持续识别高破坏性配置；在通过拜占庭鲁棒聚合放宽线性gossip假设后依然有效。实验表明不同网络结构下BI均能有效定位最具破坏力的拜占庭放置。

Conclusion: 拜占庭放置是DFL威胁模型与鲁棒性评估中关键但未被充分建模的维度，BPI为系统评估此类风险提供了可计算且有效的工具。

Abstract: Security evaluations of decentralized federated learning (DFL) typically focus on how Byzantine participants behave, while largely overlooking which participants are compromised. Yet, because aggregation is distributed over a communication graph, the placement of Byzantine nodes determines how malicious influence propagates through the network. We therefore treat Byzantine placement as an explicit adversarial decision and formulate the attacker's objective as selecting, under a fixed compromise budget, the set of participants that maximizes its finite-time impact on honest nodes. To approximate this objective without executing the learning process for every candidate placement, we introduce Byzantine Placement Influence (BPI), a set-level measure derived from the actual gossip dynamics that quantifies the cumulative exposure of honest nodes to Byzantine sources over the training horizon. Unlike placement criteria based on node centrality heuristics, BPI directly accounts for weighted multi-hop propagation and interactions among compromised nodes. We develop efficient algorithms for optimizing BPI and evaluate them across six heterogeneous graph families, untargeted model poisoning, and backdoor attacks. BPI-guided placements consistently identify highly damaging configurations across different network structures and remain effective when the linear gossip assumption is relaxed through Byzantine-robust aggregation. Our results show that Byzantine placement is a critical but under-modeled dimension of DFL threat models and robustness evaluations.

</details>


### [107] [LatentPress: Context Compression Beyond Text and Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01507)
*Zhengze Zhou,Hejian Sang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LatentPress提出将压缩上下文编码为连续记忆token，使冻结的decoder可直接通过输入嵌入接口读取，无需推理时的文本重建。在4-16倍压缩下，LongMemEval准确率优于未压缩证据和文本摘要/OCR方案，写作耗时约43ms，读取速度比原始上下文快5-9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的上下文压缩通常以人类可读文本或需解码的渲染图像形式呈现，即使消费方是语言模型也如此，这导致不必要的解码开销且压缩质量有限。本文旨在探索一种直接面向语言模型（machine-facing）的第三种上下文表示——连续潜在token，避免文本/视觉重建的中间环节。

Method: LatentPress采用"reader-matched writer"架构：一个轻量的writer模型（仅4.2M-26.2M参数，约为decoder的0.1%）将对话历史或长文档压缩为连续记忆token，冻结的decoder通过这些token的输入嵌入接口直接读取。训练仅更新一个adapter，可实现4-16倍压缩。方法在多个数据集（LongMemEval、LongBench-QA、UltraChat）上验证，并测试了零样本跨域迁移能力。

Result: 在LongMemEval上，LatentPress在7.70倍压缩下达到0.504准确率，优于未压缩证据的0.490，远超文本摘要的0.184和基于OCR的压缩（0.426降至0.312）。在LongBench-QA上，域内writer在4-8倍压缩时达到或超过原始上下文阅读效果，16倍压缩略逊于原始。写作每段对话仅需43ms，比文本摘要或OCR重建快约一个数量级；读取速度比原始上下文或缓存OCR快5-9倍。零样本迁移从UltraChat到LongMemEval以及从LongMemEval到LongBench均验证有效。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，直接软token可作为面向机器的实用上下文接口，替代文本和视觉表示。LatentPress证明压缩可以不经过人可读的解码阶段而直接服务语言模型消费，兼具更高的准确率与显著的速度优势，且具备跨域零样本泛化能力，为长上下文处理提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Compressed context is usually carried as human-readable text or as rendered images that must be decoded, even when its consumer is a language model. We introduce LatentPress, which writes conversational histories and long documents into a third representation: continuous memory tokens that a frozen decoder reads directly through its input-embedding interface, with no text reconstruction at inference. A small reader-matched writer compresses $4$-$16\times$ while training only an adapter (4.2M-26.2M parameters, $\sim\!0.1\%$ of the decoder). On LongMemEval, LatentPress reaches $0.504$ accuracy at $7.70\times$ compression versus $0.490$ for uncompressed evidence, outperforming text summaries (0.184) and OCR-based compression (0.426 to 0.312). On LongBench-QA, in-domain writers match or exceed raw-context reading at $4$-$8\times$ compression, while $16\times$ trails raw. Writing takes 43ms per conversation, roughly an order of magnitude faster than text summarization or OCR reconstruction, and reading is $5$-$9\times$ faster than raw context or cached OCR. We validate the interface under two transfer settings, zero-shot from UltraChat to LongMemEval memory QA and from LongMemEval-derived QA to unseen LongBench document domains, establishing direct soft tokens as a practical machine-facing context interface beyond text and vision. The implementation of the experiments could be found at: https://github.com/xuyd16ai/context_softtoken_compress .

</details>


### [108] [Quantum Sparse Autoencoders for Q-Matrix Estimation in Cognitive Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01537)
*Arif Hassan Zidan,Yi Pan,Bowen Guo,Xiang Li,Yu Bao,Yingfeng Wang,Tianming Liu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将量子机器学习（QML）应用于认知诊断领域，提出一种新颖的量子稀疏自编码器（QSAE）用于Q矩阵估计。通过60个模拟数据集与9个真实评估数据集的基准测试，发现QSAE虽在部分模拟条件下平均精度略低于经典自编码器（CAE），但在49/60个条件下方差更低、稳定性更强，且在9个真实数据集中有6个超越CAE，表明量子机器学习在此场景的主要优势不是普适性精度提升，而是更强的稳健性和对真实数据潜结构复杂性的探索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Q矩阵在认知诊断中至关重要，但数据驱动的Q矩阵估计面临挑战，尤其是涉及多相关技能以及真实响应模式偏离理想生成假设时。现有方法在鲁棒性和处理复杂潜结构方面存在局限，需要探索新的计算范式来应对这些困难。

Method: 提出量子稀疏自编码器（QSAE）：先将每个学生的二值响应向量通过编码器嵌入量子电路，再压缩为稀疏潜表示，最后将该表示映射到Q矩阵。同时在60个模拟数据集和9个真实评估数据集上，与经典自编码器（CAE）进行系统对比基准测试。

Result: CAE在部分模拟条件下平均精度略高，但QSAE在60个条件中的49个表现出更低的方差，稳定性显著更好；在9个真实数据集中，QSAE在6个数据集上优于CAE。

Conclusion: QML在该场景的核心贡献并非在所有情况下都能提升精度，而是提供更强的鲁棒性和对真实数据中潜结构复杂性的探索能力，凸显了基于量子的方法在认知诊断Q矩阵估计中的独特价值。

Abstract: Q-matrices play a central role in cognitive diagnosis within educational data mining (EDM), specifying which latent skills each assessment item requires. Data-driven Q-matrix estimation remains challenging when assessments involve many correlated skills and when real response patterns depart from idealized generative assumptions. We introduce a novel quantum sparse autoencoder (QSAE) for Q-matrix estimation, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first application of quantum machine learning (QML) to cognitive diagnosis. Overall, the QSAE embeds each student's binary response vector into a quantum circuit using an encoder, compresses it into a sparse latent representation, and maps that representation to the Q-matrix. We benchmark the QSAE against a classical autoencoder (CAE) across 60 simulated datasets and 9 real-world assessment datasets. The results reveal complementary strengths. Although the CAE partially achieves higher average accuracy under several simulation conditions, the QSAE is substantially more stable across replications, exhibiting lower variance in 49 of the 60 conditions. Moreover, on real assessment data, the QSAE outperforms the CAE on 6 of the 9 datasets. These findings suggest that the principal advancement of QML in this setting is not universal accuracy improvement, but enhanced robustness and capability to explore latent-structure complexity in real datasets.

</details>


### [109] [NashDreamer: Model-Based Reinforcement Learning for Zero-Sum Imperfect-Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01549)
*Tomáš Holeček,Viliam Lisý*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NashDreamer，一个面向二人零和博弈不完全信息游戏（IIGs）的基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）框架。它通过集中式的多智能体循环状态空间模型（MARSSM）将环境动态与对手策略对观测的影响解耦，从而解决对手诱导的非平稳性和分散式模型学习的可辨识性障碍。实验在四个基准游戏中验证了其相比无模型基线在训练早期的样本效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MBRL在单智能体领域已取得显著成果，但其在竞争性不完全信息游戏（IIGs）中的扩展仍未被充分探索。多智能体环境中，对手诱导的非平稳性使学习过程复杂化，且分散式模型学习面临严重的可辨识性障碍。作者论证了集中式模型学习在数学上是必要的，并据此提出统一框架。

Method: 提出NashDreamer框架，核心是集中式Multi-Agent Recurrent State-Space Model（MARSSM），它解耦环境动态与玩家策略对个体观测的影响。该框架兼容任意策略梯度算法，并在理想化模型下继承其对纳什均衡的收敛保证。

Result: 在四个基准游戏的实证评估中，NashDreamer在训练早期相比无模型基线显著提升了样本效率。此外，理论分析揭示了Dreamer系列架构在随机环境中对后验坍塌（posterior collapse）的脆弱性。

Conclusion: 本文确立了集中式模型学习在多智能体IIG中的数学必要性，提出了有效的NashDreamer框架并验证了其样本效率优势，同时指出了Dreamer架构家族在优化景观中的潜在缺陷（后验坍塌），将其作为开放挑战留给后续研究。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) has achieved remarkable results in single-agent domains, yet its extension to competitive imperfect information games (IIGs) remains underexplored. In multi-agent settings, opponent-induced non-stationarity complicates the learning process, and decentralized model learning faces severe identifiability barriers, which we argue make centralized model learning a mathematical necessity. Building on this analysis, we propose NashDreamer, a principled MBRL framework for two-player zero-sum IIGs. It introduces a centralized Multi-Agent Recurrent State-Space Model (MARSSM) that decouples environment dynamics from the effect of players' strategies on their individual observations. NashDreamer is designed to use arbitrary policy gradient algorithms and inherits their convergence guarantees towards Nash equilibria under an idealized model. Empirical evaluations across four benchmark games demonstrate that NashDreamer substantially improves sample efficiency over model-free baselines early in the training. Finally, we theoretically analyze the architecture's optimization landscape, identifying the vulnerability of the Dreamer family of algorithms to posterior collapse in stochastic environments. We leave it as an open challenge.

</details>


### [110] [A Mathematical Theory of Reusable Neural Bases for Network Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01550)
*Binshuai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了线性可复用神经基座架构（LRNBA），通过将每个网络块表示为共享神经基座的线性组合，在显著降低内存成本和提升参数效率的同时，允许在相同参数量下构建更宽更深的网络，并实现与经典架构相当甚至更优的收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型AI模型在训练和推理过程中面临着严重的内存瓶颈，参数效率低下成为制约模型规模扩展的关键问题。

Method: 受循环神经网络（RNN）设计启发，将每个网络块表示为共享神经基座集合的线性组合，通过基座复用实现高网络压缩率，同时保持稳定训练；该架构支持在相同参数预算下构建更宽更深的网络。

Result: 大量实验表明，LRNBA在收敛速度和损失值方面达到甚至优于经典架构，同时保持稳定的训练动力学。

Conclusion: LRNBA证明了通过神经基座线性组合与共享复用的方式，能够有效缓解大规模AI模型的内存瓶颈，为构建更大规模、更高参数效率的神经网络提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: As large AI models become increasingly prevalent across a wide range of applications, memory cost has become a critical bottleneck in both training and inference. To mitigate this issue, we introduce the Linear Reusable Neural Bases Architecture (LRNBA), a novel framework aimed at improving parameter efficiency and reducing memory cost. Inspired by recurrent neural network (RNN) designs, the core idea of our approach is to represent each network block as a linear combination of a shared set of neural bases, thereby enjoying highly network compression rate while maintaining stable training. The proposed architecture allows for the construction of significantly wider and deeper networks under the same parameter budget. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our model achieves comparable or even faster convergence and lower loss than classical architectures, while maintaining stable training dynamics.

</details>


### [111] [Retrieved but not ranked: surface-form bias in structural retrieval, from mathematics to agent trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01556)
*Nabira Rashid,Manolis Kellis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了"表面形式与含义被刻意分离"的嵌入检索任务，涵盖两个领域：竞赛数学（MathNet-Retrieve，500查询/117,088项语料库）与具身智能体轨迹（ALFWorld衍生，118查询/336条轨迹）。研究发现数学领域检索完全失败：最重伪装层级的Hit@1为0.0%，但正确答案几乎总在前10名；轨迹领域当金标需涉及不同物体/容器时检索降至超几何随机水平以下。词法重排器在数学中起反作用而轨迹中有帮助；LLM重排器分别恢复数学领域5-63%和轨迹43-76%的差距，但相当部分数学恢复来自记忆而非理解。下游配对实验（210查询）显示oracle检索与对抗性差检索无显著差异（McNemar p=0.678），求解器69.5%的零样本准确率主要只是截断代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入检索基准通常混同表面相似性与真正的语义/结构相似性。本文的动机是构建一个将表面形式与内在含义刻意分离的评估协议，以判断现代嵌入模型究竟是"懂得"任务结构，还是仅仅锚定字面token。通过跨两个不相关领域（数学推理与具身智能体）的统一测试，检验词法检索、LLM重排与下游端到端表现之间的关系，揭示嵌入检索能力上限与实际受益之间的鸿沟。

Method: 采用统一评估协议在两个领域部署嵌入检索任务：(1) 竞赛数学数据集MathNet-Retrieve含500查询与117,088项语料库，通过多层"伪装"（disguise tiers，即改写同义不同表面形式的题面）分离语法与语义；(2) ALFWorld衍生的轨迹数据集含118查询与336条轨迹，其中表面变化是偶然性而非对抗性的。两类系统被评估：两类生产级嵌入模型（production embedders）、词法重排器（lexical reranker）和LLM重排器（三种评判器）。检索指标使用严格Hit@1与Top-10、bootstrap 95% CI；下游采用210查询配对端到端实验，由评分者（96-99%一致性）评估完整答案与截断代理。

Result: (1) 数学检索完全失败：最重伪装层Hit@1=0.0%（CI [0.0,0.0]），但正确答案95.2-99.8%的失误案例中胜出者比正确答案更词法相似。(2) 在轨迹领域，当金标需涉及不同物体时模型接近或处于超几何随机水平；一旦同时要求不同物体与容器，三个嵌入模型全部低于随机。(3) 词法重排器：数学中损害检索、轨迹中提升26-36%（CI排除0），其符号指示基准的表面变化是"对抗性"还是"偶然性"。(4) LLM重排器在数学中恢复5-63%差距、轨迹中恢复43-76%；21个单元全部为正方向可复现，但效应量、层级分布和异常评判器因领域而异。数学恢复集中在知名竞赛（+19.8点，CI [+6.7,+33.2]，6个单元中1个显著），表明部分是记忆。(5) 下游配对实验中oracle检索与对抗性差检索无显著差异（McNemar p=0.678），求解器69.5%零样本准确率在完整答案上达97-100%，无提升空间。

Conclusion: 嵌入检索在需要理解底层结构而非表面词汇的任务上系统性失败：数学领域词语伪装即造成完全崩溃，轨迹领域物体/容器约束即跌破随机基线。词法重排器的表现符号可作为基准中表面变化性质的诊断工具；LLM重排器部分恢复的收益具有领域依赖性与记忆成分。更关键的下游证据表明，此类基准的端到端收益为"零"——检索质量好坏不影响求解表现（配对实验不显著），现有指标大多反映截断代理而非真实问题求解能力。这提示检索基准研究必须区分"嵌入检索性"与"下游可用性"，并警惕基准表面设计导致的能力假象。

Abstract: We evaluate embedding retrieval where surface form and meaning are pulled apart on purpose: retrieving items that share underlying structure but not wording, in two unrelated domains under one protocol, competition mathematics (MathNet-Retrieve; 500 queries, 117,088-item corpus) and embodied-agent trajectories (ALFWorld-derived; 118 queries, 336 trajectories). In mathematics the failure is complete: strict Hit@1 at the heaviest disguise tier is 0.0% for both production embedders (bootstrap 95% CI [0.0, 0.0]) while the correct item sits in the top 10 nearly always, and in 95.2 to 99.8% of misses the winner is more lexically similar to the query than the correct answer. In trajectories, where surface variation is incidental, the same models land at or near hypergeometric chance when gold must involve a different object, and below chance for all three embedders once gold must differ in object and receptacle: retrieval anchors on literal tokens, not task structure. A lexical reranker control hurts in mathematics and helps in trajectories (closing 26 to 36% of the gap, CIs excluding zero); its sign reveals whether a benchmark's surface variation is adversarial or incidental. An LLM reranker recovers 5 to 63% of the gap in mathematics and 43 to 76% in trajectories; direction replicates across three judges (all 21 cells positive), but effect sizes, tier profiles, and the outlier judge change with domain (paired differences excluding zero everywhere). Mathematics gains concentrate on well-known competitions (+19.8 points, CI [+6.7, +33.2], one of six cells), so part of the recovery is memorization. In a paired downstream experiment (210 queries, graders at 96 to 99% agreement), oracle retrieval was indistinguishable from adversarially bad retrieval (McNemar p = 0.678); the solver's 69.5% zero-shot accuracy is largely a truncation proxy (97 to 100% on finished answers), leaving no headroom.

</details>


### [112] [Gradient-Update Mismatch: Rethinking Conflict-Free Training of Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01558)
*Jing Xiao,Xinhai Chen,Qinglin Wang,Menghan Jia,Zhiquan Lai,Dongsheng Li,Jie Liu,Tiejun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对物理信息神经网络(PINNs)训练中损失梯度冲突问题，揭示了现有梯度手术方法构造的无冲突方向在经过优化器变换后可能失去无冲突属性（称为GUM问题），并提出Gradient-Update Alignment (GUA)方法，将优化器更新投影回无冲突锥，并调整优化器内部状态以保持一致性，实验证明该方法显著降低PINN的L2误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs训练需要联合优化物理残差与初/边界条件损失，这些损失常产生冲突梯度。梯度手术方法通过构造无冲突的方向来缓解此问题，但现有方法忽略了优化器变换（如动量历史状态、自适应缩放、预条件化、解耦权重衰减）可能破坏方向的无冲突属性，导致实际参数更新仍然存在梯度冲突。

Method: 作者首先从理论上证明无冲突方向a_t经优化器变换后得到的提议更新u_t不一定仍属于无冲突锥C_t，将此现象定义为Gradient-Update Mismatch (GUM)。随后提出Gradient-Update Alignment (GUA)：将优化器提议更新u_t投影到无冲突锥C_t上获得对齐更新p_t并应用于参数；当优化器维护内部状态时，基于实际应用的更新p_t重建目标并相应调整内部状态。

Result: 实验表明GUM在动量、自适应和曲率类优化器中普遍存在，冲突率最高达86.3%。在各类PINN设置中，GUA能够实现无冲突的实际更新，显著提升多种梯度手术方法的性能，个别设置中相对L2误差降低达98.2%。

Conclusion: 梯度手术构造的无冲突方向在经过优化器变换后可能失去无冲突属性，需要将优化器更新本身对齐到无冲突锥，并同步修正优化器内部状态。GUA方法有效解决了这一被忽视的优化器诱发的不匹配问题，为PINN训练中多损失梯度冲突提供了一种统一且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Training Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) requires jointly optimizing physics residual and initial/boundary condition loss terms, which often induce conflicting gradients. Gradient surgery methods mitigate this issue by constructing directions from loss-specific gradients to reduce conflict before optimizer transformation. However, even when the constructed direction is conflict-free, this property may not be preserved after optimizer transformation. Let $a_t$ denote the direction constructed by gradient surgery, $u_t$ the optimizer proposal, and $\mathcal{C}_t$ the conflict-free cone induced by the loss-specific gradients. We show that modern optimizers can transform $a_t$ through mechanisms such as historical state, adaptive scaling, preconditioning, or decoupled weight decay, so $a_t \in \mathcal{C}_t$ does not generally imply $u_t \in \mathcal{C}_t$. We refer to this optimizer-induced discrepancy in conflict-freeness between $a_t$ and $u_t$ as Gradient-Update Mismatch (GUM). Accordingly, we propose Gradient-Update Alignment (GUA), which projects $u_t$ onto $\mathcal{C}_t$ to obtain the aligned update $p_t$ and applies $p_t$ to the parameters. When the optimizer maintains internal state, GUA further adjusts this state toward targets reconstructed from the applied update. We conduct extensive experiments and find that GUM is widespread across momentum, adaptive, and curvature-based optimizers, with conflict rates reaching up to 86.3%. Across all PINN settings, GUA achieves conflict-free applied updates and consistently improves various gradient surgery methods, reducing the relative $L_2$ error by up to 98.2% in individual settings. Data and code are available at https://github.com/JingXiao10/GUA.

</details>


### [113] [The Structure of Quantization Damage in LLMs: Why the Next Bit Should Be Spent Globally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01587)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过因果混合精度干预实验（逐层将精度恢复到8-bit并测量精度恢复）研究大语言模型后训练量化（PTQ）的损伤分布。研究发现量化损伤在层间呈弥散分布（9个模型中8个需要约一半层数才能恢复75%的精度差距），而非集中在"任务回路"、计算层或权重统计等直观假设预测的位置。在同等精度预算下，全局采用更细的量化粒度（如group-128）比局部修复最易恢复的层效果更好，提升了21-52个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PTQ被广泛用于降低LLM推理成本，但其精度损失不均衡且需逐模型调优。作者希望弄清量化损伤发生的位置，以及如何用少量额外精度预算来最优地分配，从而指导量化策略选择。

Method: 以因果混合精度干预为基准方法——逐个将每层精度提升到8-bit并测量其恢复的准确率，作为ground truth。在4个架构家族的9个开源模型上测试了3个假设：（1）损伤集中在任务回路中；（2）损伤集中在模型计算层；（3）损伤可由权重统计预测。同时还对比了全局细粒度量化与局部层精度修复两种预算分配策略。

Result: 3个直观假设均无法预测哪些层受益于精度恢复。损伤恢复呈弥散分布：9个模型中8个需约一半层恢复75%的精度差距，唯Qwen3-8B例外（损伤高度集中）。在匹配的精度预算下，全局细粒度量化（group-128）在8个兼容模型中全部优于局部修复策略，提升21-52个点，包括集中式损伤的Qwen3-8B。次要发现：残差受预算限制（8-bit在RTN/GPTQ/AWQ下近乎无损），峰值恢复位置在家族内与架构相关但跨家族不相关。

Conclusion: 在该预算设置下，全局细粒度量化是比选择性保护关键层更好的默认策略。更广泛地，与量化损伤相关的廉价信号不一定能识别出恢复精度实际能提升准确率的层——这一点必须通过因果干预来验证。这一发现对量化调优实践（尤其为不同模型设定精度分配方案）具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is widely used to reduce the cost of serving large language models (LLMs), but its accuracy cost is uneven and is often tuned per model. We study where quantization damage occurs and how to allocate a small additional precision budget. Using causal mixed-precision intervention as ground truth (raise each layer to 8-bit in turn and measure the accuracy it recovers) across 9 open-weight models in 4 architecture families, we test 3 intuitive hypotheses: that quantization damage lives in task circuits, where the model computes, or in weight statistics. None of them predicts which layers benefit from restored precision. Recovery is instead diffuse: for 8 of 9 models, recovering 75% of the gap takes roughly half the layers; the lone exception, Qwen3-8B, is sharply concentrated. At a matched precision budget, spending it globally on finer quantization granularity beats locally repairing the most recoverable layers for all 8 group-128-compatible models (all but OpenLLaMA, whose width rules out group-128), by 21-52 points, including the concentrated Qwen3-8B. We report 2 secondary findings: the residual is budget-limited (8-bit is near-lossless in our evaluation across RTN, GPTQ, and AWQ), and the location of peak recovery correlates with architecture within a family, though not across families. Within this budget setting, global granularity is a better default than selectively protecting critical layers. More broadly, cheap signals that correlate with quantization damage do not necessarily identify where restoring precision improves accuracy; this must be tested with causal intervention.

</details>
