Ensuring reserve deliverability in all activation scenarios is naturally modeled as a stochastic programming (SP) problem, whose size grows exponentially with the number of locations in the network. This formulation scales poorly in real-world applications. We first show that activation scenarios can be expressed in an ``order-book-agnostic way", reducing the challenge to a network modeling problem and already improving computational performance. We then introduce a general inner approximation principle that we use to derive two scalable inner approximations and one column generation algorithm for tackling one of the approximations. The first inner approximation is well known to practitioners, and relates to a basic result for describing boxes in a polytope (or similarly, describing the union of ATC domains within a flow-based domain), while the second model, its associated column generation algorithm and finite-dimensional reformulation are based on semi-infinite linear programming and robust linear optimization. We compare the inner approximations to the exact SP formulation, which we also solve via a Danzig-Wolfe decomposition for comparison purposes. Numerical results show that these inner approximations are much more scalable than the stochastic programming formulation, while reaping most of the benefits of cross-zonal exchanges. The approaches are of particular interest for future pan-European cross-zonal balancing capacity markets, and can also accommodate co-optimization of energy and balancing capacity products.

</details>


### [159] [Operational Regimes in Non-Convex Optimization: A Multiplier-Based Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00471)
*Seyed Mohsen Kazemi,Ali Movaghar,Shaahin hessabi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于KKT驻点处 Lagrange 乘子"签名"的结构性分类体系，用以刻画约束非凸优化问题。通过统一八类经典算法族的博弈论视角，证明归一化乘子向量携带与算法无关的结构指纹；四个无尺度形状特征将对偶空间划分为五种运行机制（无约束、资源受限、饱和、强耦合和混合），并给出了四结构定理刻画该划分，最终提出线性时间分类器并提供其正确性、稳定性、样本复杂度及在线漂移追踪的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束非凸优化中问题结构对算法性能影响显著，但缺乏统一的、与算法无关的结构描述框架来指引算法选择和鲁棒性分析。现有方法通常针对特定算法或特定问题类别，难以在多算法族间建立可操作的对应关系。

Method: 作者引入基于 Lagrange 乘子归一化向量的四个无尺度形状特征，将对偶空间划分为五种操作机制；利用博弈论统一视角分析八类算法族（块坐标下降、ADMM、广义Benders分解、逐次凸逼近、内点法、镜像下降、Frank-Wolfe、黎曼梯度下降），并建立四个结构定理——KKT对称性下的不变性、基于Robinson强正则性的数据扰动局部稳定性（含显式Lipschitz边界）、余维一机制转变以及混合机制作为核心机制Lebesgue零测边界的拓扑识别。最后提出线性时间分类器。

Result: 理论上确立了机制划分的四条结构性定理，证明乘子特征在KKT对称性下不变、在数据扰动下具有局部稳定性、机制转变是余维一现象、混合机制具有Lebesgue零测度边界。数值实验在104个混合整数非线性规划和一个下行波束成形实例上验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 该框架为机制感知的算法设计与鲁棒性分析提供了基础性工具，使算法选择可依据问题结构化分类进行，并具备对抗数据漂移的在线追踪能力。

Abstract: This paper introduces a structural taxonomy for constrained non-convex optimization based on the signature of Lagrange multipliers at KKT stationary points. Leveraging a unified game-theoretic interpretation of eight classical algorithm families--including block coordinate descent, ADMM, generalized Benders decomposition, successive convex approximation, interior-point methods, mirror descent, Frank-Wolfe, and Riemannian gradient descent--we show that the normalized multiplier vector carries an algorithm-independent structural fingerprint. Four scale-free shape features of this vector partition the dual space into five operational regimes: Unconstrained, Resource-Limited, Saturation, Strongly-Coupled, and Hybrid. We establish four structural theorems characterizing the partition: invariance under natural KKT symmetries, local stability under data perturbation with explicit Lipschitz margins from Robinson's strong regularity, codimension-one regime transitions, and the topological identification of the Hybrid regime as the Lebesgue-null boundary of the core regimes. A linear-time classifier is proposed with provable guarantees on correctness, iteration stabilization, sample complexity, and online tracking under data drift. Numerical experiments on 104 mixed-integer nonlinear programs and a downlink beamforming instance validate the theoretical predictions. The framework provides a foundational tool for regime-aware algorithm design and robustness analysis in non-convex optimization.

</details>


### [160] [Extremum Seeking Control: Three Revolutions and the Road Ahead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00484)
*Tiago Roux Oliveira*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文以历史视角梳理了极值搜索控制（ESC）一百多年的发展历程，将其划分为三次科学革命：工程革命（1922-1999）、数学革命（2000-2010）和当前的无穷维与信息物理革命（2010至今），并阐述了其跨越时代保持影响力的根本原因——核心工程挑战从未改变，即动态系统如何在最优未知的情况下自主学习提升自身性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图通过历史回顾与个人视角，回答"ESC为何在跨越多个技术时代后依然保持生命力"这一根本问题——指出其持久的学术影响力源于模型无关优化的核心目标从未改变，同时预言优化、学习与反馈控制的深度融合将催生该领域的第四次科学革命。

Method: 本文采用历史综述与前瞻分析相结合的视角性研究方法，将ESC的发展历程系统地归纳为三次科学革命（工程革命、数学革命、无穷维与信息物理革命），从历史叙事与工程挑战本质两个维度解析该领域的演进逻辑与未来方向。

Result: 论文提出ESC的演进可分为三个阶段：1922-1999年的工程革命确立了模型无关优化的工程原理；2000-2010年的数学革命为ESC奠定了严格的非线性控制数学基础；2010年至今的第三次革命将其拓展至时滞、偏微分方程、分布式优化、事件触发实现及复杂信息物理系统。作者认为其持久相关的本质原因在于"最优未知条件下动态系统如何自主提升性能"这一核心挑战恒久未变。

Conclusion: ESC作为一门历经百年的学科仍在持续自我革新，随着优化、学习与反馈控制的深度融合，该领域有望迎来第四次科学革命，为新一代智能自主系统的构建提供独特支撑。

Abstract: The history of extremum seeking is not merely the history of an algorithm; it is the history of an idea. Few ideas in control engineering have demonstrated the remarkable longevity of extremum seeking control (ESC). Invented more than one hundred years ago, ESC has continually reinvented itself while remaining faithful to its original objective: enabling systems to optimize their performance without relying on accurate mathematical models. This perspective article proposes that the evolution of ESC is best understood through three scientific revolutions. The first Engineering Revolution (1922-1999) established the engineering principles of model-free optimization; the second Mathematical Revolution (2000-2010) provided the rigorous mathematical foundations that transformed ESC into a mature discipline of nonlinear control; and the third ongoing Infinite-Dimensional and Cyber-Physical Revolution (2010-present) continues to expand its scope toward delays, partial differential equations, distributed optimization, event-triggered implementations, and increasingly complex cyber-physical systems. Beyond recounting this historical evolution, we offer a personal perspective on why ESC has remained relevant across successive technological eras. We argue that its enduring influence arises because the fundamental engineering challenge has never changed: "how can a dynamical system learn to improve its own performance when the optimum is unknown?" As optimization, learning, and feedback control become increasingly intertwined, ESC appears uniquely positioned to contribute to a new generation of intelligent autonomous systems, pointing toward what may become the field's fourth scientific revolution.

</details>


### [161] [Stochastic Optimization of Tree Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00870)
*Marius Willner,Maximilian Scharf,André Uschmajew,Timo Felser,Marco Trenti*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为树张量网络（TTN）推导了参数流形与商流形上的随机黎曼优化器，包括适用于小批量训练的自适应和无学习率方案，并在混合CNN-TTN架构上验证于Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR10和Imagenette数据集，实现了与无约束优化相当的预测性能，同时支持数值稳定的下游压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量网络作为量子多体物理中发展起来的模型，在机器学习领域展现出潜力。然而现有训练方法缺乏对张量网络固有几何结构的考虑，导致训练不稳定且不利于后续压缩。本文动机在于开发尊重张量网络流形结构的优化算法，以兼顾训练效果和压缩可靠性。

Method: 本文在树张量网络（TTN）的参数流形和商流形上分别推导了随机黎曼优化器，引入了自适应优化器以及无需学习率的方案，使其适用于小批量（minibatch）训练范式，并在此基础上构建了混合CNN-TTN架构进行实验验证。

Result: 在Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR10和Imagenette三个数据集上的实验表明，所提出的黎曼优化器达到了与无约束优化相当的预测性能，并且在数值上能够实现稳定的下游压缩操作。

Conclusion: 本文表明在张量网络训练中引入黎曼几何结构不仅不会损失预测精度，还能显著改善模型的数值稳定性，为后续的模型压缩与部署提供了更可靠的保障。

Abstract: Tensor networks, originally developed for quantum many-body physics, are promising models for machine learning. We derive stochastic Riemannian optimizers for tree tensor networks (TTNs) on both their parameter and quotient manifolds, including adaptive and learning-rate-free schemes suitable for minibatch training. Using a hybrid CNN-TTN architecture, we evaluate the methods on Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR10, and Imagenette. The proposed optimizers achieve predictive performance comparable to unconstrained optimization while enabling numerically stable downstream compression.

</details>


### [162] [Finite-Horizon Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman Equations with State-Dependent Diffusion in Spectral Barron Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00529)
*Shaolin Ji,Xianrui Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究高维有限时域Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程，在增广谱Barron空间中提出了一个严格的分析框架，解释了神经网络在高维随机控制中的近似能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一个严格的分析框架来解释高维随机控制中神经网络近似的有效性与合理性。

Method: 针对变系数线性方程，通过在Gaussian乘子中冻结二阶系数构造参数积（parametrix），得到精确Green算子和终端传播子；随后将线性理论与非线性HJB方程的半显式梯度迭代相结合，证明短时收敛性；并通过Itô验证论证将极限识别为随机控制值函数。最后推导空间与时间的联合浅层cosine网络近似。

Result: 证明了短时收敛性，极限是有界经典解，并通过Itô验证论证与随机控制值函数等同。

Conclusion: 本文的分析将高维随机控制、变系数抛物正则性和非线性HJB理论与定量神经网络近似联系起来，为从PDE解分析通往神经网络复杂度的直接路径提供了基础。

Abstract: We study high-dimensional finite-horizon Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman equations for controlled diffusions with uniformly elliptic, state-dependent diffusion coefficients. Motivated by the need for a rigorous analytical framework that explains neural-network approximation in high-dimensional stochastic control, we formulate the analysis in the augmented spectral Barron space. For the variable-coefficient linear equation, we construct a parametrix by freezing the second-order coefficient in the Gaussian multiplier, leading to an exact Green operator and terminal propagator without requiring small spatial variation of the diffusion coefficient. We then combine this linear theory with a semi-explicit gradient iteration for the nonlinear HJB equation and prove short-horizon convergence. The limit is a bounded classical solution and is identified with the stochastic-control value function by an Itô verification argument. Finally, we derive a joint shallow cosine-network approximation in space and time. Taken together, our analysis connects high-dimensional stochastic control, variable-coefficient parabolic regularity, and nonlinear HJB theory with quantitative neural-network approximation, thereby providing a direct route from PDE solution analysis to neural-network complexity.

</details>


### [163] [Projected Gradient Method on Hadamard Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00540)
*O. P. Ferreira,M. L. N. Gonçalves,A. M. González,M. S. Louzeiro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Hadamard流形上带闭测地凸可行集的约束光滑优化问题，分析了常数步长和回溯线搜索两种投影梯度方法，证明了两者的每个累积点均为一阶稳定点（在各自假设下），并给出了投影平稳性测度的O(1/√N)迭代复杂度界及相应的ε-复杂度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前约束优化方法在Hadamard流形上的收敛性分析通常需要可行集的紧性假设，本文旨在放宽这一条件，在无需可行集紧致的前提下证明一阶稳定性和迭代复杂度。

Method: 提出了两种投影梯度方案：(1)常数步长方案，要求目标函数具有Lipschitz连续的黎曼梯度；(2)回溯线搜索方案，无需该假设即可建立累积点的平稳性。利用测地凸性和Hadamard流形的几何结构，结合投影算子的单调性等性质进行收敛性分析。

Result: 在各自假设下，两种方案的每个累积点均为一阶平稳点，紧性仅用于保证累积点的存在性。当目标函数具有Lipschitz连续黎曼梯度时，两种方案均获得O(1/√N)的投影平稳性测度迭代复杂度界及相应ε-复杂度估计；回溯方案还需要试探步长一致远离零。序列还满足渐近正则性。在对称正定矩阵流形上的约束Karcher均值问题验证了方法的实际性能。

Conclusion: 本文证明投影梯度法在Hadamard流形约束优化中无需可行集紧致即可获得一阶平稳性保证和最优的次线性复杂度，拓展了流形上约束优化算法的理论适用范围，并在SPD流形上得到了有效验证。

Abstract: We study constrained smooth optimization problems on Hadamard manifolds with closed geodesically convex feasible sets. We analyze two projected gradient schemes: one with a constant stepsize and another with a backtracking line search. The constant-stepsize scheme is analyzed under the assumption that the objective function has a Lipschitz continuous Riemannian gradient, whereas the backtracking variant does not require this assumption to establish stationarity of accumulation points. For both schemes, we prove that every accumulation point of the generated sequence is first-order stationary under the respective assumptions, without requiring compactness of the feasible set; compactness is needed only to ensure the existence of accumulation points. When the objective function has a Lipschitz continuous Riemannian gradient, we derive iteration-complexity bounds of order \(O(1/\sqrt{N})\) for projection-based stationarity measures for both schemes, together with the corresponding \(\varepsilon\)-complexity estimates. For the backtracking scheme, the complexity analysis additionally requires the trial line-search stepsizes to be uniformly bounded away from zero. Under the same respective assumptions, the generated sequences are also asymptotically regular. Finally, we illustrate the practical performance of the methods by solving constrained Karcher mean problems on the manifold of symmetric positive definite matrices.

</details>


### [164] [The Minimum Q-Order of BFGS with Exact Line Search Is One](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00596)
*Benqi Liu,Chenyi Li,Zaiwen Wen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文回答并否定了Powell关于精确线搜索变度量方法中相邻迭代误差幂律关系的猜想。作者证明BFGS在光滑强凸条件下，相邻迭代的最小Q-阶为1，且存在一个非终止的实例达到此边界，即BFGS虽达到Q-超线性收敛，但不存在大于1的固定幂次能控制所有充分晚的相邻误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典超线性收敛理论通常默认相邻迭代之间的误差满足某种固定幂律关系。Powell曾质疑这一光滑性假设是否必然导致精确线搜索变度量方法中相邻迭代满足固定幂律。本文旨在回答这一悬而未决的开放问题。

Method: 在任意至少二维的有限维空间中，针对任意指定半径和Hessian容差，构造了一个无穷可微、全局强凸的目标函数。该函数在给定球外等于标准二次型（Hessian为I），球内Hessian在算子范数意义下保持在对I的指定容差内，在原点处达到唯一极小值且Hessian为单位阵。随后以单位阵为初值、从球内出发执行精确线搜索BFGS，分析其收敛行为。

Result: 证明了对BFGS方法，光滑强凸框架下相邻迭代的最小可能Q-阶等于1，且该边界可由单一非终止运行达到。构造的函数使得精确线搜索BFGS达到Q-超线性收敛，但不存在任何大于1的固定幂次能够控制所有充分晚阶段的相邻误差。

Conclusion: Powell猜想被否定：即使光滑性假设成立，BFGS的相邻迭代误差也不必服从大于1的固定幂律关系。这揭示了精确线搜索变度量方法在局部收敛行为上的深层结构复杂性，对超线性收敛率的理论刻画提出了新的思考。

Abstract: Powell asked whether the smoothness assumptions underlying classical superlinear convergence force a fixed power law between adjacent iterates of exact-line-search variable-metric methods. We answer this question negatively for BFGS: within the smooth strongly convex setting, the smallest possible adjacent-iterate Q-order is one, and this boundary is attained by a single nonterminating run. In every finite dimension at least two, and for any prescribed radius and Hessian tolerance, we construct an infinitely differentiable, globally strongly convex objective that equals the standard quadratic outside the corresponding ball and whose Hessian remains within the prescribed tolerance of the identity in operator norm. The objective has its unique minimizer at the origin and identity Hessian there. Exact-line-search BFGS, initialized with the identity matrix and started inside that ball, converges Q-superlinearly, yet no fixed power greater than one controls all sufficiently late adjacent errors.

</details>


### [165] [Disciplined Bilevel Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00644)
*Hao Zhu,Joschka Boedecker*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出"纪律性双层规划"(DBLP)符号框架，允许用户以接近数学公式的高层可读形式指定和求解乐观双层优化问题。通过将下层问题自动规范化到锥形式，利用锥KKT条件构造等价的单层重构，并采用间隙延续过程近似求解一系列光滑非线性问题。该框架已在开源Python包BLVPY（CVXPY的扩展）中实现，用户只需几行代码即可求解双层优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层优化为层级决策问题提供了自然建模语言，但现有数值求解器通常需要大量人工分析和重表述工作，缺乏高层、用户友好的符号框架来直接规范化和求解此类问题。

Method: 提出纪律性双层规划(DBLP)符号框架：针对上层为纪律性非线性问题、下层为满足纪律性参数化规划规则的凸问题，自动将下层规范化到锥形式，利用锥Karush-Kuhn-Tucker条件构造等价单层重构；随后松弛互补约束并采用间隙延续过程近似求解一系列光滑非线性问题。框架在开源Python包BLVPY（CVXPY扩展）中实现。

Result: 在多个应用领域的双层优化问题上验证了BLVPY的建模与求解能力，证明用户无需具备双层建模和数值优化方面的专门知识，仅需少量代码即可指定并求解双层优化问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的DBLP框架和BLVPY实现有效降低双层优化问题的建模与求解门槛，使高层可读、接近数学表述的规范方式成为可能，为更广泛的双层优化应用提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Bilevel optimization provides a natural modeling language for hierarchical decision problems. However, applying existing numerical solvers usually requires substantial manual analysis and reformulation. In this paper, we introduce disciplined bilevel programming (DBLP), a symbolic framework that allows users to specify and solve optimistic bilevel problems in a high-level, human-readable way that is close to the mathematical formulation. For problems with a disciplined nonlinear upper problem and a convex lower problem satisfying the disciplined parameterized programming rules, DBLP automatically canonicalizes the lower problem into conic form and constructs an equivalent single-level reformulation using the conic Karush-Kuhn-Tucker conditions. We relax the resulting complementarity constraint and use a gap continuation procedure to approximately solve a sequence of smooth nonlinear problems. We implement DBLP in the open-source Python package BLVPY, an extension of CVXPY for bilevel programming. We demonstrate the modeling and solution capabilities of BLVPY on a range of bilevel optimization problems from several application domains. The proposed framework and implementation allow users to specify and solve bilevel optimization problems within a few lines of code, without prior expertise in bilevel modeling and numerical optimization.

</details>


### [166] [Low-Thrust Trajectory Optimization with Quantum Computing and Sequential Convex Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00861)
*Carmine Giordano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出quSCP框架，将序列凸规划中的每个凸子问题重构为量子退火适用的二次无约束二进制优化（QUBO）问题，并在地球-火星燃料最优低推力转移任务中验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低推力轨道优化的非凸动力学与运行约束使其难以求解；序列凸规划是有效手段，而量子退火可求解QUBO问题，但两者结合仍缺乏系统研究，尤其需解决约束嵌入与二进制离散化精度损失等难题。

Method: 提出quSCP框架：(1) 将每个凸子问题重构为QUBO格式；(2) 通过二次惩罚项嵌入等式、不等式与信赖域约束；(3) 采用迭代细化策略降低二进制离散化引入的精度损失；(4) 兼容量子与混合量子-经典求解器。

Result: 在地球-火星燃料最优低推力转移上，quSCP产生的轨迹物理一致，推进剂消耗和非线性约束违反量接近经典基准。直接量子退火因嵌入开销和硬件连接性限制仅适用于小规模问题，混合求解器可扩展到更大离散化。当前硬件下未展示量子计算优势。

Conclusion: 尽管现有量子硬件未体现计算优势，但结果表明量子及混合量子-经典优化已能为高要求的轨道设计问题提供有竞争力的解，为未来量子计算在航天任务设计中的应用开辟了途径。

Abstract: Low-thrust trajectory optimization is a central task in interplanetary mission design, but its nonlinear dynamics and operational constraints often lead to challenging non-convex optimal-control problems. Sequential convex programming has emerged as an effective approach to address these problems, while quantum annealing offers a complementary paradigm for solving quadratic unconstrained binary optimization problems. This paper introduces quSCP, a quantum-based sequential convex programming framework that reformulates each convex subproblem as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization problem suitable for quantum and hybrid quantum--classical solvers. Equality, inequality, and trust-region constraints are embedded through quadratic penalty terms, while an iterative refinement strategy is used to reduce the accuracy loss introduced by binary discretization. The method is assessed on a fuel-optimal Earth--Mars low-thrust transfer by comparing standard sequential convex programming, a continuous quadratic unconstrained formulation, direct quantum processing unit sampling, and D-Wave hybrid solvers. Results show that quSCP produces physically consistent trajectories with propellant consumption and nonlinear constraint violations close to classical benchmarks. Direct quantum annealing is feasible only for small instances because of embedding overhead and hardware connectivity limits, whereas hybrid solvers scale to larger discretizations. Although no computational quantum advantage is demonstrated with current hardware, the results show that quantum and hybrid quantum--classical optimization can already provide competitive solutions for demanding trajectory design problems.

</details>


### [167] [DOFFO_TR: a Decentralized Objective Function-Free Optimization method with Trust-Region](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00878)
*Stefania Bellavia,Greta Malaspina,Benedetta Morini*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种无目标函数的分布式信任域优化方法，自适应选择步长，支持一阶和二阶模型，无需共享局部函数值和梯度以增强隐私，迭代复杂度与集中式信任域方法匹配，通信开销适中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分布式优化方法往往依赖步长调优，且需要在智能体之间共享函数值和梯度，存在隐私泄露风险且计算效率受限。作者旨在设计一种无需步长调优、无需共享局部信息的方法，以实现隐私保护和高效计算。

Method: 提出一种目标函数无关（objective function-free）的信任域（trust-region）框架，用于分散式网络上的优化。该方法通过函数无关的信任域过程自适应选择步长，可适配一阶和二阶模型，并消除了智能体间共享局部函数值与梯度的需求。

Result: 从理论上建立了可证明的迭代复杂度保证，其中部分变体的复杂度与经典集中式信任域方法相匹配。数值实验表明，该方法在性能与效率之间取得了良好折中，与现有最先进方法相比仅需要适度的通信开销。

Conclusion: 该无目标函数的信任域方法在去中心化优化中实现了自适性步长选择、隐私保护与计算效率的兼顾，并通过理论保证与数值验证展示了其有效性和竞争力。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a novel objective function-free trust-region method designed to solve optimization problems over decentralized networks. Unlike traditional approaches that often rely on stepsize tuning, our framework employs a function-free trust-region procedure that enables adaptive selection of the step length. Our approach accommodates first- and second-order models and eliminates the need to share local function values and gradients among agents, thereby enhancing privacy and computational efficiency. On the theoretical side, we establish provable iteration complexity guarantees that, for some variants, match those established for classical centralized trust-region methods. Numerical evaluations demonstrate that our approach achieves a favorable trade-off between performance and efficiency, requiring only moderate communication overhead compared to state-of-the-art methods in the literature.

</details>


### [168] [Partially Observed Mean Field Games Without Perfect Recall: Optimality Conditions and Equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00880)
*Xuanping Zhang,Xiao Zhang,Wang Yao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究无完美回忆（WPR）的部分可观测平均场博弈。代理仅基于当前观测σ(y_t)进行控制（信息族非嵌套），而条件总体律基于观测滤波F^Y，两层信息尺度不一致导致闭环困难。作者通过确定性兼容联合律参数化环境，结合Girsanov定理建立WPR随机最大值原理，用弱Kushner-Stratonovich表示条件总体律，通过Schauder-Tychonoff不动点定理证明弱WPR均衡存在，并通过兼容Yamada-Watanabe定理提升为强实现。线性二次银行间借贷示例对比了PR与WPR的均衡行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统部分可观测平均场博弈假设完美回忆（PR），即代理拥有完整观测历史信息。但在现实场景中（如高频交易、有限记忆决策），代理只能使用当前观测信息进行控制，形成非嵌套的信息族G_t^I=σ(y_t)。此时控制层的个体信息与条件总体律所依赖的观测滤波F^Y信息尺度不一致，导致均场博弈难以在单一构造中闭合，需要新的理论框架。

Method: (1) 用确定性兼容联合律将状态、驱动变量和随机均场项参数化，保持依赖结构但不扩大代理控制信息；(2) 对固定律，通过参考测度和Girsanov定理推导WPR随机最大值原理，用条件Hamiltonian和WPR信念测度表示选择响应；(3) 隐状态与均场项的联合路径后验给出条件总体律的弱Kushner-Stratonovich表示；(4) 在紧凸兼容律集合上证明递归响应映射连续，借助Schauder-Tychonoff不动点定理得到弱WPR均衡；(5) 对固定均衡律和反馈，用兼容Yamada-Watanabe定理由路径唯一性提升到强实现；(6) 线性二次银行间借贷模型中进行PR与WPR的数值对比。

Result: 建立了WPR部分可观测均场博弈的完整均衡存在性理论：证明弱WPR均衡的存在性，并给出了从弱解到强解的提升路径。在线性二次银行间借贷示例中，PR响应遵循Kalman-Bucy反馈，而WPR响应满足Fredholm-Volterra方程，在高斯设定下为当前观测的仿射函数。数值实验表明PR与WPR的均衡行为存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 本文为无完美回忆的部分可观测均场博弈提供了系统性的理论框架，解决了不同信息层级间耦合导致的闭环困难。理论贡献在于将兼容联合律参数化、WPR随机最大值原理、弱Kushner-Stratonovich表示与不动点方法相结合，为更一般的信息受限均场博弈分析奠定了基础；数值结果进一步揭示了记忆约束对均衡行为的实质性影响。

Abstract: This paper studies partially observed mean field games without perfect recall (WPR). The representative agent observes a noisy signal, but the control at time \(t\) uses only \(\mathcal G_t^I=σ(y_t)\), a generally non-nested information family. The conditional population law instead uses the observation filtration \(\mathbb F^Y\). These coupled levels rely on different information scales and are difficult to close within one construction. We parameterize the environment by a deterministic compatible joint law of state, driving variables, and random mean field term, thereby preserving its dependence structure without enlarging the agent's control information. For a fixed law, a reference measure and Girsanov's theorem yield a WPR stochastic maximum principle; the selected response is represented by the conditional Hamiltonian and WPR belief measure. The joint path posterior of hidden state and mean field term gives a weak Kushner-Stratonovich representation of the conditional population law. A recursive response map is continuous on a compact convex set of compatible laws, so Schauder-Tychonoff yields a weak WPR equilibrium. For a fixed equilibrium law and feedback, a compatible Yamada-Watanabe theorem lifts pathwise uniqueness to a strong realization. Finally, a linear-quadratic interbank lending example compares perfect recall (PR) with WPR. The PR response follows the Kalman-Bucy feedback, whereas the WPR response solves a Fredholm-Volterra equation and is affine in the current observation under Gaussianity. The numerical experiment illustrates how equilibrium behavior differs between PR and WPR.

</details>


### [169] [Real-Time Model Predictive Control Algorithms for Autonomous Spacecraft Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00927)
*Mohammed-Adnane Garab*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了四类MPC算法（线性MPC、Tube MPC、Fast/Embedded MPC、SCvx）用于航天器自主交会制导，证明了CWH动力学边界稳定性导致条件数随预测时域增长，解释了梯度类求解器发散而精确求解器不受影响的原因；验证了Tube MPC在五种标准机动中的鲁棒性，推导了基于代价权重矩阵的双侧最坏情况跟踪误差界，并扩展到任意参考轨迹跟踪及无外部库的实时实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航天器自主交会制导需要实时、鲁棒且计算可行的MPC算法，但不同MPC框架（线性MPC、Tube MPC、Fast MPC、SCvx）在长预测时域高应力场景下的数值稳定性、可行性与安全性保障尚未被系统比较验证，缺乏对实际飞行软件可实现性的评估。

Method: 采用CWH相对运动模型，通过分析condensed Hessian矩阵条件数与预测时域的关系解释梯度求解器发散机理；对比ADMM+Cholesky精确求解器与梯度方法；Tube MPC在五种标准机动（Translation、R-bar、V-bar、NMC、Corkscrew）中用单一固定配置验证；从代价权重矩阵推导双侧最坏跟踪误差界；通过在线重新线性化扩展至非闭合形式参考轨迹跟踪；从第一性原理无外部库重新实现Cholesky分解与Riccati方程求解器并验证至机器精度。

Result: 证明了CWH动力学的边界稳定性使condensed Hessian条件数随预测时域急剧增长，导致Fast MPC等梯度解法在长时域下发散，而ADMM配合Cholesky因子分解不受影响；Tube MPC以单一固定控制器配置成功通过五种标准机动验证；推导出可包围最坏跟踪误差的双侧、可证明正确的边界；扩展至任意参考轨迹时安全保证全程成立；第一性原理实现的核心数值例程与标准工具达到机器精度一致。

Conclusion: 综合结果表明Tube MPC是一种鲁棒且计算可行的片上实时航天器交会制导控制器，适合实际飞行软件部署。

Abstract: This report studies and compares four families of Model Predictive Control (MPC) algorithms for autonomous spacecraft rendezvous guidance: Linear MPC, Tube MPC, Fast/Embedded MPC, and Successive Convexification (SCvx). Using the Clohessy-Wiltshire-Hill (CWH) relative-motion model, we show that the marginal stability of the underlying dynamics causes the condition number of the condensed Hessian to grow sharply with the prediction horizon, which explains why gradient-based solvers such as Fast MPC diverge under long-horizon stress tests while exact linear-algebra solvers (ADMM with Cholesky factorisation) remain unaffected. Tube MPC is validated across five standard rendezvous manoeuvres (Translation, R-bar, V-bar, Natural Motion Circumnavigation, and Corkscrew) using a single fixed controller configuration, and a two-sided, provably correct bound is derived to bracket the worst-case tracking error directly from the cost weight matrices. The framework is further extended to track an arbitrary, non-closed-form reference trajectory through online-recomputed linearisation, with the safety guarantee holding throughout. Finally, to assess feasibility for real flight software, the core numerical routines (Cholesky factorisation and the Riccati equation solver) are re-implemented from first principles, without external libraries, and validated against standard scientific computing tools to machine precision. Taken together, these results support Tube MPC as a robust and computationally realistic controller for onboard, real-time spacecraft rendezvous guidance.

</details>


### [170] [Further analysis and extension of the higher-order Newton method of Ahmadi, Chaudhry, and Zhang](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01001)
*Lucas ter Voert,Etienne de Klerk*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将该方法从无约束优化推广到具有SOS-凸多项式约束的优化问题。给定迭代点x，下一步迭代定义为在约束下最小化f的d阶Taylor展开加一个d′阶正则项（d′为大于d的最小偶数，使得该多项式为SOS-凸）。构造该多项式及其约束下的最小化均可多项式时间内化为SDP求解。若f强凸且d阶导数张量Lipschitz连续，算法以d阶收敛到最优解；在约束规格条件下，最优解处的积极约束可在单次迭代内被局部识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Ahmadi等人提出的d阶Newton方法（原针对无约束优化）拓展到带SOS-凸多项式约束的约束优化场景，填补高阶方法在约束优化领域的研究空白，并进一步研究其收敛率、积极约束识别和全局收敛性。

Method: 1) 推广d阶Newton方法：将无约束情形中的d阶Taylor展开加上d′阶正则化项拓展为受SOS-凸多项式约束的情形。2) 利用SDP在多项式时间内构造该多项式并求解其约束下的最小化问题。3) 证明局部d阶收敛性（强凸性+张量Lipschitz条件）。4) 在约束规格下证明积极约束集合单步局部识别。5) 对三阶Newton方法在无约束单变量两类函数上利用性能估计（performance estimation）分析最坏情形性能。6) 将有全局收敛保证的修正d阶Newton方法扩展到SOS-凸多项式约束设置。

Result: 建立了该方法在SOS-凸多项式约束下的局部d阶收敛性；证明了在约束规格条件下积极约束集合在单次迭代内被局部识别；给出了三阶Newton方法在两类单变量函数上的最坏情形性能估计分析；完成了带全局收敛保证的修正版本在SOS-凸约束下的推广。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将高阶（d阶）Newton方法从无约束优化推广至SOS-凸多项式约束优化，具备快速的局部d阶收敛率、高效的SDP实现、积极的约束识别能力以及全局收敛的修正版本，为该类约束优化问题提供了一套理论完备且计算可行的高阶求解框架。

Abstract: We extend a $d$th-order Newton method for unconstrained optimization by Ahmadi, Chaudhry, and Zhang [Advances in Mathematics, 452:109808] to optimization with SOS-convex polynomial constraints. Consider the problem of minimizing a smooth function $f:\mathbb{R}^{n}\to\mathbb{R}$ subject to SOS-convex polynomial constraints. Given an iterate $x\in\mathbb{R}^n$, Ahmadi et al. define the next iterate $x^{+}$ as the minimizer of the $d$th-order Taylor expansion of $f$ at $x$ with a regularization term of degree $d^{\prime}$, where $d^{\prime}$ is the smallest even number greater than $d$, chosen such that this polynomial is SOS-convex, subject to the constraints. Constructing this polynomial and minimizing it subject to the constraints can both be reduced in time polynomial in $n$ to a semidefinite program (SDP). We prove that, if $f$ is strongly convex and the tensor of the $d$th-order partial derivatives of $f$ is Lipschitz continuous, then our method converges locally to the optimal solution $x^{\ast}$ with order $d$. We further prove that, under certain constraint qualifications, the set of active constraints at $x^{\ast}$ is identified locally in a single iteration. Next, we study the worst-case performance of the third-order Newton method in the unconstrained setting for two classes of univariate $f$ using performance estimation. Finally, we extend a globally convergent modification of the $d$th-order Newton method to the setting of SOS-convex polynomial constraints.

</details>


### [171] [Sum-of-Squares Certificates for Copositive Matrices via Recursive Identities: The de Klerk-Pasechnik Conjecture and Hoffman--Pereira Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01010)
*Jineon Baek,Luis Felipe Vargas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文证明了de Klerk和Pasechnik（2002）提出的猜想：关于稳定性数α(G)的半定界ϑ^{(r)}(G)在r=α(G)-1时是精确的，并通过显式的平方和证书加以证明。该证书还用于恢复Motzkin-Straus公式极小化子的已知刻画，并给出了满足Hoffman-Pereira符号条件的矩阵的平方和余非负性证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证并证明de Klerk与Pasechnik在2002年提出的关于图稳定性数半定界精确性的猜想，该猜想涉及半定规划界与图论中独立数之间的深层联系，其证明对于理解半定界在组合优化中的紧性具有重要意义。

Method: 通过构造显式的平方和（sum-of-squares）证书来证明半定界在指定指数下精确；利用该证书恢复Motzkin-Straus公式极小化子的刻画；进一步针对满足Hoffman-Pereira符号条件的矩阵建立平方和余非负性（copositivity）证书。

Result: 成功证明了de Klerk-Pasechnik猜想，即在r=α(G)-1时半定界ϑ^{(r)}(G)精确等于稳定性数；获得了相应的平方和证书，恢复了已知的Motzkin-Straus极小化子刻画，并给出了Hoffman-Pereira符号条件下矩阵的余非负性平方和证书。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了半定界理论中的一个重要理论空白，确立了平方和证书方法在证明半定界精确性方面的有效性，并为余非负矩矩阵的刻画提供了新的代数工具。

Abstract: We establish the conjecture by de Klerk and Pasechnik (2002), claiming that the semidefinite bounds $\vartheta^{(r)}(G)(r\geq 0)$ for the stability number $α(G)$ are exact at $r=α(G)-1$, by exhibiting an explicit sum-of-squares certificate. This certificate allows us to recover a known characterization of the minimizers of the Motzkin-Straus formulation for $1/α(G)$. Additionally, we give sum-of-squares copositivity certificates for the matrices satisfying the Hoffman--Pereira sign condition, a crucial condition for characterizing copositive matrices with $\{-1,0,1\}$ entries.

</details>


### [172] [The Complexity of Approximating the Value in Revealing POMDPs with Long-Run Average Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01099)
*Ali Asadi,Krishnendu Chatterjee,David Lurie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有长期平均目标的部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），聚焦于揭示型（revealing）POMDP子类，证明了其长期平均值的近似问题是EXPTIME完备的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般POMDP的长期平均值既不可计算也不可近似，这使得该问题在理论上不可解。作者因此考虑揭示型POMDP这一子类，旨在找出一个既具实际意义又能在计算上处理的求解方向。

Method: 首先通过控制与优化领域的应用实例说明揭示型POMDP的实际相关性；然后从理论层面证明揭示型POMDP的长期平均值近似问题属于EXPTIME，同时给出其下界，从而建立精确的计算复杂度。

Result: 揭示了揭示型POMDP的长期平均值近似问题是EXPTIME完备的，给出了紧的计算复杂度界限。

Conclusion: 虽然一般POMDP的长期平均目标不可判定/不可近似，但揭示型POMDP子类在保持实际应用价值的同时，其近似复杂度被精确刻画为EXPTIME完备，为后续算法设计提供了理论基准。

Abstract: We study partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) with long-run average objectives, defined as the limit inferior of the expected average rewards. In general, the long-run average value of a POMDP is neither computable nor approximable. We therefore consider the subclass of \emph{revealing POMDPs}, in which the current state is revealed to the controller with positive probability at each stage. First, we illustrate the practical relevance of this class through an application in control and optimization. Second, we establish that approximating the long-run average value of revealing POMDPs with long-run average objectives is $\mathrm{EXPTIME}$-complete, thereby providing a tight computational complexity.

</details>


### [173] [Accelerated primal--dual dynamics and algorithms for convex optimization with nonlinear inequality constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01415)
*Xin He*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对带非线性不等式约束的凸优化问题，提出了一个在连续和离散时间下自洽的原始-对偶乘子框架。通过含Nesterov型消失阻尼α/t的连续时间动力学及适当的对偶变量和非线性约束映射外推，在凸性假设和α≥3下建立了非线性可行性和目标残差的O(t^{-2})收敛率；随后通过兼容离散化导出非精确加速原始-对偶算法，在复合凸目标下以加权可和条件获得O(k^{-2})加速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Nesterov型加速方法主要集中于无约束或线性约束凸优化，针对非线性不等式约束的加速原始-对偶乘子方法在连续与离散时间框架下的统一分析尚属空白。本文旨在填补这一空缺，构建首个Nesterov型原始-对偶乘子框架。

Method: 提出连续时间动力学，包含Nesterov型消失阻尼α/t及对偶变量与非线性约束映射的适当外推；在此基础上，通过对动力学的扰动版本进行兼容离散化，推导出非精确加速原始-对偶算法，并施加关于原始非精确度的加权可和条件以保证加速率。

Result: 在凸性假设与α≥3条件下，连续时间动力学实现了非线性可行性与目标残差的O(t^{-2})收敛率；离散算法在复合凸目标下达到O(k^{-2})收敛率，与连续时间加速率相匹配。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个针对非线性不等式约束凸优化的Nesterov型原始-对偶乘子框架，实现了连续与离散时间下一致的加速收敛保证，为相关问题的算法设计提供了统一的理论基石。

Abstract: We consider convex optimization with nonlinear inequality constraints and develop a primal--dual multiplier framework that is consistent in continuous and discrete time. We first propose continuous-time dynamics with Nesterov-type vanishing damping $α/t$, together with suitable extrapolations of the dual variable and the nonlinear constraint mapping. Under convexity assumptions and $α\geq3$, we establish $\mathcal O(t^{-2})$ convergence rates for both nonlinear feasibility and the objective residual. We then derive an inexact accelerated primal--dual algorithm through a compatible discretization of a perturbed version of the dynamics. For composite convex objectives, a weighted summability condition on the primal inexactness yields the $\mathcal O(k^{-2})$ rates for feasibility and the objective residual, thereby matching the accelerated rates of their continuous-time counterparts. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first Nesterov-type primal--dual multiplier framework for convex optimization with nonlinear inequality constraints.

</details>


### [174] [Optimal Gradient-Norm Minimization in Non-Euclidean Hölder-Smooth Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01122)
*Nico Pelleriti,Maryam Shiran,David Martínez-Rubio,Max Zimmer,Sebastian Pokutta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了凸函数在ℓ_p范数下(L,κ)-Hölder光滑条件下的梯度范数最小化问题，提出了两组达到近最优梯度预言复杂度的算法：第一组利用镜像对偶现象（引入算法误差与近似计算的双重行为），第二组通过在多个近似解处累积正则化子并顺序最小化来实现近最优收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度范数最小化在优化与学习任务中很重要——梯度提供了近似驻点性的可直接计算的证书，且其最小化通常比函数值最小化产生更强的结果。然而在ℓ_p范数（p≥1）下的Hölder光滑凸函数的梯度最小化问题上，存在未解决的开放情形（光滑情形p>2）以及复杂度间隙（Hölder光滑情形全p范围，包括欧几里得情形）。

Method: 本文提出两组算法族：第一组算法具有简单的迭代形式，推广了"镜像对偶"现象——利用带误差和不精确计算的算法在双重空间中的行为特性；第二组算法利用在不同近似解处累积的正则化项，通过顺序最小化这些累积正则化子来获得近最优的收敛速率。

Result: 在光滑情形下，解决了此前开放的问题（p>2）；对Hölder光滑目标，在整个p值范围内闭合了复杂度间隙，包括欧几里得情形（此前存在的间隙）。两组算法均实现了近最优的梯度预言复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文系统地解决了凸函数在一般ℓ_p范数下Hölder光滑条件下的梯度范数最小化问题，通过两组不同的算法设计策略，在所有p值范围内达到了近最优的复杂度下界，填补了此前文献中的多项空白。

Abstract: Minimizing gradients of a convex function is an important problem across optimization and learning tasks. The gradient provides a directly computable certificate of approximate stationarity, and its minimization usually implies stronger results than those for minimization of function values. In this work, we study gradient-norm minimization for convex functions that are $(L,κ)$-Hölder smooth with respect to the $\ell_p$-norms, $p \geq 1$. We develop algorithms that achieve near-optimal gradient-oracle complexity for this problem. In the smooth case, our results resolve the previously open setting $p>2$. For Hölder-smooth objectives, we close the complexity gap throughout the full $p$-range, including to the best of our knowledge, a gap in the Euclidean case. We provide two families of algorithms: the first one comes with a simple iteration and generalizes a phenomenon known as mirror duality, exploiting dual behaviours of algorithms with errors and inexact computations. The second makes use of accumulating regularizers centered at different approximate solutions, which we sequentially minimize in order to provide our near-optimal rates.

</details>


### [175] [New insights into the NLP-Id bound for maximum-entropy sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01214)
*Kurt Anstreicher,Marcia Fampa,Jon Lee,Yongchun Li,Gabriel Ponte*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了最大熵采样问题（MESP）中NLP-Id上界的性质，特别是其目标函数关于缩放参数的凹性，从而允许更宽松的缩放参数选择，并能获得更紧的MESP上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大熵采样问题（MESP）是一个重要的组合优化问题，需要有效的上界来加速分支定界等求解方法。NLP-Id是MESP的一个上界方法，但对其目标函数关于缩放参数的数学性质（尤其是凹性）尚缺乏深入研究，这限制了缩放参数的选择灵活性。

Method: 作者细致考察了NLP-Id上界中目标函数关于缩放参数（scaling parameter）的凹性性质，基于该性质推导出相比以往更松弛的缩放参数可行范围，进而获得改进的MESP上界。

Result: 建立了NLP-Id上界目标函数关于缩放参数的新性质，特别是凹性特征的详细分析，实现了更宽松的参数选择策略和更优的MESP上界。

Conclusion: 通过揭示NLP-Id目标函数关于缩放参数的凹性结构，本文为MESP上界计算提供了更灵活的参数设置依据，并同时提升了上界的紧度。

Abstract: We establish new properties of the NLP-Id upper bound for the maxi\-mum-entropy sampling problem (MESP). In particular, we give a detailed look at the concavity of its objective function as a function of the scaling parameter employed for NLP bounds for MESP. This leads to more relaxed choices for the scaling parameter for NLP-Id and even improved upper bounds for MESP.

</details>


### [176] [Optimal Inflow Control for Transport Equations with Uncertain Velocities and Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01291)
*Simone Göttlich,Thomas Schillinger*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究线性输运方程在需求与运输速度均存在不确定性时的最优入流控制问题。随机速度导致随机运输时间，从而改变入流决策与其到达时观测到的需求之间的关系。作者推导了连续时间与分段常数控制下的显式最优控制，将不可约的随机误差分解为需求与速度不确定性两部分，并证明后者存在与速度方差线性相关的界。此外，提出确定性平均速度代理策略作为计算高效的替代方案，并建立了最优控制关于速度律Wasserstein距离扰动的Lipschitz稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在包含传输延迟的实际系统中（如供应链、交通流、流体网络），下游需求与运输速度常受随机因素扰动，导致传统确定性控制方法失效。现有研究多假设速度确定或降低到数值方法层面，缺乏对随机速度如何影响最优入流控制的可解析刻画。作者旨在为这一基础随机控制问题提供显式解与理论保证，并探索计算可行的代理方案。

Method: 采用最优控制与随机分析框架：(1) 对具有随机运输时间的线性输运方程建立入流控制问题，针对固定下游观测窗口推导连续时间与分段常数控制的最优解；(2) 将随机误差按需求不确定性（含到达时间随机性耦合）与速度不确定性进行正交分解，导出误差界；(3) 提出以确定性均值速度替代随机速度的代理策略，分析其性能损失的高阶界；(4) 以Wasserstein距离度量速度律扰动，证明最优控制满足Lipschitz稳定性。

Result: 获得连续时间与分段常数两类控制场景下入流最优控制的显式表达式；随机误差中速度不确定性部分的界关于速度方差呈线性增长；均值速度代理策略的附加性能损失具有比线性更高阶的界；最优控制在Wasserstein度量下关于速度律扰动是Lipschitz稳定的；数值实验验证了上述结果，并将代理策略推广到非局部随机输运模型。

Conclusion: 该工作为含随机运输时间与随机需求的最优入流控制提供了完整的理论框架，显式解揭示了误差结构；确定性平均速度代理在计算效率与精度之间实现了优异折中，其高阶误差界说明在速度不确定性较小时代理策略近乎最优；Lipschitz稳定性保证了控制对模型扰动的鲁棒性。上述结果可作为更复杂随机网络控制问题的分析基础。

Abstract: We study optimal inflow control for a linear transport equation subject to uncertainty in both time-dependent downstream demand and transport velocity. The random velocity induces a random travel time and thereby changes the relation between an inflow decision and the demand observed at its arrival time. For a fixed downstream observation window, we derive explicit optimal controls in the continuous-time setting and for piecewise constant controls. We decompose the irreducible stochastic error into contributions from demand and velocity uncertainty and show that the latter admits a bound that is linear in the velocity variance. As a computationally attractive alternative, we analyze a deterministic mean-velocity proxy whose additional performance loss admits a higher-order bound. We also establish Lipschitz stability of the optimal control with respect to perturbations of the velocity law in the Wasserstein distance. Numerical experiments illustrate the analytical results and investigate the proxy strategy for an uncertain nonlocal transport model beyond the linear theory.

</details>


### [177] [Consensus-based optimization for linearly separable functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01317)
*Christian Fiedler,Tim Roith*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对基于共识的优化（CBO）在高维非凸优化中受维度诅咒困扰的问题，提出了一种重参数化方案，用于估计目标函数仅在线性变换坐标系下可分的坐标变换，从而充分发挥各向异性CBO的效率优势，并通过数值实验验证了该方法的显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBO作为一种具有全局收敛理论保证的元启发式优化算法，在实践中受维度诅咒影响严重。现有的各向异性噪声模型虽然在一定程度上缓解了高维问题，但近期研究指出该方法仅在可分离目标函数上表现良好，微小的坐标旋转就会严重损害性能。本文旨在解决各向异性CBO对坐标旋转敏感的局限性。

Method: 作者提出一种重参数化方案：首先推断目标函数所在的可分性线性变换坐标系（该变换可被证明地估计），然后在此新坐标系中应用CBO算法。该方案可被诠释为CBO中的噪声自适应机制，即根据目标函数的结构动态调整探索噪声的协方差方向。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的方法在具有挑战性的基准目标函数上相比原始各向异性CBO实现了显著的性能提升，有效克服了坐标旋转导致性能退化的问题。

Conclusion: 通过将可分性线性变换的估计与各向异性CBO相结合，所提出的重参数化方案在保留CBO全局收敛理论优势的同时，显著增强了其在旋转非可分高维优化问题上的实际性能，为CBO在高维非凸优化中的应用提供了新的有效策略。

Abstract: Consensus-based optimization (CBO) is an efficient metaheuristic for global optimisation with attractive mathematical properties, allowing global convergence results even in non-convex settings. In practice it suffers greatly from the curse of dimensionality, as do most particle-based optimisers. Different strategies have been proposed to apply CBO even for high-dimensional optimisation problems, the most prominent being the so-called anisotropic noise model. However, a recent work by Bonandin et al. highlighted that this method performs well primarily on separable objective functions, and even small coordinate rotations severely degrade the performance. Motivated by this observation, in this work we study the case where the objective function is separable only in a linearly transformed coordinate system. To leverage the efficiency of anisotropic CBO we propose a reparametrisation scheme, which provably estimates such a coordinate transformation and then applies CBO in the new variables. Furthermore, we show how our scheme can be interpreted as noise adaptation in CBO. Numerical examples highlight the efficacy of the method, demonstrating significant performance improvements on challenging benchmark objectives.

</details>


### [178] [A Scalable Framework for Simultaneously Optimizing Damper Positions and Viscosities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01398)
*M. Ugrica Vukojević,P. Goyal,Z. Tomljanović*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对二阶微分方程描述的振动系统中的阻尼优化问题，提出了一种新框架，通过重新加权l1最小化和剪枝技术来确定减振器的数量与安装位置，以实现最低的平均总能量。数值实验验证了该方法的有效性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在振动系统中，阻尼优化是一个关键问题。现有的方法在确定阻尼器的位置和粘度值时面临挑战，尤其是在确定阻尼器的最优数量与位置方面缺乏系统性框架。本文旨在解决这一问题，以最小化系统的平均总能量。

Method: 提出一种新框架，采用重新加权的l1范数最小化（re-weighted l1 minimization）结合剪枝（pruning）技术，同时确定阻尼器的数量、位置和粘度值。

Result: 通过多个数值实例验证，该方法在确定阻尼器位置与数量方面具有较高的效率和良好的性能表现。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于重新加权l1最小化与剪枝技术的框架能够有效地解决振动系统中的阻尼优化问题，在获得最低平均总能量的同时确定了最优的阻尼器配置。

Abstract: We consider damping optimization for vibrating systems described by a second-order differential equation. The goal is to determine the damping positions and viscosity values of the dampers in the system so that they produce the lowest average total energy. We propose a new framework for determining the number and positions of the dampers, using re-weighted $l_1$ minimization and pruning techniques. The efficiency and performance of our new approach are verified and illustrated in several numerical examples.

</details>


### [179] [Generalized Semi-Infinite Programming for Robust Optimal Control with Decision-Dependent Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01538)
*J. Wehbeh,E. C. Kerrigan,E. Scaccia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通用框架，将任意满足温和正则条件的广义半无限规划（GSIP）重新表述为存在约束的半无限规划，通过平滑析取可行性条件为可微的存在性约束，并借助自适应离散化（割平面）方法配合现成的非线性规划求解器进行求解，适用于鲁棒最优控制中状态相关不确定性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GSIP广泛出现在鲁棒最优控制中（当允许的不确定性依赖于状态或控制时），但现有方法要么施加严格的结构假设，要么需要全局优化而难以扩展至控制问题，缺乏一种可扩展且通用的求解框架。

Method: 该方法将任何满足温和正则条件的GSIP重构为具有存在约束的半无限规划，将析取可行性条件平滑为在固定索引超集上的可微存在约束；随后利用成熟的适应性离散化（割平面）方法求解，仅需现成的非线性规划求解器，并在标准假设下保证收敛。此外，将状态轨迹视为不确定性的一部分，可将框架推广至具有状态依赖不确定性的鲁棒非线性最优控制问题。

Result: 在一个非凸基准GSIP问题以及一个具有动态变化惯量的卫星消旋（detumbling）问题上进行了验证，证明了框架的可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种通用、无需全局优化且仅依赖现有NLP求解器的GSIP求解途径，能够有效扩展到鲁棒非线性最优控制中状态依赖不确定性的场景。

Abstract: Generalized semi-infinite programs (GSIPs) arise in robust optimal control whenever the admissible uncertainty depends on the state or controls. Existing GSIP methods either impose restrictive structural assumptions or require global optimization that scales poorly to control problems. We present a general framework that reformulates any GSIP with mild regularity as an existence-constrained semi-infinite program, smoothing its disjunctive feasibility condition into differentiable existence constraints over a fixed index superset. The resulting program is solved by established adaptive discretization (cutting-plane) methods using only off-the-shelf nonlinear-programming solvers, and converges under standard assumptions. Treating the state trajectory as part of the uncertainty extends the framework to robust nonlinear optimal control with state-dependent uncertainty. We demonstrate it on a nonconvex benchmark GSIP and a satellite de-tumbling problem with dynamically varying inertia.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [A coercive space-time variational approach to fractional diffusion problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00091)
*Herbert Egger,Marvin Fritz,Barbara Wohlmuth*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究具有时间非局部性的分数阶扩散问题，建立了Bochner值分数阶Sobolev空间中的强制时空变分公式并证明解的存在性、唯一性及正则性；进一步提出相容的张量积Galerkin离散格式，证明各向异性能量范数下的拟最优误差估计及基于对偶论证的弱范数改进收敛率。该方法保持了演化问题的因果结构，产生带记忆项的时间推进过程，在均匀时间网格上离散历史算子具有下三角Toeplitz结构，可通过快速递归卷积技术高效实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶扩散问题中时间非局部性作用于扩散通量，需要建立严格的数学分析与高效数值方法；同时需避免经典空间-时间公式破坏因果结构的缺陷，实现既有理论保证又保持物理因果性的离散方案。

Method: 1) 在Bochner值分数阶Sobolev空间中推导强制的空间-时间变分公式并分析解的存在唯一性与正则性；2) 采用相容的张量积Galerkin离散，通过对偶论证在能量范数和弱范数下给出误差估计；3) 利用离散历史算子的下三角Toeplitz结构，结合快速递归卷积技术实现高效的时间推进计算。

Result: 证明了变分公式的强制性与问题适定性；Galerkin离散在能量范数下达到拟最优收敛，弱范数下利用对偶得到改进的收敛阶；离散格式保持因果结构，生成的离散历史算子在下三角Toeplitz结构下可高效计算。

Conclusion: 该方法相比经典空间-时间扩散公式，保持演化问题的因果结构并自然形成带记忆项的时间推进格式；均匀时间网格下Toeplitz结构为快速卷积算法提供了可能，兼顾了理论精度与计算效率。

Abstract: We consider a fractional diffusion problem with temporal nonlocality acting on the diffusive flux. A coercive space--time variational formulation in Bochner-valued fractional Sobolev spaces is derived and the existence, uniqueness, and regularity of solutions are established. We further develop a conforming tensor-product Galerkin discretization and prove quasi-optimal error estimates in the anisotropic energy norm and improved convergence rates in weaker norms using duality arguments. In contrast to some space-time formulations for classical diffusion, the method preserves the causal structure of the evolution problem and leads to a time-stepping procedure with memory terms. On uniform time grids, the discrete history operator has a lower-triangular Toeplitz structure which enables an efficient implementation using fast recursive convolution techniques.

</details>


### [181] [Solving the Incompressible Navier-Stokes Equations on Oriented Curved Surfaces Discretized by Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00216)
*Alejandra Foggia,Ivo F. Sbalzarini*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于无网格的点云数值求解器，用于在曲面（以点云表示）上求解不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程，采用弱人工可压缩性约束局部施加不可压缩条件，无需网格或全局矩阵求逆，空间和时间上可实现高达六阶的一致收敛精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在曲面上求解不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程时，刚度问题和压力-速度耦合问题比欧几里得空间更加严峻，且曲面上的向量微积分与平坦空间不同。现有方法通常需要网格或结构化计算网格，难以直接处理由点云表示的曲面（如图像衍生几何），因此需要一种无需网格、可直接处理无结构点云的高阶数值求解器。

Method: 该方法采用欧拉框架下的曲面点云表示，不依赖任何计算网格。不可压缩约束通过弱人工可压缩性（weak artificial compressibility）近似局部施加，从而避免了全局矩阵求逆。在空间和时间上实现一致的高阶逼近（最高达六阶）。通过分析人工可压缩性引入的振荡频率谱，定量研究误差、空间分辨率与人工马赫数之间的关系。

Result: 方法在对称曲面（球面、环面）及参数化和非参数化不对称曲面上均提供了不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程的一致且收敛的数值解，能够对曲面向量场和微分算子进行一致的收敛逼近，并展示了高达六阶的精度。

Conclusion: 由于该方法可直接作用于无结构曲面点云，无需网格生成，因此为基于图像衍生几何的仿真（如利用显微视频进行生物形态发生建模）提供了有前景的求解途径。

Abstract: We present a meshfree numerical solver for the incompressible Navier-Stokes equations on oriented curved surfaces that are represented by surface point clouds. On curved surfaces, numerical challenges pertaining to stiffness and pressure-velocity coupling are exacerbated. Moreover, vector calculus on curved surfaces differs from its Euclidean counterpart. The presented method operates on surface point clouds in an Eulerian frame of reference without requiring a computational grid or mesh. It achieves consistent approximation in space and time with high order of accuracy; we demonstrate up to order six. The incompressibility constraint is locally imposed as a weak artificial compressibility approximation, avoiding global matrix inversion. We show that the method provides consistent and convergent approximations of surface vector fields and differential operators. We study the relationship between error, spatial resolution, and artificial Mach number and characterize the frequency spectrum of the artificial oscillations. We provide numerical solutions of the incompressible Navier-Stokes equations on symmetric surfaces, such as the sphere and torus, and on parametric and non-parametric asymmetric surfaces. Since the proposed method works directly on unstructured surface point clouds, it provides a promising approach for simulations on image-derived geometries, such as in biological morphogenesis from microscopy videos.

</details>


### [182] [Shape Holomorphy and Sparse Approximation of the Maxwell Electric Field Integral Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00466)
*Paul Escapil-Inchauspé,Carlos Jerez-Hanckes*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了不确定形状障碍物的时谐Maxwell散射问题中边界积分算子族的形状全纯性。通过表面逆变Piola变换将几何依赖的Maxwell迹空间与固定参考空间等同，并利用复变形标量单层算子的均匀分数阶映射定理，证明了拉回算子关于形状参数是(𝒃,p,ε)-全纯的，从而获得算子族、表面电流和远场的稀疏多项式逼近，且具有维度无关的最佳N项逼近率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不确定形状障碍物的Maxwell散射不确定量化中，边界元方法要求边界积分算子族本身关于形状参数全纯依赖。但存在两个障碍：电场积分方程的自然能量空间H^{-1/2}_{div_Γ}(Γ)依赖于几何形状，且现有的算子值形状全纯理论仅适用于L^2空间的弱奇性核，无法覆盖到该能量空间。

Method: (1) 通过表面逆变Piola变换将几何依赖的Maxwell迹空间与固定参考空间等同，并在拉回变分形式中消除表面Jacobian；(2) 在均匀C^{1,1}曲面上，将Laplace主部实现为固定环境空间上复系数Newton问题的迹，建立复变形标量单层族从H^{-1/2}到H^{1/2}的均匀分数阶映射定理；(3) 证明拉回算子是(𝒃,p,ε)-全纯的，并在紧实数参数集上排除内部电谐振以获得均匀可逆性；(4) 据此推导Legendre系数的ℓ^p可和性，得到稀疏多项式逼近的维度无关最佳N项逼近率。

Result: 拉回的积分算子族关于形状参数(𝒃∈ℓ^p(ℕ), 0<p<1)是(𝒃,p,ε)-全纯的，并在能量空间算子范数意义下可获得均匀可逆性。Legendre系数为ℓ^p可和，因此算子族本身、表面电流和远场均具有维度无关的最佳N项稀疏多项式逼近率。

Conclusion: 本文建立的算子级形状全纯性是对单一解全纯性的本质扩展，保证了在整个能量空间算子范数意义下的稀疏多项式可逼近性，为不确定形状Maxwell散射问题的边界元方法不确定量化提供了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification for time-harmonic Maxwell scattering by obstacles of uncertain shape needs more than holomorphic dependence of the scattered field: for a boundary element method it is the boundary integral operator family itself that must depend holomorphically on the shape parameters. Two obstructions stand in the way. The natural energy space of the electric field integral equation, $\boldsymbol H^{-1/2}_{\mathrm{div}_Γ}(Γ)$, depends on the geometry, and the available operator-valued shape-holomorphy theory for weakly singular kernels is set in $L^2$, which does not reach it.
  We remove both. A surface contravariant Piola transformation identifies the geometry-dependent Maxwell trace spaces with a fixed reference space, and in the pulled-back variational formulation the surface Jacobians cancel exactly. The principal analytical ingredient is then a uniform fractional mapping theorem $H^{-1/2}\to H^{1/2}$ for the complex-deformed scalar single-layer family on uniformly $C^{1,1}$ surfaces, obtained by realizing the Laplace principal part as the trace of a complex-coefficient Newton problem on a fixed ambient space. The pulled-back operators are consequently $(\bm b,p,\eps)$-holomorphic for $\bm b\in\ell^p(\N)$, $0<p<1$, and pointwise exclusion of interior electric resonances over the compact real parameter set yields uniform invertibility.
  Legendre coefficients are therefore $\ell^p$ summable, so the operator family, the surface current and the far field all admit sparse polynomial approximations at dimension-independent best $N$-term rates. These statements are for the operator family itself in its energy-space operator norm, not only for individual solutions.

</details>


### [183] [Spectral Bounds for Kernel Quadrature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00553)
*A. Cloninger,Q. T. Le Gia,H. N. Mhaskar*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文指出在核方法的机器学习中，存储瓶颈可通过显式特征图（如高斯核的傅里叶特征）来缓解，但通常采用蒙特卡洛近似积分。作者证明使用合适的求积公式（quadrature）对积分进行离散化，所得到核的特征值远比蒙特卡洛近似下核的特征值更接近原始核的特征值，并在高斯核及四维球面上的神经正切核（sigmoid 与 ReLU 激活）上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核方法的存储需求是理论瓶颈，标准缓解手段是用显式特征图替代核，但这需要数值近似积分。传统上常用蒙特卡洛方法，但其特征值近似精度有限，因此需要探索更优的数值积分策略来提升近似质量。

Method: 作者将核 K 表示为涉及非对称核 G 的积分表达式，系统分析用求积公式（而非蒙特卡洛）离散化该积分所得核的特征值逼近性质，并在高斯核以及单位球面上的神经正切核（sigmoid 与 ReLU 激活）情形下进行数值验证与对比。

Result: 实验表明，使用合适的求积公式离散化积分得到的核，其特征值对原始核 K 的特征值的近似效果显著优于蒙特卡洛近似方案，覆盖了高斯核和两种激活函数的神经正切核情形。

Conclusion: 采用确定性求积公式替代蒙特卡洛方案可以显著改善离散核特征值对原始核特征值的逼近精度，为核方法的特征图近似提供了更优的数值策略。

Abstract: A bottleneck in the theory of kernel methods in machine learning is the storage requirement. To ameliorate this, a standard trick is to replace the kernel with an explicit feature map. Perhaps, the most well known example is the Gaussian kernel which can be expressed in terms of the Fourier features. Analytically, the kernel $K$ can be expressed in terms of an integral expression that involves a possibly asymmetric kernel $G$ representing the feature map. Numerically, one needs to approximate this integral by a suitable numerical integration scheme, typically Monte Carlo. In this paper, we demonstrate that the eigenvalues of $K$ are approximated much better by the eigenvalues of the kernel obtained by discretizing the integral using suitable quadrature formulas instead. We illustrate this fact in the case of the Gaussian kernel and neural tangent kernels on the unit sphere of a four-dimensional Euclidean space corresponding to the sigmoid and ReLU activation functions.

</details>


### [184] [Fast computation and convergence analysis of the infinite-product representation of the Schottky--Klein prime function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00785)
*Shuntaro Yamamoto,Hiroyuki Miyoshi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对Schottky–Klein素数函数在多重连通圆域边值问题中的数值计算困难，提出了一种基于交比势（cross-ratio potential）的截断策略，替代传统的字长截断方法，实现对Schottky群元素的高效枚举，并证明误差按阈值指数衰减，衰减率由极限集的Hausdorff维数决定。数值实验表明该方法在边界圆接近相切或接近单位圆的困难几何构型下相比字长截断具有显著加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schottky–Klein素数函数在多重连通圆域的边值问题中具有广泛应用，但其表示为Schottky群的无穷乘积，数值上必须截断。标准字长截断方法的计算成本随字长指数增长，当边界圆彼此接近或接近单位圆（即病态几何构型）时效率极差，需要一种更高效的截断与枚举策略。

Method: 为每个群元素定义一种交比势（cross-ratio potential），用以度量其在无穷乘积中的贡献大小；仅保留势低于给定阈值的项。建立了在该势上加上Schottky群生成元时变化量的闭式一致上界，并据此推导出高效的群元素枚举算法。

Result: 证明了相对误差随阈值按指数速度衰减，衰减率由Schottky群极限集的Hausdorff维数决定。数值实验表明，在边界圆接近相切或接近单位圆的困难几何构型中，该方法的计算速度相比字长截断获得显著提升。

Conclusion: 基于交比势的阈值截断方法为Schottky–Klein素数函数的高效数值计算提供了一种比传统字长截断更优的途径，特别适用于病态几何情形，理论误差分析清晰、数值实证有力。

Abstract: The Schottky--Klein prime function is a standard tool for boundary-value problems on multiply connected circular domains. Because this function is represented as an infinite product over a Schottky group, numerical evaluation requires truncation to finitely many factors. The standard word-length truncation grows exponentially in cost and becomes inefficient when the boundary circles nearly touch one another or the unit circle. To address this difficulty, we assign to each group element a cross-ratio potential measuring the size of its contribution, and retain only terms below a prescribed threshold. We establish uniform closed-form bounds on the change in this potential when prepending Schottky-group generators, and from these bounds we derive an efficient enumeration algorithm. The resulting relative error decays exponentially with the threshold at a rate determined by the Hausdorff dimension of the limit set of the Schottky group. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed formulation achieves substantial computational speedups over word-length truncation in challenging geometric configurations.

</details>


### [185] [On the Gram matrix of standard inner products of asymmetrically-weighted Hermite functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01144)
*Ruiyang Dai*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了非对称加权（AW）Hermite函数的无穷Gram矩阵A，推导了其元素的显式表示、Cholesky分解、在缩放Bargmann-Fock基上的自然解释以及A的逆的显式公式；同时分析了对应的有限Gram矩阵及其Schur补的渐近性质，并展示了这些结果在Vlasov-Poisson系统的Galerkin谱方法分析和实现中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机源于等离子体物理中数值方法的需求，特别是应用于Vlasov-Poisson（VP）系统的Galerkin谱方法。理解和利用AW Hermite函数的Gram矩阵结构对这类数值方法的高效实现和严格分析至关重要。

Method: 作者首先推导了无限Gram矩阵A的元素显式表示及其Cholesky分解，并在缩放Bargmann-Fock基上给出了该分解的自然解释；随后得到A的逆的显式公式；最后考察有限Gram矩阵及其Schur补的渐近性质，并将其应用于VP系统的Galerkin谱方法分析。

Result: 获得了AW Hermite函数Gram矩阵的完整显式结构（元素表示、Cholesky分解、逆的公式），建立了其在Bargmann-Fock框架下的自然解释，并得到了有限维情形及其Schur补的渐近性质，为VP系统Galerkin谱方法的分析和实现提供了可行的工具。

Conclusion: 所推导的Gram矩阵公式和渐近结果可有效用于Vlasov-Poisson系统Galerkin谱方法的分析与实现，为等离子体物理中相关数值方法提供了理论支持。

Abstract: Let A denote the infinite Gram matrix associated with the standard L2 inner product of asymmetrically-weighted (AW) Hermite functions. We derive an explicit representation of its entries and its Cholesky factorization. We further show that this factorization admits a natural interpretation on a scaled Bargmann-Fock basis. An explicit formula for the inverse of A is also obtained. We then consider the corresponding finite Gram matrix and analyze its asymptotic property, as well as that of its Schur complement. The analysis is motivated by numerical methods for plasma physics, in particular Galerkin spectral methods applied to the Vlasov-Poisson (VP) system. As an application, we demonstrate how the derived Gram matrix formulas and asymptotic results can be exploited in the analysis and implementation of a Galerkin spectral method for the VP system.

</details>


### [186] [Asymptotic behavior of Eckhoff's method for convergence acceleration of Dirac eigenfunction expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00895)
*R. H. Barkhudaryan,G. G. Gevorkyan,L. D. Poghosyan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维Dirac系统特征函数展开的有限系数重构问题，分析了Krylov-Lanczos-Eckhoff-Gottlieb加速方法在边界值需从广义Fourier系数推算时的表现，显式计算了2q×2q块Vandermonde系统矩阵的行列式与逆，并给出了L2误差渐近常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典三角情形下Krylov-Lanczos-Eckhoff-Gottlieb加速方法已被充分研究，但将其推广到一维Dirac系统特征函数展开时，边界值无法直接获取而需从广义Fourier系数中计算，这种情形下的理论分析尚属空白。

Method: 将边界条件从广义Fourier系数推算的问题转化为一个2q×2q线性系统，该系统矩阵为块Vandermonde矩阵；作者显式推导了该矩阵的行列式与逆矩阵，进而分析了方法的L2渐近误差常数。

Result: 成功显式计算了块Vandermonde矩阵的行列式与逆，并获得了该加速方法的L2渐近误差常数，该结果与经典三角情形下已有的渐近常数相平行。

Conclusion: 将经典的Krylov-Lanczos-Eckhoff-Gottlieb加速方法从三角基推广到一维Dirac系统特征函数基，建立了相应的误差渐近理论，为更一般特征系统下的谱方法加速提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The current paper considers the problem of recovering a vector-function on $[-1,1]$ from a limited number of coefficients of its expansion into a series of eigenfunctions of a one-dimensional Dirac system. The Krylov--Lanczos--Eckhoff--Gottlieb acceleration method is examined in the situation when the boundary values it requires have to be computed from the generalized Fourier coefficients themselves. This leads to a $2q\times 2q$ linear system whose matrix is a block Vandermonde matrix; its determinant and inverse are computed explicitly, and the asymptotic $L_2$-error constant of the method is found, paralleling the classical trigonometric case.

</details>


### [187] [OptiXDE: A fast optical-inspired solver for differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01009)
*Yang Yang,Mingjiao Yan,Zongliang Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: OptiXDE是一个基于矩阵自由谱算子框架的微分方程求解框架，适用于均匀网格和嵌入域。它将变换对角空间算子映射为解析模态乘子，并与物理空间算子组合以处理非线性、几何和边界约束。框架在多种问题上验证（扩散、Poisson、非线性薛定谔方程、Burgers、Allen-Cahn、不可压缩流等），线性问题接近浮点精度极限，非线性问题恢复二阶时间收敛，不可压缩性在长时间涡脱落中保持近舍入误差水平。矩阵自由更新只需O(N log N)计算和O(N)内存，GPU加速可达94.9倍，完整求解器CPU-GPU加速42.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对均匀网格和嵌入域上微分方程的高效求解，作者希望建立一种确定性、可扩展、算子中心（operator-centric）的计算框架。现有谱方法通常受限于稠密矩阵或特定几何，难以同时高效处理非线性、嵌入几何与边界约束，因此需要一种矩阵自由（matrix-free）且能统一处理线性和非线性问题的谱算子框架。

Method: 受傅里叶光学中角谱传播方法的启发，OptiXDE将变换对角空间算子映射为解析模态乘子，再与处理非线性、几何及边界约束的物理空间算子进行组合。采用统一的"变换-算子-逆变换（transform–operator–inverse-transform）"主干结构，对所有变换兼容的线性问题实现矩阵自由更新，计算复杂度为O(N log N)，内存占用O(N)。

Result: （1）变换兼容的线性问题误差接近浮点极限；（2）奇异L形域上的误差仅集中在re-entrant角与正则化界面附近；（3）非线性基准恢复二阶时间收敛以及预期的守恒/耗散行为；（4）长时间涡脱落中不可压缩性保持在近舍入误差级别；（5）设备内变换负载实现94.9倍GPU加速，完整嵌入圆柱求解器在相同数值设置下实现42.1倍CPU-GPU加速。

Conclusion: OptiXDE被确立为一个确定性的、可扩展的算子中心框架，可高效求解结构化及嵌入域微分方程，兼具谱精度、矩阵自由的计算效率和出色的GPU并行性能。

Abstract: OptiXDE is a matrix-free spectral operator framework for differential equations on uniform grids and embedded domains. Inspired by angular-spectrum propagation in Fourier optics, it maps transform-diagonal spatial operators to analytical modal multipliers and composes them with physical-space operators for nonlinearities, geometry and boundary enforcement. A common transform--operator--inverse-transform backbone is demonstrated across transient diffusion, periodic and embedded-domain Poisson problems, the cubic nonlinear Schr"odinger equation, viscous Burgers dynamics, the two-dimensional Allen--Cahn equation and incompressible flows from the Taylor--Green vortex to embedded-cylinder vortex shedding. Transform-compatible linear problems are recovered near the floating-point limit, whereas errors on the singular L-shaped domain remain localized near the re-entrant corner and regularized interface. Nonlinear benchmarks recover second-order temporal convergence and the expected conservative or dissipative behavior, while incompressibility remains near round-off level during long-time vortex shedding. The matrix-free updates require \(\mathcal{O}(N\log N)\) work and \(\mathcal{O}(N)\) memory. Device-resident transform workloads reach \(94.9\times\) GPU acceleration, and the complete embedded-cylinder solver achieves a \(42.1\times\) CPU--GPU speedup under matched numerical settings. These results establish OptiXDE as a deterministic and extensible operator-centric framework for structured and embedded-domain differential equations.

</details>


### [188] [Randomized inexact block triangular preconditioners for double saddle-point systems in PDE-constrained optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01031)
*Siqi Liang,Na Huang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对PDE约束优化中出现的双重鞍点系统，提出了一类新的非精确分块三角预条件子，通过矩阵分解技术构造，并引入随机化策略选择子块以高效构建。给出了预条件矩阵的特征值显式界与近似误差的高概率界，数值实验验证了其有效性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双重鞍点系统在PDE约束优化中广泛出现，求解这类系统的高效预条件子具有重要实际意义。现有预条件子构造通常成本高昂，且缺乏对构造误差的严格理论保证，亟需一种既能保留原始系统块结构、又可高效构建且具备完整谱理论分析的预条件方法。

Method: 通过矩阵分解技术构造非精确分块三角预条件子，保留原始系统的内蕴块结构；对预条件矩阵进行全面的谱分析，推导实特征值与复特征值的显式界；引入随机化策略选取所需的子块以实现非精确预条件子的高效构建，并以相关矩阵特征值显式刻画期望近似误差的高概率界。

Result: 建立了预条件矩阵实与非实特征值的显式界，给出了随机化构造引起的近似误差的高概率界（以相关矩阵特征值表征），数值实验证明了所提预条件子的有效性、鲁棒性和可扩展性，并验证了随机化构建策略的高效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的非精确分块三角预条件子结合随机化构建策略，为双重鞍点系统的高效求解提供了一种既有严格理论保证又具备实际可操作性的新方案，在理论分析与数值性能之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: We develop a new class of inexact block triangular preconditioners for double saddle-point systems arising from PDE-constrained optimization. The proposed preconditioners are constructed through matrix factorization techniques while preserving the inherent block structure of the original systems. A comprehensive spectral analysis of the preconditioned matrices is provided, yielding explicit bounds for both real and nonreal eigenvalues. To enable efficient construction of the inexact preconditioners, randomized strategies are introduced to select the required subblocks. We establish high-probability bounds for the expected approximation error, with the error estimates explicitly characterized in terms of the eigenvalues of the associated matrices. Numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness, robustness, and scalability of the proposed preconditioners, and validate the efficiency of the randomized construction strategies.

</details>


### [189] [Symmetry Inheritance and Symmetry-Reduced Finite Element Analysis for Second-Order Linear Elliptic Problems with Robin Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01107)
*Wei Jiang,Xianlong Pan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文为有界域上满足Robin边界条件的二阶线性椭圆方程建立了对称性框架。通过正交变换的群作用表示域变换，推导了梯度、散度、扩散通量和共法向边界项的变换规则，并定义了主系数张量、低阶系数、Robin系数及边界算子的对称群。在唯一可解性假设下证明了公共对称群的元素也是解的对称性；对反射对称性，在人工对称边界上获得齐次广义Neumann条件，实现计算域的精确约简，并通过有限元数值实验验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对带Robin边界条件的二阶线性椭圆方程，缺乏系统性的对称性理论框架来指导领域约简和数值求解。该文旨在建立完整的数学框架，使对称性不仅能用于理论分析，还能直接转化为计算域精确缩减的有效策略，提高复杂系数问题（尤其径向对称降至低维）的求解效率。

Method: 采用群论方法：以正交变换通过左群作用作用于标量场、向量场和二阶张量场；详细推导梯度、散度、扩散通量与共法向边界项在变换下的规则；据此为各类系数和算子定义对称群并融合公共对称群；结合有限元格式实现约简域的数值离散。

Result: 证明了公共对称群的每个元素在唯一可解条件下均为解的对称性；反射对称情形在人工对称边界产生齐次广义Neumann条件，从而可将计算域精确缩小。提供三维数值示例：径向对称多维降至一维、反射域约简、及全系数非零的变量系数问题，验证了理论正确性。

Conclusion: 该对称性框架为带Robin条件的椭圆方程提供了从系数层到解层的完整理论保障，并给出了计算上可行且保精确性的域缩减策略，有力支持了高效有限元求解。

Abstract: A symmetry framework is developed for second-order linear elliptic equations subject to Robin boundary conditions on bounded domains. Orthogonal transformations of the domain are represented through left group actions on scalar, vector, and second-order tensor fields. The corresponding transformation rules for the gradient, divergence, diffusion flux, and conormal boundary term are derived in detail. Based on these relations, symmetry groups are introduced for the principal coefficient tensor, the first-order and zeroth-order coefficients, the differential operator, the Robin coefficient, and the boundary operator. The symmetry properties of the volume and boundary source terms are then incorporated into a common symmetry group for the complete boundary value problem. Under the assumption of unique solvability, it is proved that every element of this common group is also a symmetry of the solution. For reflection symmetries, homogeneous generalized Neumann conditions are obtained on artificial symmetry boundaries, leading to an exact reduction of the computational domain. A corresponding finite element formulation is presented, and numerical results are provided to verify the theoretical symmetry properties and the validity of the resulting domain-reduction strategy. Three examples illustrate radial reduction from multiple dimensions to one dimension, reflection-based domain reduction, and a variable-coefficient problem in which all coefficients and source terms are nonzero.

</details>


### [190] [Projection-based low-rank assembly in IgA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01218)
*Tom-Christian Riemer,Martin Stoll*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于投影的低秩方法，用于等几何分析中质量张量和刚度张量的组装。该方法利用张量列车（TT）格式和交替最小能量（AMEn）方法，将多维积分简化为单变量积分和收缩积，避免了形成全高阶系数张量，适用于保定向的张量积B样条几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等几何分析（IgA）使用相同的样条函数表示计算域和逼近解，能实现精确的几何描述，但由此产生的质量矩阵和刚度矩阵在装配和存储上代价高昂，尤其在三维情况下。现有的插值基低秩方法在奇异插值系统和近奇异几何等场景下存在问题，因此需要一种更稳健的替代方案。

Method: 该方法的核心包括两部分：(1) 对质量张量，利用几何映射Jacobian行列式的多项式结构，通过单变量系数传递算子在缩减的样条积空间中表示它，在精确求积且不截断的条件下，所得低秩张量是标准Galerkin质量张量的精确重构；(2) 对刚度张量，将有理权重函数拆分为多项式分子（在缩减样条积空间中表示）和倒数行列式（通过L²投影到张量积样条空间进行逼近）。两种构造完全在张量列车（TT）格式中完成，投影系统由交替最小能量（AMEn）方法求解。

Result: 该方法在MATLAB中用GeoPDEs和TT-Toolbox实现。数值实验表明，该方法与全量装配以及基于插值的低秩方法具有竞争力，并且适用于两个插值方法存在问题的场景：奇异插值系统和近奇异几何。

Conclusion: 该投影基低秩方法为等几何分析中的张量组装提供了一种高效且稳健的替代方案，克服了插值基方法在奇异场景下的局限。但构造仅限于保定向的张量积B样条几何，不适用于NURBS。

Abstract: Isogeometric Analysis (IgA) uses the same spline functions to represent the computational domain and to approximate the solution. This allows exact geometry descriptions, but the resulting mass and stiffness matrices are expensive to assemble and to store, especially in three dimensions. We present a projection-based low-rank approach for assembling the mass and stiffness tensors of orientation-preserving tensor-product B-spline geometries. For the mass tensor, we exploit the polynomial structure of the determinant of the Jacobian of the geometry map and represent it in reduced spline product spaces by univariate coefficient transfer operators; with exact quadrature and without truncation, the resulting low-rank tensor is an exact reformulation of the standard Galerkin mass tensor. For the stiffness tensor, we split the rational weight function into a polynomial numerator, again represented in reduced spline product spaces, and the reciprocal determinant, which is in general not a spline function and is therefore approximated by an $L^2$-projection onto a tensor-product spline space. Both constructions are carried out entirely in the tensor-train (TT) format, with the projection system solved by the alternating minimal energy (AMEn) method, so that full high-order coefficient tensors are never formed and the multidimensional integrals reduce to univariate integrals and contracted products. The method is implemented in MATLAB using GeoPDEs and the TT-Toolbox. Numerical experiments show that it is competitive with full assembly and with the interpolation-based low-rank method, and that it applies in two situations in which interpolation is problematic: a singular interpolation system and nearly singular geometries. The construction is restricted to orientation-preserving tensor-product B-spline geometries and does not cover NURBS.

</details>


### [191] [Accelerating the Improved Arrow--Hurwicz Iteration via the Anderson Algorithm for Steady-State Navier--Stokes Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01288)
*Sinan Ergen,Mustafa Ağgül,Mustafa Türkyılmazoğlu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文将Anderson加速应用于改进型Arrow–Hurwicz（IAH）方法，用于求解稳态不可压缩Navier–Stokes方程的有限元解。IAH通过解耦速度和压力更新避免了鞍点求解，但在高雷诺数下迭代次数过多。本文将IAH迭代重构为非线性不动点算子并验证其光滑性条件，数值实验表明加速后的算法显著减少迭代次数和CPU时间，同时保持收敛率和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进型Arrow–Hurwicz（IAH）方法虽然通过解耦速度与压力更新避免鞍点求解，但其收敛速度缓慢，尤其在Reynolds数较高的流动问题中可能需要极其大量的迭代，导致计算开销难以接受。本文旨在通过加速技术提升该方法的实际计算效率。

Method: 本文将Anderson加速技术应用到IAH迭代中。为了为加速提供严格的理论基础，作者将IAH迭代重新表述为关于grad-div增广离散格式的非线性不动点算子G，并证明其适定性、Lipschitz连续性以及Fréchet可微性，从而在不动点附近验证了Anderson加速所需的光滑性条件。

Result: 数值实验覆盖了具有解析解的问题、Reynolds数高达15,000的方腔驱动流以及全台阶上的通道流。结果表明，Anderson加速后的IAH算法大幅减少了迭代次数和CPU运行时间，同时保留了文献中已报道的人工解收敛率以及中心线速度的一致性。

Conclusion: Anderson加速显著提升了IAH方法在稳态不可压缩Navier–Stokes问题上的收敛效率，且理论分析为加速技术在非线性不动点迭代中的可靠性提供了保证，证明了该算法在保证求解精度的同时具备实用高效的性能。

Abstract: We apply Anderson acceleration to the improved Arrow--Hurwicz (IAH) method for the finite element solution of the steady-state incompressible Navier--Stokes equations. The IAH scheme avoids saddle-point solves by decoupling the velocity and pressure updates, but can require prohibitively many iterations, particularly at high Reynolds numbers. To place the acceleration on a rigorous footing, we reformulate the IAH iteration as a nonlinear fixed-point operator $G$ for the grad-div augmented discrete formulation induced by the scheme and establish its well-definedness, Lipschitz continuity, and Fréchet differentiability, thereby verifying the required smoothness conditions locally near the fixed point. Numerical experiments on problems with known analytical solutions, lid-driven cavity flow up to $Re = 15{,}000$, and channel flow over a full step demonstrate that the resulting Anderson-accelerated improved Arrow--Hurwicz algorithm substantially reduces iteration counts and CPU time while retaining the reported manufactured-solution convergence rates and centerline-velocity agreement.

</details>


### [192] [Score-Based Generative Data Assimilation for Integrating Aggregated Surveillance Data into Agent-Based Models in Epidemic Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01434)
*Siming Liang,Jacob Hauck,Minglei Yang,Adam Spannaus,Heidi Hanson,Guannan Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于生成式AI的数据同化框架(GenDA),用于对部分观测的流行病Agent-based模型(ABM)进行在线校准,同时估计流行病情状态和异质参数场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的流行病监测需要从噪声、空间聚合且可能稀疏的监测数据中推断区域感染负担和传播异质性。ABM虽能刻画个体行为、接触异质性和局部干预,但其复杂性使其难以在线校准。

Method: GenDA框架包含三个关键步骤:(1)基于分数、无需训练的生成式更新用于宏观状态修正;(2)基于宏观状态差异的直接参数更新;(3)宏-微重分配步骤,恢复与ABM的一致性,弥补可观测宏观状态与潜在智能体级微观状态之间的鸿沟。

Result: 在受控和地理显式的合成实验中,该框架能够从聚合观测中恢复区域感染负担、主导热点结构和有效传播异质性,并相对仅状态同化方法提升了同化后的预测性能。

Conclusion: GenDA为部分观测的流行病ABM提供了一种同时估计状态和异质参数场的有效在线数据同化方法,在保持微观-宏观一致性的同时改善了预测准确性。

Abstract: Reliable epidemic monitoring often requires inferring regional infection burden and transmission heterogeneity from noisy, spatially aggregated, and potentially sparse surveillance data. Agent-based models (ABMs) are attractive for this task because they represent individual behavior, contact heterogeneity, and localized interventions, but these same features make them difficult to calibrate online. We develop a generative AI-based data-assimilation (GenDA) framework for partially observed epidemic ABMs that estimates both the epidemic state and a heterogeneous parameter field while respecting the gap between observable macrostates and latent agent-level microstates. GenDA combines a training-free, score-based generative update for macrostate correction with a direct parameter update based on macrostate discrepancies, followed by a macro-micro reassignment step that restores consistency with the ABM. In controlled and geographically explicit synthetic experiments, the framework recovers regional epidemic burden, dominant hotspot structures, and effective transmission heterogeneity from aggregated observations, while improving post-assimilation forecasts relative to state-only assimilation.

</details>


### [193] [Greedy sampling designs via reduced basis methods: optimal recovery in the uniform norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01578)
*Sebastian Neumayer,Kateryna Pozharska,Tino Ullrich*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究RKHS（再生核希尔伯特空间）中均匀范数下的最优采样恢复问题。针对每个具有有界核的RKHS，作者建立了线性采样宽度与Gelfand宽度之间的新比较，克服了已知的平方根间隙，且无需测度或Christoffel型条件。该界依赖于通过(弱)P-贪婪点处的核插值获得的嵌套采样设计。在额外的多项式衰减假设下，Gelfand宽度的衰减率可直接转移至采样宽度。通过对数过采样或取Gelfand宽度的平方根，可在无需衰减假设的情况下获得直接比较。这一结果对超多项式衰减（如Paley-Wiener空间）尤为有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数值逼近和高维采样理论中，线性采样宽度与Gelfand宽度之间的关系是核心问题。此前已知存在平方根间隙，即采样宽度的下界通常只能达到Gelfand宽度的平方根级别，这限制了最优采样策略的设计与分析。已有方法需要引入测度或Christoffel型条件，且对一般RKHS适用范围有限。作者旨在无需这些额外条件的情况下，建立两者之间更精确的比较关系，从而为最优采样方案的构造提供理论保证。

Method: 作者利用(弱)P-贪婪点处的核插值构造嵌套采样设计，通过引入过采样（对数过采样）或取Gelfand宽度的平方根来直接比较线性采样宽度与Gelfand宽度。两种宽度均以核平移（kernel translates）的形式表示，由此推导出比较不等式。在多项式衰减假设下，Gelfand宽度的衰减率可直接传递给采样宽度；对于超多项式衰减（如Paley-Wiener空间），该方法尤为有效。反方向上，该比较结果还导出了锐利降阶基选择（reduced basis selection）的新存在性结果。

Result: 作者建立了线性采样宽度与Gelfand宽度之间克服平方根间隙的新比较界，无需测度或Christoffel型条件。在多项式衰减假设下实现了衰减率的直接传递；通过对数过采样或平方根处理实现了无衰减假设的直接比较。数值实验在Legendre、混合Sobolev和Paley-Wiener核上验证了理论结果。同时得到了锐利降阶基选择的新存在性定理。

Conclusion: 该工作为RKHS中均匀范数下的最优采样恢复提供了新的理论框架，通过核插值和P-贪婪点构造了有效的嵌套采样设计，显著改进了采样宽度与Gelfand宽度之间的比较精度，对超多项式衰减情形尤其有效，并为降阶基选择提供了新的理论保证。

Abstract: We study optimal sampling recovery in reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces (RKHS) in the uniform norm. For every RKHS with bounded kernel, we establish new comparisons between linear sampling widths and Gelfand widths that overcome the known square-root gap, without requiring a measure or a Christoffel-type condition. Our bounds rely on nested sampling designs obtained by kernel interpolation at (weak) P-greedy points. Under additional (polynomial) decay assumptions the decay rate of the Gelfand widths directly transfers to the sampling widths. With either a logarithmic oversampling or passing to the square root of the Gelfand widths we obtain a direct comparison (requiring no decay assumption) between them. This is particularly effective for super-polynomial decay, such as in Paley-Wiener spaces. Our results follow from representations of both widths in terms of kernel translates and yield, in the opposite direction, a new existence result for a sharp reduced basis selection. Numerical experiments for Legendre, mixed-Sobolev, and Paley-Wiener kernels illustrate our findings.

</details>
